1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/shri ra kA ta ya mA ri tan tra rA dzA na ma//bod skad du/kas dpal gshin rje gshed ;
1b2(rgyud): dmar po zhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po/bcom ldan 'das gshin rje gshed la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag 
1b3(rgyud): gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang/gsung dang/
1b4(rgyud): thugs rje btsun mo'i b+ha ga la bzhugs so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi 
1b5(rgyud): snying po rje btsun gsang ba las shin du gsang ba la/phyag na rdo rjes gsol pa/'dir gshin rje gshed ci tsam gsungs pa 
2a1(rgyud): @#//lags kye phyag na rdo rje legs so legs so//rtse ba chen po byang chub sems dpa' chen po'o//rdo rje zhe sdang rdo rje/byang chub sems dpa'i 
2a2(rgyud): snying po/gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rang bzhin nyon cig/gti mug rdo rje gshin rje gshed dang/phra dog rdo rje gshin rje gshed dad/'dod chags rdo rje 
2a3(rgyud): gshin rje gshed dang/phra dgo rdo rje gshin rje gshed ces bya'o//rdo rje tsar tsi ka dang/rdo rje phag mo dang/rdo rje dbyangs can ma dang/rdo rje gA ri ma rnams kyang ngo 
2a4(rgyud): 'tshams rnams la bum pa rnams so//de lta bu la sogs pa'i gshin rje 'joms pa'i tshogs rnams kyis so//de nas bcom 
2a5(rgyud): ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po bdud thams cad 'joms pa/rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin las rang gi sku dang gsung 
2a6(rgyud): dang thugs rdo rje las rnam par spro bar mdzad nas/sngags kyis 'gugs dang dbang byed tshe//cho ga mang po gus de bzhin//ral gri sgrub dang rgyal po dang/
2b1(rgyud): pha rol dmag tshogs 'joms pa dang//mi sdang mig sman bcud len dang//de bzhin grib gnon rig pa dang//ro langs gnod sbyin sgrub pa dang//sbrul rdug 
2b2(rgyud): la sogs rtses pa dang//sbyin sreg dkyil 'khor las rnams dang//byang chub sems kyis rnam spyad dang//thabs rnams kun gyis spyod pa dang//pha rol grong 
2b3(rgyud): du 'jug rnams la//'od zer rnam lnga 'khrigs pa yi//rdo rje bsrung ba'i mchog tu bsgom//rdo rje bzhi'i cha rnams la//rab gur rnams de bzhin no//de nas 
2b4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yab//bdud thams cad rnam par 'joms pa//rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms 
2b5(rgyud): par zhugs nas/gti mug gshin rje gshed las pa/thams cad kyi sa bon nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs nyid rnams las nges par spro bar 'dzad do//ya ni 
2b6(rgyud): dbus su k+she ma me dang/yats+tsha ni ra dzA sa do ru na yo ni ra/ra sogs dang po gshin rje gshed//k+she yig las ni rmongs par brjod//ma yig las ni phra mar bshad//
3a1(rgyud): @#//de bzhin me yig 'dod chags so//sa yig las ni 'phra dog ste//gshin rje gshed rnams lnga ru grags//dza yig tsa ra kar bshad do//sa yig dbyangs
3a2(rgyud): can mar bshad do//ro yig gA+o ri mar bshad do//Na yo ni ra 'tshams bzhi la//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po gshin rje
3a3(rgyud): gshed/rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/zhe sdang chen po'i rigs sgom pa'i rnal 'byor gsungs pa/sbyin dang tshul khrims bzod par ldan//
3a4(rgyud): ro gcig mkhas pas sems can don//byams sogs yon tan phun sum tshogs//thod pa yis ni rnam brgyan pa'i//dang po kha sogs yongasu gsang 
3a5(rgyud): snga bar snying rje'i bdag nyid can//rab tu brtsom pa dben pa'i phyogs//dud khrod sogs su 'dre ba'i bstan//dben bzhugs nyid kyi thugs khar ni//pad nyi lta zhing 'od 
3a6(rgyud): rab gsal//de'i 'od kyis bkug byas nas//sems can don rnams phun sum tshogs//bsgom bya bla ma sangs rgyas rnams//ji snyed ma lus namakha'i 'thil;
3b1(rgyud): dmigs te phyag 'tshal de rnams kyi//sku'i bkod pa rnam par bsgrub//tsar tsi ka sogs rnams kyis mchod//de la sdig pa rab tu gshegs//lus dang 
3b2(rgyud): ngag dang yid rnams kyis//yang dag sdig gyur byas de bzhin//bdag gis de bshags gang zhig yod//dus mgon brjod rnams rjes yid rang//rdzogs pa'i 
3b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas 'tshungs rnams kyis//bsod nams tshogs la rjes yid rang//rdzogs pa sangs rgyas dam chos dang//tshogs mchog rnams la bdag skyabs mchi//
3b4(rgyud): yon tan rgya mtsho khyed rnams la//bdag gi lus 'di dbul bar bgyi//gnas dang chos kun ston pa dang//shin du mthar yang 'jam pa dang/
3b5(rgyud): yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis//de nyid sbyin sogs gzugs can gyi//sku gsung ye shes thob bya'i phyir//sten cing 'di la rab tu dang//sems can rnams kyi 
3b6(rgyud): don byed phyir//byang chub sems dag bdag gis bskyed//gang zhig bsod nams nyer bsags rnams//rdzogs pa'i byang chub bdag gis bsngo//'gro 'di kun la phan 'dzad phyir/
4a1(rgyud): @#//dpal ldan gshin rje mthar byed 'gyur//lhag par smon lam 'di byas pas//de rnams la ni gshegs su gsol//brtse bas byang chub bzhin du lta//khams gsum rnams
4a2(rgyud): ni stong par bsam//rang bzhin gyis ni spror bral bas//rgyu dang de bzhin mtshan nyid la//rnam par rtog pa bral ba dang//lha dang smon lam rnam par spangs//oM shu n+ya ta dz+nya na badzra swaM b+ha ba ad ma
4a3(rgyud): ko ha~M//ya~M ra~M ba~M la~M bzhi la dkyil 'khor bsdus nas//b+h+rU~M yig las skyes pa'i gzhal yas khang bskyed par bya'o//zur bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi yis brgyan pa la//dra ba
4a4(rgyud): dra phyed kyis spras pa//lha dang lha mos rnam par 'dzes//ba dan dril bu bcas pa rnams//sna tshogs rtse mo rlung gis skyod//ba~M yig las ni de dbus gnas//sna tshogs pad
4a5(rgyud): ma rnam par bsam//de'i ze 'bru'i dbus gnas pa//sna tshogs rdo rje dbyug par bcas//yongs su gyur las mtha' dag rtag//zhe sdang bdag nyid rnam par bsgom//
4a6(rgyud): ri 'tshungs pad ma nyi ma la//ma he dkar po'i steng du ni//sprul pas bdud rnams ma lus pa//g.yon rkyang zhabs kyis mnan par bya//nyi ma bye ba'i 'od 'bar lha//ro langs ma yis mgul nas
4a7(rgyud): 'khyud//dpa'o zhal cig phyag gnyis pa//klu brgyad kyis ni rnam par brgyan//spyan gsum dmar zhing 'jigasu rung//mgo bo dmar po brgya'i 'phreng//g.yas na rdo rje dbyug pa dang//g.yon na
4b1(rgyud): thod pa snams pa'o//khro bo'i 'tshungs par ldan pa dang//ljags ni skyod cing 'che ba brtsigs//khro gnyer sku 'khregs rkyed pa la//stag gi pags pas rnam par brgyan//thung zhing lto 'phyang rngam
4b2(rgyud): zhing dmar//shin du rnam dag dri ma bral//ro langs ma'i g.yas gri khug//g.yon na mi'i thod pa'o//spyan gsum skra ni grol ba dang//phyag rgya lnga yis rnam par brgyan//mgo bo'i phreng bas 'tshungs
4b3(rgyud): par ldan//ro bcud ldan pa 'dzin gtso bo//shar gyi 'dab ma la gti mug rdo rje dkar mo//rdo rje tsar tsi ka dang snyoms par 'jug pa'o//lho'i 'dab ma la gshin rje gshed kha dog ser po'o//rdo rje phag
4b4(rgyud): mo dang snyoms par zhugs pa'o//nub kyi 'dab ma la 'dod chags gshin rje gshed kha dog dmar po/rdo rje dbyangs can ma dang snyoms par zhugs pa 'o//byang gi 'dab ma la
4b5(rgyud): phra dog rdo rje gshin rje gshed kha dog ljang khu rdo rje gau rim dang snyoms par zhugs pa'i//tsar tsi ka nag mo'o/phag mo ser mo'o//dbyangs can ma dkar mo'o//gau mi sngon mo'o/
4b6(rgyud): g.yon na thod pa dang g.yas pa na gri khug go//phyag dang zhal dang sku mdog yang dag par gnas pa ni/dpa'o'i ji lta ba bzhin no//me'i 'tshams na mu men gyi bum pa bzhi mnyam gyis gang ba'o/
4b7(rgyud): bden bral du gser gyi bum pa bla ba yis gang ba'o//rlung du bye dur+ya'i bum pa se h+las gang ba'o//dbang ldan du dngul gyi bum pa ga bur gyis gang ba'o//thod pa kha g.yogs pa rnam par sgom par bya'o/
5a1(rgyud): /khro bo'i rgyal po lasogs pa/dpa' bo gtso bo'i sngags gsungs pa ni//oM hrI Sh+TiHbi tri tI na na hU~M hU~M phaT//swahA//snying po'i sngags so/oM hrI Sh+Tau k+sha ya~M ka rA ya/g+h+ri ha na 
5a2(rgyud): g+h+ri h+na/hU~M hU~M phaT swAhA//nye ba'i snying po'i sngags so//oM mahA yak+sha se na pa ta ye/mahA dur dAn ta da ma na ya na sha ya na sha ya na/Sha na ya bi tA pa ya b+hi ta pa la hU~M hU~M phaT/swahA/phreng sngags so/
5a3(rgyud): oM d+he sha ri ta nu ta nu ta tru ta Tu ta hU~M dza/swAhA/rje btsun ma'i sngags so//oM ma hA na ta tri ta hU~M hU~M phaT/oM pa shu na na ta hri da hU~M hU~M phaT/o~M ra gan ta krid hU~M hU~M phaT//oM ir Shu na ta 
5a4(rgyud): krid hU~M hU~M phaT/dpa'o bzhi'i sngagaso//oM mo ha ra ti hU~M dza/oM bi shu na tra ti hU~M dzaHoM ra ka ra ti hU~M dzaHoM ir+ya Sha ra ti hU~M dzaHdpa' bo'i dbang phyug ma 
5a5(rgyud): bzhi'i sngags so//oM mar me mar mA b+hi gi te/oM badzra tsar tsi ke ha ra ha bu k+ka ma rA yA/che ge mo la dzaHoM g+ho ne su g+ho ne badzra ma ma ki/b+hi ra b+ha ra/saM b+ha ra 
5a6(rgyud): saM b+ha ra/hU~M dzaHoM mar me ma k+sho b+ha ni sa ra si ta/da ra da ra/tha ra tha ra/pha ra pha ra/ka~M pa ka~M pa/d+hu na d+hu na hU~M dzaHche ge mo la dzaHoM b+hi ma b+hi re badzra ka ra ya~M tsi ke g+he g+ha/ra g+ha 
5a7(rgyud): sho ni ta tri ye gA+au ri//gdengs ka'i dbang po dga'o ni//sngon pos skra cig 'jigs par byed//klu mchog dmar pos rna ba brgyan cing rtse mo 'khyil pa ste/rigs ldan khra bos dpung pa rgyan//
5b1(rgyud): lag rdub gdengs ka dung skyong ser//shin du dkar ba pad mo shal//tha mas sngon po mchod phyir thogs//shin du 'dzes par byas pa'o//bdud rtsi kha dog gdengs ka ni//nor rgyas kyis ni gdengs ka 
5b2(rgyud): byas//pad mo'i sdong bu kha dog dkar//rkang rdub pad ma chen po yis//thams cad phung ta'i yi ge sgrogs//phyag gnyis pa dang mche ba brtsigs//'khor ba dang ni mya ngan 'das//gshin rje 
5b3(rgyud): 'joms pa zhes bya ba//rnam rtog thams cad 'joms pa yi//sngags kyis byin gyis brlabs par bya//mkha' khyab rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun//rang gi snying khar sa bon gyis//lcags kyus 
5b4(rgyud): kun bkug khar bzhugs nas//bde ba snying khar zhugs bzhin du//de las spros byas sa bon rnams//de zhu rdo rje'i lam nas ni//rang 'od pad ma'i nang mthar gnas//de ltas
5b5(rgyud): sa bon rang gzugs la//de'i sa bon las skyes pa'i//'khor lo sa bon kun byin brlab//me stag 'khro ba'i tshogs gsal ba//cho ga bzhin du mkhas pas bsgoms//lta 
5b6(rgyud): zhing snying khar zhu byas pa//rdo rje'i lam nas dbyung bar bya//rang rig pad ma'i ze 'bru la//lha'i tshogs rnams lta byas la//sna tshogs me tog la sogs dbul//lag na lo ma 'dzin rnams kyis/
5b7(rgyud): legs par mchod par bya ba ste//mchod pa'i klu ni yid 'ong dang //sna tshogs gar gyi rang bzhin dang //de bzhin bde ba chen pos mchod//e ma gshin rje gshed dmar po//bla ma srin 
6a1(rgyud): @#//po'i rang bzhin te//bdag ni khyod mthong 'jigs pa nas//bla ma srin po'i rang bzhin spongs//mtho ris sa stengs sa 'og rnams//khyod kyi gar gyis g.yo bar byed//khrag gi(sa)
6a2(rgyud): mig sman khro bo'i sems//gar mkhan khyod ni ro langs gzugs//dmar zhing thung ba'i tshad ldan pa//rnam pa mang po'i sprul gzugs byed//rdo rje dbyangs can ma yis zhus//khyod kyis gar 
6a3(rgyud): mdzad bde ba che//khyod ni hrISh+Ta sngags kyi gar//sa gsum pa yi 'khrul pa sel//rtse bas khro bo'i rje btsun khyod//'gro la lta zhing gar 'dzad do//oM sarba ta thA g+ha ta pu tsa saM b+ha ba 
6a4(rgyud): ad mo ko haM//sgra'i tshogs pa thams cad la//sgra snyan dang ni 'tshungs par lta//'khor lo yi ni 'dren pa la;de yis de la de bzhin bstod//gti mug rdo rje'i rang 
6a5(rgyud): bzhin khyod//gshin rje gshed po shin du 'jigs;ston pa sangs rgyas kun rang bzhin//rdo rje'i sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//'phra ma rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod//
6a6(rgyud): gshin rje gshed po shin tu 'jigs//rdo rje'i thugs dang rab 'tshungs pa'i//rang bzhin rdo rje phyag 'tshal bstod//'dod chags rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod //'dod chags chos kyi rang bzhin gtso//
6a7(rgyud): gsum mchog kun gyi dbang mchog ste//gsum gyi rdo rje phyag 'tshal bstod//'phra dog rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod//gshin rje gshed po las kun pa//rdo rje'i sku dang rab 'tshungs pa//phyag na 
6b1(rgyud): ral gri phyag 'tshal bstod//sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin khyod//sangs rgyas thams cad gcig bsdus pa//sangs rgyas kun gyi rab mchog gtso//dkyil 'khor dbang phyug phyag 'tshal stod/
6b2(rgyud): a yig rnam dag pa//mgo bo gsum gyi steng du gnas//pad ma'i snod ni gang gang ba'i//de 'og me'i dkyil 'khor ste//ra~M yig las byung gru gsum po//de 'og rlung gi dkyil 'khor ste//
6b3(rgyud): gnag cing g.yo ba rlung gi dbyibs//ya~M las byung ba ba dan mtshan//de las rnam par snang 'dzad swogs//da yig lasogs las byung ba//dbang po lasogs lnga rnams so//hU~M skyes rdo rje'i dgab 
6b4(rgyud): bya 'di//bkab pa mkhas pa sgom par bya//rlung gis skyod cing me yis zhu//de la rnam par sgom par bya//rdo rje 'tshungs par ro gcig 'gyur//shin du dri med dag par lta//hU~M 
6b5(rgyud): yig 'od kyi sangs rgyas rnams//ye shes gzugs kyis dgug par bya//rab zhugs sngags kyi dbang gis ni//mchog gi bdud rtsi lnga rnams lta//de nyid gsum gyis byin 
6b6(rgyud): gyis brlab//dkyil 'khor bdag po rnams la mchod//lce la rnam dag rdo rje nyid//hU~M yig las byung de la lta//de'i 'od zer sbubs rnams las//'tshungs par dgug cing de yis ni//kham+sa 
6b7(rgyud): gsum bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas kun//de bzhin dkyil mkhor mchog 'di la//snying kha'i ye shes dgug par bsam//rnam par rtog pa thams cad spangs//thog mtha' dbus ma'i dbye ba y+si//
7a1(rgyud): @#//dpag med bzung bya de la yang//'khrug pa'i gzugs la de nyid med//gang la 'khrug pa gnyis la gnas//'khrul med thams cad sems la sbyin//'khro dang bsdu ba rnam par bzung//
7a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//sku dang gsung dang thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/mngon rtogs kyi rnam par phye ba ste/dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
7a3(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyis /bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' chen po la bstod pa'i rgyal po 'dis byin gsol pa btab pa dang/ji ltar sems can rab 'jug la//las kyi dbye bas rab dbye las//
7a4(rgyud): ye shes kyis ni rgya mtsho rnams//rdo rje chen po bshad kyis nyon//de nas rdo rje 'dzin rgyal pos//las kun thams cad rab sgrub pa//nyes kun bso ba'i bdag nyid kyis//tshig 'di nyid ni 
7a5(rgyud): bka' stsal to//de nas bcom ldan 'das da bzhin gshegs pa rnams//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thugs rdo rje rab tu gsal bar 'dzad pa la/bcom ldan 'das ma phyag 'tshal 
7a6(rgyud): zhing mchod te/g.yas phyogasu lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/yang dang yang du phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' nyan par 'dzad do//de nas rdo rje 'dzin brgyal pos//dbang du bya ba'i 
7a7(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor gsungs//'khrul 'khor mthar thug gshin rje gshed//ma byung bar ni mi 'gyuro//bud med dbang du bya ba dang//srung ba'i don du de bzhin du//zhi ba la ni de gzhin du//de bzhin gro ga snyug
7a8(rgyud): shun lo//'khor lo gnyis ni mngon par bri//ming ni na mos rten par bya//nag po'i rtse swogs dang bral ba'i//kham phor gnyis ni yang dag sbyar//dbus su zhun mar sbrang blug//skrang pa dkar pos bri bar bya//
7b1(rgyud): bdud rtsi lnga yis yongs su gang//me tog dkar pos thun 'tshams gsum//mchod bya ba ni shar du phyogs//gshin rje mthar byed chu shel bzhin//bdag nyid sgoms nas bsgrub bya ni//'dun du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor ste/
7b2(rgyud): ltas la mngon par dbang skur ro//bum pa dkar po zla ba bzhin//bdud rtsi lnga yis yongasu gang//sangs rgyas lnga yis khrus 'dzad pa//yang dag lta zhing zlas pa brtsam//oM hriSh+Ti bi tri ta na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT na ma//
7b3(rgyud): lhas byin la shA na ta~M/ku ru na ma/zhi ba'i dam tshig 'di lta ste/skyes bu rnams kyi zhi ba la//gur gum gyis bris rgyas pa yang//'khor lo gnyis bris kha phye yis//swahA'i dbus su ming bcug ste//sgrub
7b4(rgyud): bya'i ming blang de rnams la//phu yis mngon par rtor bar bya//kham phor kha sbyor nang bcug ste//srad bu ser pos bkri bar bya//kha ni byang ltas thun 'tshams su//me tog ser pos mchod par bya//
7b5(rgyud): kha dog ser po gshin rje gshed//bdag nyid nges par lta bar bya/sgrub bya zla ba ser la gnas//bum pa ser po chu yis bkru/mngon par dbang skur sgom par bya//rgyas par bya phyir yang dag zla//
7b6(rgyud): oM hrISh+Ti bi kri ta na na hU~M hA~M phaT swahA//lhas byin buSh+Tid ku ru ye swAhA//bA+o Sh+ra Ta//oM hrISh+Ti bi tri ta na na hU~M hU~M phaT/phaT//lhas byin gyi puSh+Ti ku ru /bau Sh+ka Ta/ces pas ni/bcom ldan 'das kyis rgyas par
7b7(rgyud): tshig tu gsungs so//gro ga 'am yang na ras dag la//rgya skyags dag gi khu ba 'am//yang na tsan dan dmar po la//ming med dag gi khrag sres nas//'khor lo gnyis pa yang dag bri//hU~MHyiges rten byas pa yi//
7b8(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor nag sogs dang bral ba//kham phor gnyis ni yang dag sbyar//mar dang sbrang rtsi bcug byas la//srad bu dmar pos mchod par bya//kha ni nub tu mngon phyogs nas//me tog dmar pos mchod par bya//
8a1(rgyud): /kha dog dmar po 'bar ba che//bdag nyid gshin rje 'joms byed pa//sgrub bya za ba dmar po'i steng//de bzhin du ni gnas par bya//rang gi lus las thon gyur te//'od zer dmar po'i lcags
8a2(rgyud): kyu yis//sgrub bya 'gugs par bsgom par bya//sngags ni khri tsam spro zhing zlas//des ni sgrub bya smyos 'gyur te//rkang pa dag ni de bzhin 'gyel//gos bral ba ru//bsam zhing zlas pa brtsam par bya//
8a3(rgyud): gal te dbang du ma gyur na//rtul shugs can gyi 'khor lo spro//mar sogs bral bar byas nas ni//du med seng lteng me la'o//oM hrISh+Ti bi kri ta na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT/lhas byin gyi mchod byin gyi
8a4(rgyud): dbang du gyis shig swAhA//oM hrISh+Ti bi tri ta na na hU~M hUM phaT phaT bau Sha Ta/lhas byin mchod sbyin du gyis shig/bau shra Ta zhes pa ni/'dir bcom ldan 'das kyis dbang gi dam tshig tu gsungaso/
8a5(rgyud): yang na dud khrod gos dag gam//rang 'byung gis byugs ras dag la/rgya skyegs khrag dang sres pa yis//'khor lo gnyis ni yang dag bri//dzaHyig dang ni hriHyig gis//sgrub bya'i ming ni
8a6(rgyud): rten byas la//bud med pad ma'i snod dag ni//kha sbyar nang bcu ga mchod par bya//srad bu dmar pos bkri byas la//me tog dmar pos mchod par bya//nyi ma 'char ka dang 'tshungs dmar//bdag nyid
8a7(rgyud): gshin rje 'joms byed do//kha dog dmar po'i lcags kyu yis//sgrub bya 'gugs par bsgom par bya//snying khar lcags kyus dbyug bya zhing//mgrin par zhags pas bcing bar bya//seng ge dmar la zhon
8a8(rgyud): nasu//rlung gi dkyil 'khor sbyor ba yis//'ong ba dag tu bsam bya zhing//bdud rtsi lnga yi chu yis bkru//gal te sgrub pa ma 'ongs na//rtul shugs can gyi 'khrul 'khor spro//seng lteng shing gi me la'o//
8b1(rgyud): de nas zlas pa yang dag brtsam//oM hrISh+Ti bi tri te na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT/che ge mo khug cig hriHoM hrISh+Ti bi trite na na hU~M hU~M phaDa phaT/dza//lhas byin khug cig dza ces pa ni/'dir bcom ldan 'das kyis dgug 
8b2(rgyud): pa'i dam tshig tu gsungaso//blo ldan gang gis dud khrod gos//yung ba dag gi khu ba yis//'khor lo gnyis ni yang dag bri//lam yig dag gis rten byas la//sgrub bya ming ni yang dag bzung//kham phor kha sbyor
8b3(rgyud): dbus su 'o//kham phor ser po steng du gzhag//de la mkhas pas re kha bri//bdun dang bzhi pra ka swogs//yang dag bris te ting 'dzin bya//ri rab rtse mo brgyad par grags//de'i steng du yang dag bri//hU~M yig dang 
8b4(rgyud): ni ba~M yig ste//de'i dbus su ri rab po//de'i steng du lam yig gis/dbang chen dkyil 'khor dgod par bya//sna tshogs rdo rjes mnan zhing bya//srad bu ser pos bcing bar bya//gshin rje 'joms 
8b5(rgyud): byed kha dog ser//bdag nyid kha ni lho ru phyogs//dbang chen dkyil 'khor mnan pa ni//sgrub bya ser mo rnam par bsam//myur du ri ba dgod cing mnon//sna tshogs rdo rjes mnanabyas la//
8b6(rgyud): dbang chen dkyil 'khor 'og dag tu//man d+ha ra sogs ri 'jigs dang//gangs ri mi g.yo gyur pa yis//sgrub bya man par sgom par bya//de la 'di bzhin zlas par bya//oM hriSh+Ti bi kri ta na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT//la~M lha 
8b7(rgyud): rang gnas su rengs la swAhA//ces pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi rengs pa chen po'i dam tshig go//ngag ni rengs par 'dod pa yis//'khor lo gnyis po de bzhin bri//nang du sgrub bya'i ming byas nas//de la sngags ni spro 
8b8(rgyud): zhing zlas//oM hrISh+Ti bi tri te na na hU~M hU~M phaDa phaT/lhas byin gyi 'dod pa'i don gyi phyir 'gog cing ngan par smros shig//mchog sbyin gyi ngag stam b+ha ya/nam ku ru la~M/ces pa ni bcom ldan 'dasakyis ngag rengs pa'i 
9a1(rgyud): @#//dam tshig tu gsungaso//shin tu mthar thug legs par sba//ji ltar 'di'i bshad par bya//nyi ma gcig gis grub par 'gyur//gshin rje gshed po sbyor bas so//dud khrod ras la sngon bzhin du//
9a2(rgyud): 'khor lo gnyis bris rtul shugs can//skye tse ba dang tsha dang ni//dug dang nim pa ka 'am ni//tsha ba gsum dang chos kyang ngo//de bzhin (dang rung ngo)sal ba dang//mdo stu ra'i lo ma'i chu//tsan Ta'i sa bor de bzhin du//
9a3(rgyud): 'dzub mo yi ni khrag rlangs te//tsi kra ka'i khu bas sam//tsha sgo can gyi sa blangs te//rdol pa'i rdza'i breg pa dang//bya rog sgro'i snyug gu yis//bcu bzhi la bris rtul shugs can //gud thung khro bo'i sems kyis 
9a4(rgyud): ni//bdug pa can rnams bsad pa'i phyir//sna tshogs sems can rnams 'joms pa//hU~M gi yi ges mtha' rten la//rnal 'byor kha ni lho ru phyogs//bdag nyid gshin rje 'joms byed ste;
9a5(rgyud): khro bo'i gzugs can rtum po che//mgo bo'i dum bus rnam par rgyan/mahe la zhugs ljags skyod cing//gsus chen 'jigs par byed pa'o//mche rtsigs skra ni gyen du rje sa//smin ma dmar ser 
9a6(rgyud): de bzhin du//g.yas na rdo rje dbyug chen la//de bzhin g.yon na thod pa'o//mi sdug mche ba rab tu rtsigs//skal pa'i me ni rab 'bar bzhin//de bzhin bdag nyid rnam par sgom//myur du bsgrub bya de bdun bzung//
9a7(rgyud): dri ma dang ni rma dag kyang//bdug pa'i nad kyis bzung zhing 'joms//gos dang bral zhing 'dar bar byed//ngal zhing lus ni rma dang ldan//phol gyi phreng bas lus la bzung//mtshon gyis rma ni gdod par byed//grang ba 
9b1(rgyud): dang ni rlung gis rtses//me'i dbus su yang dag rten//ma he stag dang khyi rnams dang //mchu rings rnams kyis za bar byed//sangs rgyas sde rnams 'joms byed pa'i//ma rungs chen po 'arnams kyis ni//lto'i nad nas 
9b2(rgyud): rgyu ma dang /lus la tsher mas gang ba dang //rkang pa gdos dang sgrog gis sdams//de bzhin dum bur dum bur byas//pags pa nye bar bshu bar byas//skye tsha yis ni byug par bya//drang ba yis ni lus 'don sgom;
9b3(rgyud): de'i lus kyi srung la sogs//bcad cing bdag nyid mdun du bzung //de'i go cha bcad par bya//lus ni de bzhin stong par sgom//bdag gi lus las skyes pa yi//'khro bo gshin rje gshed gzugs kyis//
9b4(rgyud): 'jom pa dag tu sgom bya zhing //rkang dang tshil rnams 'thung ba ste//de'i mdun du gshin rje sgom//lag na dbyug pa stobs chen ldan//ma rungs de yis bdug pa de//rnam par 'joMs
9b5(rgyud): par sgom par bya//de'i rgyu ma rtsa rnams ni//bya dgod kyis bzung mkha' la 'gro//klu yis sa 'og 'gugs par byed//lha ma yin la sogs pas btsir//de ltar sgrub bya bsam par bya//
9b6(rgyud): snying rje'i sems kyis rab bcings nas//'khor ba las ni pha rol 'gro//sangs rgyas rnams kyi zhing du 'gro//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams//thugs dgas nas gsungs pa 'di lta ste/
9b7(rgyud): kye ma gsang bya legs par byas//bsad par byas pa ma bsad te//ji srid sdig rnams grol bar byas//shi ba de yang ma shi ste/sdig pa stong phrag byas pa rnams//mnar med la sogs mi skye'o//e ma 
9b8(rgyud): bdag nyid chen po ste//bsad pas sangs rgyas byang chub thob//slar yang rtse bas sems can rnams//bsad par bya bas bsad par bya//e ma snying rje stobs kyis te//snying rje dman pas 'grub mi 'gyur//
10a1(rgyud): //oM hrISh+Ti bi tri tI na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT/hU~M lha byin sod/hU~M/oM hrISh+Ti bi tritA na na hU~M hU~M phaDa phaT/hU~M phaT/lha byin ma ra ya hU~M phaT/ces pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyis rtog pa chen po bsad pa
10a2(rgyud): rdo rje'i 'khrul 'khor dang/sngags dang rgyud kyis bsad pa la sogs pa'i dam tshig tu gsungs//de nas 'khor lo gnyis bris te//phaT kyi yi ges nang du ni//sgrub bya'i ming dang bcas pa yang//thod pa yang dag
10a3(rgyud): sbyar bya ste//srad bu nag pos bkri bar bya//zhe sdang sems kyi nyin gung la//dud khrod du ni zhugs par bya//gshin rje mthar byed 'tshungs ltas nas//bsgrub bya rang gi mdun du bya//rta dang mahe steng na gnas//
10a4(rgyud): gnyis po de'i gal na mtshon//khro bo'i tshogs kyis kun nas skor//gnyis po chogs la bsam par bya//gnyis po dag ni mtha' byed cing//khro bas sems can bcings pa ste//'thab mo byed par sgom
10a5(rgyud): byas la//sgom zhing zlas pa rtsam par bya//oM hrISh+Ti bi tri tI na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT/lha byin dang mchog sbyin dag rnam par zhe sdang bar gyis shig//phaT ces pa ni/bcom ldan 'das kyis
10a6(rgyud): zhe sdang med pa'i dam tshig tu gsungs so//cho ga de yis snga ma bzhin//'khor lo gnyis ni kun du bri//hU~M phaT yi ge dag gis rten//thod pa gnyis ni yang dag sbyar//srad bu nag pos yang dag kri
10a7(rgyud): pha gshin nags su gzhag byas la//gshin rje gshed kyi rnal 'byor bya//bsgrub bya sngon du bzung bar sgom//ya~M las byung ba bzhu'i dbyibs//rlung gi dkyil 'khor nag po la//rnga mo chen mo kha dog sngo//de'i steng du
10a8(rgyud): sgom par bya//de la ma rungs chen po ste//bde gcing lho phyogs 'ded pa dang//khro bo rnams kyis 'joms pa dang//sangs rgyas yin yang zlog par dka'//oM hrISh+Ti bi+i tri tA na na hU~M hU~M phaDa phaT/lha sbyin skrod par gyis shig//
10b1(rgyud): hU~M phaT ces pa ni/bcom ldan 'das kyis srad pa chen po'i dam tshig tu gsungaso//rnal 'byor khro zhing lho phyogs la//khrag bcas dud khrod thal ba dang//dug dang tsha dang skye tshe dang//tsha ba'i mar khu snyo lo mas//
10b2(rgyud): de bzhin dud khrod gos la ni//'khor lo gnyis pa de bzhin bri//gsang bya gshin rje'i lus la gnas//sdang sems ma he ta dag la//skrad pa lnga po lus la gnas//zhi bzla ba'i dkyil 'khor dkar//
10b3(rgyud): rgyas pa zla ba ser po la//bud med snying kha dbang du bya//ngag bcing ri lu la gnas te//rengs pa'i ri rab dbus su ste//'gugs pa seng ge stag la gnas//de bzhin gzhan yang mtshon par bya//g+hi 
10b4(rgyud): wang dang rgya skyegs dang//de bzhin kha phye'i bye brag gis//dbang bya gro ga shing shun bri//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//kham phor kha sbyar nad du gzhag//dge ba'i sems kyis 
10b5(rgyud): yang dag bzung//bya rog sgro yis bri bar bya//pad ma kha sbyar dud khrod la//nyi ma gung la sba bar bya//gshin rje gshed kyi rnal 'byor dag//hrI Sh+Ti la sogs sngags zlas la//
10b6(rgyud): lho phyogs gnas te tshar bcad do//zhi ba'i kha dog ye yig dbyi//ni gnyis kyang ni de bzhin no//phyi nas dbus su ya yig las//lhag ma'i yi ge mi bde bar//sngon du snyen pa khri zlas la//
10b7(rgyud): las 'di rab tu grub par bya//ye shes rdo rjes bya ba ste//gshin rje gshed kyi sbyor ba yis//drag po chen po'i dkyil 'khor 'di//gang du bris shing gnas pa dang//yi ge bris par gyur pa yis//khyim de 
10b8(rgyud): rtag tu thab mor gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/'khrul 'khor gyi skabs rim par phye ba gnyis pa'o//ri dang skye bos dben pa'i phyog+sa//
11a1(rgyud): @#//gsang zhing yang rtags sa gzhi la (mo)gsal bris rnal 'byor dag//sngags kyis 'grub pa brtsam par bya//dkar zhing zhal cig phyag gnyis pa//gri khug thod pa 'dzin pa yis//tsar tsi ka 
11a2(rgyud): ni sgom par bya//rnal 'byor khrag 'gugs rab sbyor bar//de nas bcom ldan 'das gshin rje gshed chen po bkrug pa rdo rje khrag 'gugs zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la bzhugaso//rdo rje tsar tsi ka'i sngags 
11a3(rgyud): gsungs pa 'di lta ste/oM badzra tsar tsi ke si d+han tra na la ha ri ni rad na tra ya pa ri tse ru d+hi ra ma kar sha ya dza//zhal ni rab tu rkyang 'dzad nas//sngags 'di rab tu dran byas la//khams gsum pa'i khrag chen po//
11a4(rgyud): 'gugs byed 'di la the tsom med//de nas gshin rje gshed po'i rdo rje/nyos pa 'gugs pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugaso//rdo rje phag mo chad 'gugs pa 
11a5(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i dam tshig gsungs pa 'di lta ste//oM g+ho Ne su g+ho Ne badzra ma ma ki/b+ha ra b+ha ra/saM b+ha ra saM b+ha ra/tre d+ha tu ka ma t+yu ka ra sha ya dza//rdza mkhan lag pa 
11a6(rgyud): phyis pa'i sas//bum pa kha ni 'dzes byas te//rkun mo'i bkra'i steng du gzhag//sngags ni rjesu bsgrub par bya//sngags 'di bdag nyid che ba ni//mchod rten pa ta na ru bstan//mkhas pas 'bad pas 
11a7(rgyud): dmigs byas nas//chad ni 'gugs par rab tu grags//de nas bcom ldan 'das gshin rje gshed kyi rdo rje/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs so//oM pi dzu bi 
11a8(rgyud): dzu dz+ha bar ha ni//dz+wa la dz+wa la/me d+ha khar d+ha ni/d+hi ri d+hi ri/bud d+hi bar d+ha ni swAhA//tshes gcig nas ni bzung nas ni//ji srid rtsa lnga'i mthar thug kun//legs par sbyar bar brtsam par bya//ji srid zla ba'i 
11b1(rgyud): rjes 'brangs nas//gsung gi dbang phyug ma ni 'grub//'di yis tshigs bcad rgya phyed do//khams gsum pa ni ma lus shing//ma lus lus pa med pa 'grub //de nas bcom ldan 'das gshin rje gshed mthar
11b2(rgyud): byed chen po drug pa rdo rje 'gugs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugaso//khu ba 'gugs pa gau ri sngags gsungs pa//'di lta ste/oM ka Ti nI/kar shrI g+ho ri dza hri/du sha Ta bya du shra Ta ya/
11b3(rgyud): shu kra ma ka ra sha ya/dza dza hU~M swAhA//nam mkha'i gdong dang rdo rje'i lam//rlung dang bril sbyor lcags kyu yis//khu ba'i tshogs rnams 'gugs par byed//sgom dang zlas pa rab sbyor bas//skye bos dben
11b4(rgyud): dang nags tshal du//las 'di rab tu grub par bya//mtshan mo bdun gyi sbyor ba yis//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//de nas bcom ldan 'dangs rdo rje gshin rje gshed chen po//dus kyi dbye
11b5(rgyud): ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinal snyoms par bzhugaso////'dir mchog gi dbang skur ba'i dam tshig nyid kyi sku dang/gsung dang thugs rdo rje rnams las spro ba 'dzad nas/oM dum
11b6(rgyud): par man d+ha ra/yongs su skyes dang sna ma dang//kar Ni ka'i phreng ba rnams//bu khyod gzungs la byin gyis rlabs//khams gsum ral gri chen po la//sangs rgyas kun gyis phyag byas pas//bzung na bdud
11b7(rgyud): las rgyal bar 'gyur//las kyi ral gri dam pa 'di//thabs dang shes rab rang bzhin gyis//rdo rje dril bu bsgrub pa'i phyir//bu khyod bsad pa'i rdo rje zung//slob ma yang dag bzung bar gyis//'di ni btung ba rin chen ste//
11b8(rgyud): mi'i btung bya ye shes dgu//bu khyod kyis ni thams cad du//slob ma'i khar yang blud par bya//gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/rab tu gsungs//sems ni rab tu dga' ba yis//sa rdo rje slob ma snying rje che//
12a1(rgyud): @#//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las sngags kyis dgug pa rab tu sbyor ba ste/rim par phye ba gsum pa'o//de nas dpal ldan rdo rje ma chad//
12a2(rgyud): ral gri sgrib pa gsum par 'dzad//skyob pa sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//lha mo khyod ni dpang du gyur//gshin rje gshed kyi dkyil 'khor ni//slob dpon rnam gsum rgyu sgrub phyir//khab len lcags kyi ral
12a3(rgyud): gri nyid//de ni yab ni bcud can gyi//ral gri khru gang tshad bcas tshad//rtul shugs can gyis rnam par bsam//bcu bzhi 'am ni brgyad dag la//dud khrod dbus su sba bar bya//bdud rtsi lnga dang 'tshungs par

12a5(rgyud): grub nas ral gri rab tu 'bar//'od zer lnga'i klog chen 'khyug/grub pa de ni bzung byas pas//mi rnams khams gsum las rgyal 'gyur//rnal 'byor gang gis ral gri grub//
12a6(rgyud): gshin rje gshed po 'jigs byed sbyor//bde bar 'grub cing sa chen gyi//de'i 'thab pa bzod mi nus//rkang gnyis pa dang rkang bzhi kun//mthong bas gnas skabs bcu ru 'gyur//lha dang lha
12a7(rgyud): min gyis mi bzod//mi'i 'jig rten smos ci dgos//rgya mtsho yis ni blangs byas la//klu'i gzugs ni sbyar bar bya//oM phu'i yi ge hU~M las dgod//hrI Sh+Ti la sogs sngags rnams bzla//
12a8(rgyud): char mi 'bab pa'i dus rnams la//khri ni zlas pas 'bab par 'gyur//sbrul nag sha dang nim ba dang//lo ma dag ni sres pa yis//de'i ri lu legs par bya//rtul shugs can gyi rgya mtshor 'phang
12b1(rgyud): khri khrag zlas par 'gyur ba yis//rba rlongs rengs byed mchog yin no//tsan Ta'i sa bon brgya bzung zhing//mon sran bre 'u 'bras sres te//'bum phrag brgyad zlas sbyin sreg gi//de'i lha rnams mthong bar 'gyur/
12b2(rgyud): dud khrod ro sregs 'gyel ba'i shing//rtul shugs can gyi gzugs rnyan bri//snyo byed khu bas byug byas te//khri khrag zlas na 'dze yis 'khyer//nyungs kar sa bon sdus pa las//'bum phrag bzhi yis

12b4(rgyud): zug rnams 'joms par 'gyur//bdun nyid mngon par sngags byas te//lag pa mgo bo mnye byas nas//mgo bo'i zug rngu mi 'byung zhing//nyams par 'di la the tsom med//bram ze'i

12b7(rgyud): smras mtshan mo nyal ba la//dgra bo tho rangs mchi bar 'gyur//yang na nad kyis thebs 'gyur te//chus 'gags la sogs kyis khyab 'gyur//dgra las rnam par snang 'dzad blang//mi'i rkang sbubs yongs bkang ste//
12b8(rgyud): sngags ni 'bum du zlas na ni//shing gi drung du mngon par 'gyel//yid tags gnas su rtag tu 'khyam//rnal 'byor dbang phyug tshim pas bde//sna tshogs don rnams rab tu 'grub//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
13a1(rgyud): //gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs gshin rje gshed po'i rgyud las 'jigs par byed pa zhes bya ste/rim par phye ba bzhi pa 'o////de nas rgyal po rab sgrub pa//bdag gis yang

13b1(rgyud): 'phreng bas 'khrigs pa ste//rgyal po bsod par byed pa ni//'og gi cha la nges par sbyar//ming bcas kha dog yang dag ldan//ji lta ba'i rnam par gnas//lha la yongasu rdzogs par bya//ji lta ba'i dngos po'i tshad//

23'a3(rgyud): bdud rtsi rab tu mang po 'od zer gyis bkug ste/de la rab tu bzhug par bya'o//de la rab tu bzhugs pas zla ba'i 'od zer 'tshogs pa shin tu lhag pa yongasu gyur pa las/kha ba lta bu sbrang rtsi 'tshogs pa'i 
23'a4(rgyud): gzugs kyis mnan par bya'o//phyi nas yi ge gsum gyis byin gyis brlab par bya ste/kha dog dang dri dang ro dang bcud dang ldan pa/ston ka'i zla ba dri ma med pa'i 'od lta bu/ye shes kyi 
23'a5(rgyud): bdud rtsi'i sbyor bas gtor nas grub pa bdun pa bya'o//gshin rje gshed kyi gzugs 'dzin pa'i sngags kyis khro bo rnaMs la zhags pa dang lcags kyu 'dzin cing 'khrog pa'i phyogs 
23'a6(rgyud): rnams kyi snying kha nas lcags kyus 'bigs shing /'grin par zhags pa bcings te/'gugs par sgom par bya'o//de nas oM hri Sh+Ti bi kri ta na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT tu Sh+Ti na pra sa pa ri pa ra a gar Sha ya dza/
23'a7(rgyud): sngags 'dis dbang po mig stong ser skya blang po ched kar po la zhon pa//lag pa g.yas pa dang g.yon pa rdo rje dang sdig 'dzub can bkug ste/shar du rnam par dgod bya'o//de bzhin du de'i sngags kyis nye ba'i 
23'a8(rgyud): dbang po bya khyung la gnas pa/rin po che'i spyod pan can/g.yas pa dbyug pa dang 'khor lo g.yon na dung dang nor bu 'dzin pa de'i g.yon mi ring ba na gnas pa'o//yang de bzhin du chu bdag kha dog dkar po 
23'b1(rgyud): sbrul la gnas pa/lag na klu'i zhags pa 'dzin pa/nub tu rnam par gnas pa'o//yang de bzhin du lus ngan po ser po gter la gnas pa/lag na pi tsu pu ra ka dang na ku li snams pa/byang du rnam par gnas pa'o/
23'b2(rgyud): de bzhin du dang ldan dkar po byi ba la gnas pa/gnas na brang dreng dang dgra sta 'dzin pa/g.yon na la pug dang la tu'o//de bzhin du dbang ldan dkar po khyu mchog dkar po la gnas pa//ral ba 'dzin pa rtse 
23'b3(rgyud): gsum dang thod pa lag na snams pa/lag na pa gnyis g+hau ri la 'khyud pa/dbang ldan du gnas pa'o//de bzhin du me lha dmar po ra la gnas pa/lag na brang dreng dang spyi blugs snams pa/me'i phyogasu rnam 
23'b4(rgyud): bar gnas pa'o//yang de bzhIn du srin mo ni ro la zhon pa lag na dri khug dang thod pa kha dog sngon zhing khro ba rtum pa bden bral gi phyogasu rnam par gnas pa'o//yang de bzhin du rlung ni du ba'i kha dog ri dgas ser po la gnas pa 
23'b5(rgyud): rlung gi ba dan snams pa/rlung gi phyogs su rnam par gnas pa'o//yang de bzhin du nyi ma ni dmar po lag pa gnyis na pad ma 'dzin pa/rta bdun gyi steng na steng gi phyogasu rnam par gnas ba'o//yang 
23'b6(rgyud): de bzhin du zla ba ni dkar po lag pa gnyis na ku mu ta snams pa/ngang pa la zhon pa/steng gi phyogs su rnam par bzh-is pa'o//yang de bzhin du tshangs pa ni ser skya gdong bzhi pa/spyi blugs dang mi 'jiT 
23'b7(rgyud): pa dbyug pa dang brang dreng snams pa/ngang pa la zhan pa/steng gi phyogs su snam par gnas pa'o//yang de bzhin tu sa'i lha mo phyag g.yon na bum pa snams pa/g.yas na yang dam cha dang khas len gyi phyag rgya ste/
23'b8(rgyud): 'og gi phyogasu rnam par gnas nas pa'o//yang de bzhin thag bzangs ris sngon po shing rta la gnas pa/g.yas dang g.yon na ral gri dang sdig 'dzub 'dzin pa/de 'ang de bzhin du rnam par gnas pa'o//de rnams 
24a1(rgyud): /khro bo gshin rje gshed kyi gzugs lag na phur bu 'dzin pas/khro bo 'i 'grin pa dang snying ka'i sa bon dang 'od zer gyis bkug ste/de rnams kyi snying kha dang mgrin pa dang /spyi bo dang rkang pa'i mthar
24a2(rgyud): sngags 'dis phur bus gdab par bya/ste/oM hri Sh+Ti bi kri ta na na hU~M hU~M phaT phaT/hU~M sar+b+be na tra tI na ki la ye hU~M phaT/ces pa ni phur bu bar chad pa grub pa'i khu ba 'o//tshogs pa la sogs pa de thams cad kyang
24a3(rgyud): gshin rje gshed mthar byed kyi rnam par grub pa'i mthar/byang chub sems kyi khu ba'i gzugs rang gi sa bon gyi rtags kyi go rims kyis grub pa'i mthar la/de la hri yi ge'i sa bon las grub pa ni dbang po'o/
24a4(rgyud): Sh+Ti yi ge sa bon las grub pa ni/gshin rje 'i bi yig gi sa bon las grub pa ni chu bdag go//kri yig gi sa bon las grubapa ni lus ngan no//ta yig gi sa bon las grub ba ni dbang ldan no//
24a5(rgyud): a yig gi sa bon las grub ni me'o//na yig gi sa bon las grub po ni bden bral lo//na yig gi sa bon la grub pa ni rlung lha 'o//hU~M yig gi sa bon las grub na ni zla ba'o//hU~M yig
24a6(rgyud): gi sa bon las grub pa ni nyi ma'o//phaT yig gi sa bon las grub da ni tshangs pa'o//phaT yig gi sa bon pas grub pa ni nor rgyun ma'o//sa yig gi sa bon las grub ci thag bzangs ris so//ha yig
24a7(rgyud): gi sa bon las grub ni 'byung po thams cad do ces so//de bzhin du sgrub pa bsam par bya'o//de rnams kyi mig la sogs pa rnams la yang ji ltar gnas pa yang mig la sogs pa yang /rdzogs pa'i sku la
24a8(rgyud): sogs pa rnams la yang/sku rdo rje la sogs pa ji ltar mngon par dbang skur ba bzhin du/gshin rje mthar byed kyis dbu brgyan pa nyid do//yang mchod yon la sogs pa'i gsang gtor dang/zhal
24b1(rgyud): bsil lasogs pa dbul bar bya'o//phyi nas gtor ma nye bar dbul ba ni bdud rtsi sgrub pa'i go rims sngon du rdzogs pa la/da nyid gsum gyis mngon par sngags te/yang de rnams kyi ljags rab 
24b2(rgyud): tu sbyor ba sgom par bya'o//sngags 'dis bdud rtsi dbul bar bya ste/o rak+sha mu ku ya rag+ta mu kha ya/raka ta sha rA rA ya/rak+sha kA b+ha dzA yA /rak+ta mu Na Ta ma lA/a ba ra Na ya rak+ta ra tha sa mu ru 
24b3(rgyud): d+ha rak+ta pad ma a sa na ya/rak+sha b+ha ra Na b+hu sha Na ya/i dam b+ha ling griha na g+h+ri/ma ma b+hi g+hi/ha ra ha/tsa dur m+ma ra N+ni pu ra ya/pra sha ya pra sha ya/b+h+ra ma ya b+h+ra ma ya/tsh+tshin d+ha tsh+tshin d+ha/
24b4(rgyud): b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha/na sha ya na sha ya/sho Sha ya sho Sha ya/ts+tshed ts+tshed/b+he da b+he da/hU~M hU~M phaT phaT swahA//phyi nas so so'i cha yis bcad par bya/oM in+ta ya na maHhri ya ma ya na ma Sh+Ti ba ru na ya na ma b+hi /ku be 
24b5(rgyud): ra ya na ma hri/i sha ra ya na ma tA/a ge ya na ma/a ne ro t+ye/na ma na/ba ye ba na ma/tsan ta ya na ma hU~M/ar+k+ka ya na ma hU~M/pad me Ni na ma phaT/ba su d+ha ra ye na ma phaT/be ma tsi tri Ni na ma swa 
24b6(rgyud): sarba b+hud d+he b+h+yo na maHha ha ha/hi hi hi/hU~M hU~M hU~M/phe phe phe/phyi nas me tog dang /spos dang /mar me dang /zhal zas la sogs pa yang dbul bar bya'o//de rnams kyang sgrub pa po la mngon par rab tu 
24b7(rgyud): dang bas ro bcud thams cad myong bas longs spyod par 'gyur ro//rdo rje bsor zhing dril bu dag gis mkhros pas/gnas de la bstod par bya'o//gti mug dga' ma la swogs pa rnams la/phyi nas mngon par 'dod pa 
24b8(rgyud): zhus te/mchod pa'i mthar gzhan pa'i sngags rnams pa brgyad brjod par byas te/de la sngags 'dis gshegs su gsol lo//ha ha ha he ho ho hU~M hU~M/b+hir sar dz+dza ye ta/oM a mu ces pas kyang ngo //phyi nas sgrub pa 
25a1(rgyud): @#//po'i mngon par 'dod pas/sems can kun gyi don 'dzad phyir/ji ltar rjes 'jug dngos grub rtsol/sangs rgyas yul du gshegs nas kyang//slar yang 'dir ni byon par zhu/de bzhin gshegs pa 
25a2(rgyud): thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/gtor ma'i rim par phye ba ste bcu gcig pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bcom ldan 'das 
25a3(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' la bstod pa mdzad de/gti mug rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod/gshin rje gshed po shin tu 'jigs/ston pa sangs rgyas kun rang bzhin//rdo rje'i sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//'phra ma rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod|
25a4(rgyud): gshin rje gshed po shin du 'jigs//rdo rje'i thugs dang rab 'tshungs pa//rin chen rdo rje phyag 'tshal bstod//'dod chags rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod //'dod chags chos kyi 'byung gnas gtso//gsung 
25a5(rgyud): mchog kun gyi rab mchog ste//rdo rje'i gsung la phyag 'tshal stod/'phra dog rdo rje'i rang bzhin khyod//gshin rje gshed po las kun pa//rdo rje'i sku dang rab 'tshungs pa//lag na ral gri phyag 
25a6(rgyud): 'tshal bstod//sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin khyod//sangs rgyas thams cad gcig bsdus pa/sangs rgyas kun gyi rab mchog gtso//dkyil 'khor dbang phyug phyag 'tshal bstod//rdo rje'i mig dang/rdo rje'i 
25a7(rgyud): rna ba dang /rdo rje'i sna dang/rdo rje'i lce dang rdo rje'i lus dang/rdo rje'i yid ces bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa ni/mig la sogs pa byin gyis brlabs pa'i dam tshig go//de nas gshin rje gshed 'jigs 
25a8(rgyud): kyis//dkyil 'khor dag gis rab bshad bya//gshin rje gshed po dang 'tshungs pa//don rnams thams cad sgrub byed pa//srad bu bsar pa legs sgrims shing//'dzes shing tshad dang yang dag ldan//gshin rje gshed kyi 
25b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor la//shes rab can gyis thig btab po//mtshan nyid thams cad yongs rdzogs pa//don med thams cad rnam par 'jig//zhi ba la sogs rab sgrub pa'i//maN+Dal dkyil 'khor rnam pa la//las kyi rdo rje bris byas te/
25b2(rgyud): de la rtse lnga mtha' dag la//de'i 'bras bu rdo rje bri//rdo rje 'bar bas kun du 'khrigs//shar gyi rtsibs la 'khor lo bri//'od 'khor lo'i 'od zer kun du 'khrigs//lho ru rin chen bri byas te//'od zer phreng bas
25b3(rgyud): kun du khyab//nub tu pad ma bris byas te//pad ma ra ga'i 'od zer 'khrigs//byang du ral gri bri byas te//srin gyi 'od zer kha dog 'tshungs//shar gyi 'tshams su 'khor lo bri//lho ru rdo rje bri bar bya//nub du pad
25b4(rgyud): ma bri byas te//pad ma gdong byas rgyas pa'i zhal//byang du ral gri bri ba byas te//'od zer lnga dang 'tshungs par ldan//shar gyi sgo ru tho ba ste//rdo rje dbyug pa lho ru 'o//nub kyi sgo ru chu skyes te//rdo rje ral gri byang du'o/
25b5(rgyud): dkyil 'khor rdzogs par shes bya la//khyad par can gyis mchod par bya//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga rnams kyis//the tsom med pas sangs rgyas mchod//de bzhin gshegs pa
25b6(rgyud): thams cad kyi bdag po//rdo rje 'chang bo la bstod pa'i rgyal po 'dis gsol ba btab po//phyag rgya gsungs pa rab sbyor ba//gang gis rdo rje rnams la bstod//phyag rgya rdo rje'i phyag rgya yis//
25b7(rgyud): dngos grub mang po grub par 'dzod//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'chang //tshig 'di rnams ni gsung bar 'dzad//dkyil 'khor pa rnams thams cad kyi//spyi bor gshin rje gshed po sgom//rtag tu dkyil 'khor zlum byas la/
25b8(rgyud): rtag tu sbyin sreg mchod par bya//rtag tu dam tshig bsgrub bya zhing //de'i sgom par bzung bar bya//dang po spyod pa na dbang yin te//gnyis pa ral gri bsgom par bya//gsum pa rdo rje dril bu ste//bzhi pa zla ba bzang po'o//
26a1(rgyud): /dbang skur chen po bzhi po ni//raka ta'i zhal nas byung ba 'o//dbang 'di'i skur ba'i mthu yis ni//byang chub rgyal ba'i ro bcud thob//de nas bdag gis sngags bshad pas//'byung po kun la gtor ma bya//
26a2(rgyud): sngags chen po ni brjod pa yis//'byung po thams cad rab tu 'dar//sngags kyi sa bon hriSh+Ti sogs//mtha' ni swAhA nges par sbyar//bi ma Ta chu sres kyis//maN+Dal zur gsum byas nas ni//lha rnams mnyes byas 
26a3(rgyud): rnal 'byor pas//ha ha zhes sgrogs slar dran bya//'khor lo rim gsum bya ba ni//brgyad dang bcu gnyis bcu drug rnams//mkhas pas cho ga bzhin du bri//sngags ni kun du dgod par bya//rtul shugs can gyis 
26a4(rgyud): bcu gnyis par//sgrub pa'i sngags rnams dgod par bya//khams gsum g.yo dang mi g.yo ba//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//ya ma ra dza sa do me ya//Na yo da ya/ya da yo ni ra ya k+she ya/ya k+she ya 
26a5(rgyud): ts+tsha ni ra ma ya//ya dbus k+she ni shar du ste//'jam pa'i rdo rje lho ru ma//me yig nub tu bri bar bya//byang du da ni dkar po ste//sgrub bya'i ming ni bzung byas nas//stong pa'i 'tshams la dgod par bya//hU~M 
26a6(rgyud): gnyis dag gis rten par bya//dgos pa thams cad grub par byed//ya dza ni ra dza sa to ru//Na yo na ra ces pas kyang ni pha rol du//g.yon nas rtsam zhing yang dag bri//don kun rab tu sgrub bya'i phyir//ri dmig gsum 
26a7(rgyud): pa'i 'dun nas ni//shar nas brtsams te bri bar bya//hriSh+Ti ces pa'i sngags rnams kyis//re dmig brgyad kyi bar bar du//re dmig rnams la yang dag bri//che ge mo ku ru ma ra ya//u tsa ma ra Ta ya/b+ha shi ku be te 
26a8(rgyud): sha ya/shan ting ku ru Sh+TiM ku ru/ces pa la sogs ni/las kyi rjes su 'brang ste/dang por oM na mo mthar la ba Sha Ta bau Sha Ta hU~M phaT yi ge dang ldan pas/zhi rgyas dbang du byed pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 
26b1(rgyud): sku dang gsung thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/dbang skur ba dang 'khrul 'khor gyi snang ba zhes bya ba'i rim par phye ba ste bcu gsum mo////bcom ldan 'das kyis rtse bar 'dzad//gshin rje
26b2(rgyud): gshed chen yang dag gzugs//zhe sdang ginan 'gro la gzugs//zhe sdang thams cad zad par 'dzad//bcom ldan 'das kyis rtsa bar 'dzad//gti mug gshin rje gshed mchog gzugs//gti mug gis mnan
26b3(rgyud): 'gro la gzugs//gti mug thams cad zad par 'dzad //bcom ldan 'das kyis rtse par 'dzad//'phra dog gshin rje gshed po gzugs//'phra mas mnan pa'i 'gro la gzugs//phra ma thams cad zad par 'dzang/
26b4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis rtse ba 'dzad//'dod chags gshin rje gshed mchog gzu+ugs//'dod chags kyis mnan 'gro la gzigs//'dod chags thams cad zad par 'dza//bcom
26b5(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis rtse bar 'jad//'phra dog rdo rje gshed mchog gzugs //'phra dog gis mnan 'gro la gzigs //'phra dog thams cad zad par 'jad//byams pa tsar tsi kar gsungs te//
26b6(rgyud): phag mo de bzhin snying rje'o//dbyangs can dga' bar shes bya ste/btang snyoms gori gzugasu'o//bdag nyid dngos po thams cad kyis//slar yang dkyil 'khor yongs rdzogs pa//rnal 'byor snying kha'i sa bon spro//'grub
26b7(rgyud): 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//de nas spyod pa'i mchog//bdag gis yang dag bshad par bya//gshin rje gshed po'i 'jigs gzugs kyis//sku dang gsung thugs rnam par dag//mgon po chen po'i phyogs rnams la//
26b8(rgyud): mahe mchog la zhon byas te//sbrul gyi rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//lcags kyi rdo rje sgom par bya//skra ni dmar ser gyen du 'khyil//khyad du 'phags pa'i sku gzugs bya//g+ho la thod pas yang dag bskor//smag ra
27a1(rgyud): //dmar ser rjes pa ste//hriSh+Ti la sogs sngags rnams brjod//rdo rje dbyug pa yang dag sor//gshin rje gshed kyi rnal 'byor pas//seng ge'i sgra ni bsgrags par bya//cung zad mthu ni thob pa yis//grong khyer 
27a2(rgyud): zhugs te rol par bya//rab mdzes gar ni bya ba dang //drug 'gyur la sogs klu blang ngo //srang du rgyal mtshan de lta zhing //de la 'o ma rab tu sgrub//'o ma la bris rtse gcig sems//phyag rgya chen po rab bsgrub pa//phag mo'i 
27a3(rgyud): gzugs su sgom bya la//'dod pa'i mtha' la btsun mo kun//seng ge bzhin du 'jigs med las//'dod don kun 'grub rnal 'byor pas//de nas lag brgya rab sgrub pa//bdag gis yang dag bshad par bya//yi ge 
27a4(rgyud): gcig gi sngags chen te//'di ni zlas pa nges par bshad//Sh+Ti yig sa bon las byung ba'i//ma he'i gdong pa rnam par sgom//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor la gnas pa//ma he'i gdong du sgom par bya//gangs gzhi 
27a5(rgyud): la gnas pa yi//sna tshogs tshe'i rang gzugs rnams//blo dang ldan pas lag na sgom//ma he'i gdong can sgrub pa'o//de nas rdo rje dbyug pa'i sgrub thabs bdag gis rab bshad bya//yi ge hriHni
27a6(rgyud): gsang sngags che//ma he chen po la zhon pa//'bum phrag gnyis ni rdzogs pa'i phyag//thal ba'i rdul gyis lus la byugs//ri rab shin du mtho ba la//ro ldan 'og tu yang dag gnod//de nas phyag ni bya ba yi//
27a7(rgyud): sgrub thabs bdag gis rab gshad bya//bi kri ta na na gsang sngags che//hU~M yig las byung gtso bo ste//oM hriSh+Ti ta na na/ha~M ha hiHho hU~M hU~M he he swA hA//dgongs pa thams cad rab sgrub pa'i//dam tshig sgrub pa 'dir bshado//
27a8(rgyud): sngags kyis dub pa kha zas za//kha na ma tho med rnyed bla//sha chen de bzhin rta dang ni//de bzhin blang chen khyad par can//ba glang gzugs dang khyi dang ni//bong bu rnga mo lce spyang ngo //ram 'dang sre zhing btso byas nas//
27b1(rgyud): zhag chen lus la mngon par byug//'di yis lus la byugs tsam gyis//'gro ba mtha' dag dbang du byed//rnam snang si du ba ra nya//de bzhin bil pa'i lo ma dang//so phag phye ma lhan cig tu//gser zhing lo ma'i
27b2(rgyud): khu ba rnams//'di yis lus la bril phyir bya//rdo rje chen po bdud rtsi bzhin//rang 'byung me tog btung ba dang//'di ni rnal 'byor rab mchog//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs
27b3(rgyud): gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las spyod pa'i dam tshig sgrub pa rim par phye ba ste//bcu bzhi pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das skyes bu chen po'i dam tshig tsar ci ka'i rnam pa'i ting nge 'dzin la
27b4(rgyud): snyoms par zhugs pa na/rdo rje phag mo'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoMs par zhugs pa na/dbyangs can ma'i rnam pa'i ting nge 'dzin/la snyoms par zhugs pa dang//gau ri ma'i rnam pa'i ting nge 'dzun
27b5(rgyud): la snyoms pa las//mchod pa'i klu 'dis gsol ba btab po//e ma gshin rje gshed dmar po//bla ma srin po'i rang bzhin ste//bdag ni khyod mthong bas 'jigs pas//bla ma khro bo'i rang bzhin
27b6(rgyud): spongs//mtho ris sa stengs steng 'og rnams//khyod kyi gang gis g.yo bar byed//khrag gi mig sman khro bo'i sems//gar mdzad khyod ni rol pa'i gzugs//dmar zhing thung ba'i tshad ldan pa//rnam pa mang po
27b7(rgyud): sprul gzugs byed//rdo rje dbyangs can ma yis zhus//khyod kyi gar 'dzad bde ba che//khyod kyi hri Sh+Ti'i sngags kyi gar//sa gsum pa'i 'khrul pa sel//rtse ba'i khro bo'i rje btsun khyod//'gro la lta zhing
27b8(rgyud): gar mdzad do//de nas zlas pa'i mtshan nyid mchog//bdag gis yang dag rab bshad bya//gang gis zlas pa rab sbyor bas//mang po yis ni dngos grub thob//myur ba ma yin bul ba min//ring po ma yin thung ba min//
28a1(rgyud): @#//cung zad sngags ni thos par ro//gshin rje gshed kyi slob dpon mchog//mi dang mahe gnyis 'thung dang //ba glang gzugs dang rta dang ni//bung bu rnga mo rung bzung dang //zlas pa'i phreng bas rab sgrub bya//
28a2(rgyud): zla ba zla ba'i bcu bzhi la//bdud rtsi lnga dang ldan pa yis//sha lnga yis ni byug par bya//zlas pa yis ni dngos grub thob//so so'i rkang pa'i ri lu bya//gshin rje gshed sgom rnal 'byor pas//mkhas pas 
28a3(rgyud): yang na mi mgo ni//khrag 'dzag rlon pa sgom par bya//khrag gi sngags kyis 'byung pa rnams//mkha' 'gro ma rnams stong gis ste//gshin rje gshed po rab sbyor bas//yid tags rnams ni gzhom zhing bshad//
28a4(rgyud): rnal 'byor bdag nyid 'bum zlas na//las kun de yis byed par 'gyur//bye ba zlas na dngos grub ster//bye ba lnga yis smos ci dgos//nyi ma so so 'am yang na zla//yang na lo ni so so la//mkhas pas dbyug 
28a5(rgyud): gu drug bcu bzhi//gtor ma drug bcu rtsa bzhi sbyin//g+ha zhig rtag tu bza' ba dang //g+ha zhig rtag tu btung ba dang //gzhan yang bza' ba thams cad kyi//dang po gshin rje gshed la 
28a6(rgyud): 'bul//de nas bla ma yon gyi mchog//dbul bar bya ba bdag bshad bya//bdag nyid zhi ba'i phyir dang ni//de bzhin las kun grub pa'i phyir//bdag gi lus dbul nges par 'dud//
28a7(rgyud): rta dang ba klang blang po dang //gser dang nor dang ma ma dang //rang gi chung ma de bzhin du//ma dang sring mo tsha mo dang //gos dang rnam pa sna tshogs dang //gdugs dang mdzes pa'i rnga yab dang //khyim dang khri dang dri bzangs dang //
28a8(rgyud): klu dang de bzhin rol mo dang //ral gri brgyan rnams de bzhin du//rtul shugs can gyis bla ma dbul//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las//thams cad 
28b1(rgyud): sgrub pa'i rim par phye ba ste////bco lnga pa'o////de nas sems can thams cad kyi bdud dang nyo 'dod chags lasogs pa'i nyon mongs pa gcod cing/gzhom pa'i don gyi phyir sku mdog dmar po'o//spyan gsuM 
28b2(rgyud): ni 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar gyi dus gsum mo//phyag gnyis ni dngos po dang dngos po med pa'i rnam par rtog pa rnams 'joms pa'o//shes rab ma dang 'khyud pa ni stong pa nyid snying rje tha mi dad par sbyor ba'o/
28b3(rgyud): klu brgyad ni rnam par shes ba brgyad du gsung so//mgo bo'i brgya phyed phreng ba ni/yi ge lnga bcu sbyor ba'o//g.yon na thod pa ni ye shes lnga'i bdud rtsi'i bcud kyis yongasu gang zhing/stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin no//
28b4(rgyud): g.yas na dbyug pa ni phung po dang nyon mongs pa dang lha'i bu dang/'chi ba'i bdud rnams 'joms shing snying rje'i rang bzhin no//zhabs g.yon brkyang ba ni khams dang gsum du bshad do//lha mo'i phung po phyag g.yon na 
28b5(rgyud): thod pa bdud rtsi bzhi'i khrag dpag tu med pa ni dngul chu dang/bcud len dang ro dang bcud do//g.yas na dngos po dang dngos po/med pa rnam par gcortog pa gcod pa'i gri gugo//khro bo'i 
28b6(rgyud): gzugs phyag rgyas pa ni bdug pa can rnams 'dul ba'o//skra dmar ser gyen du rdzos pa ni sprul pa dpag tu med pa'i phyir ro//gru bzhi ni sdug bsngal ba dang/kun 'byung ba dang 'gog pa dang/
28b7(rgyud): lam gyi bden pa bshad do//sgo bzhi ni bdud bzhi skrag par byed pa'o//zur bzhi ni tshangs pa'i gnas bzhi rab tu rdzogs pa'i phyir ro//ka ba brgyad ni sgyu ma'i dpe byad kyi phyir te/sprul ba'i grong khyer dang/
28b8(rgyud): sgra rnyan dang sgyu ma dang srin dang/ri dgas sred dang me long dang/gzugs rnyan dang ni chu zla'o//namakha' ni dngos po dang dngos po med pa'i nyon mongs pa 'joms pa'i phyir ro//yang dag par slong ba ni dra ba dang dra phyed do//
30a1(rgyud): //rta babs bzhi ni sbyin pa dang/snyan par smra ba dang /don spyod pa dang/don 'thun pa bzhi'i rnam par spyod pa dag pa'o//de nas rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i sgrub thabs bdag gis rab bshad bya//nam mkha'i
30a2(rgyud): khams kyi dbus song nas//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor mchog bsams te/ce lnga pa ni rnam par sgom/de'i steng du pad ma dgod//Ti dog nag las 'jigs chen mo//phyag drug 'dzes pa'i gzugs ldan ma//
30a3(rgyud): phyag na rdo rje snam par sgom//lhag ma'i phyag du gzhan dgod do//shar du sangs rgyas mkha'agro ma//phyag drug gti mug dang 'tshungs ma//lag na 'khor lo 'jigs chen mo//rnal 'byor dkyil 'khor
30a4(rgyud): la sgom bya//lho ru rin chen mkha'agro ma//phra ma dang 'tshungs phyag drug ma//lag na rin chen 'jigs chen mo//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor la sgom bya//nub tu phyogs kyi mkha'agro ma//phyag drug pad ma
30a5(rgyud): 'dzin pa mo//byang na las kyi mkha'agro ma//snying po'i dkyil 'khor ral gri gri//me la sogs pa'i 'tshams bzhi la/rtul shugs can gyis lha mo sgom//dri ma de bzhin gar ma dang//
30a6(rgyud): klu ma sgyeg mo khyad par can//de na sgro skyong sgom pa ru//cho ga bzhin du las rnams lta//tho ba dbyug pa pad ma ral gri//oM bud d+ha Ta ki ni/oM badzra Da ki ni/oM d+harma Da ki ni/oM rad na Da 
30a7(rgyud): ki ni/ma~M la~M na~M gA~M 'tshamasu'o//oM mud ka ra dza/oM daN Da hU~M/oM pad ma ba~M/oM khad ga ho//ces pa ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje can gyis rdo rje mkha'agro ma'i sgrub thabs gsungs pa'o//ring po nas
30a8(rgyud): thob pa sgrub pa'i phyir//rdo rje mkha'agro ma sgom par bya'o//de'i sngags kyi tshig rnams 'di yin te//oM a ka sha tsa tsa ra badzra Da ki ni swAhA//de nas gzhan yang rdo rjes 'og gi//sgrub thabs bdag gis rab
30b1(rgyud): /bshad bya//sa 'og bu ga sgrub pa'i phyir//gnod mdzes rdo rje sgom par bya//phyag drug kha dog sngon po la//phyag na rtun shing 'dzin par byed//snying po'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//'di ni rab tu gcod par byed//
30b2(rgyud): shar na spyan ma dgod bya ste//lho ru de bzhin ma ma ki//nub tu de bzhin gos dkar mo//byang du sgrol ma dgod par bya//me tog spos dang mar me dang//dri rnams me tog la sogs rnams la dgod//sgo la tho ba la sogaste/
30b3(rgyud): sbrul gyi brgyan gyis skye rags bya//de bzhin 'khor lo sgom bya zhing//cho ga lta bas las rnams so//rdo rje sa 'og sbyor bldan pas//rmi lam du ni bu ga shes//de nas rgyal po bdun gyi ni//dkyil 'khor bdag gis 
30b4(rgyud): rab bshad bya//mkha' spyod rab tu sgrub bya'i phyir//rta mchog rab tu sgrub par bya//zhal cig pa dang phyag gnyis pa//sku mdog sngon po 'jigs par byed//nyi ma'i steng du gnas pa ste//shin du 
30b5(rgyud): 'jigs byed rta'i gdong//g.yas na gri'u khu tshur ste//phyag g.yon na ni thod pa yi//oM phu phu hi hi/shes rab can gyis sngags 'di zla//rta mchog rab tu sgrub ba'i phyir//shar la ba 
30b6(rgyud): Ta pa'i sgom bya//lho phyogasu ni myur 'gro ma//nub tu bdun pa'i rgyal mo ste//rta mchog ma ni byang du dgod//bdag po'i zhal dang phyag bzhin du//thams cad de bzhin 'tshungs par sgom//sngags 
30b7(rgyud): 'dis thams cad 'tshungs pa dang//spro ba ru ni rab tu grags//gling bu pi bang rnga zlum dang//rnga bran rnams kyang zur la dgod//ba blang rna dang blang chen sna//de bzhin bzhin bzangs bzhin ngan pa//rtun shing dang ni sta re dag//
30b8(rgyud): lcags kyu dang ni zhags pa'o//gang la sogs pa'i rnal 'byor gyis//sgo bzhi pa la rtag tu sgom//nas ljang nyi ma dang bcas bsam//rang gi rtags rnams de bzhin g.yas//ji ltar rta mchog gi ni gnas//
31a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rjes 'og gi yang ngo//rta mchog sgom pa'i sbyor ba las//khams gsum mkha' la bsgyur bar byed//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i 
31a2(rgyud): rgyud las//dngos grub nges pa'i rim par phye ba ste//bcu drug pa'o//de la 'dir thig bdab pa'i dam tshig mchog ni//a ka ro mu kha~M sarba d+harma NA ma ar+ya nud pa na twa ta//dpal ldan gshin rje mthar
31a3(rgyud): byed gzugs//slob ma rnam par snang 'dzad byas//ye shes theg mchog rtse dag bzung//mnyam gzhag nas ni legs par brdab//de la 'dir ni dkyil 'khor chen po rab tu 'jug pa'i dam tshig go//oM a hU~M//
31a4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor gnyis 'gyur ring ba la//sgo yi nyi shu cha yis sgom//ba'i rnam lngas legs par byug//skud pa gnyisu med par rtags//de la 'dir ni rdo rje chen po rab tu 'jug pa'idam tshig ste/kye ma 
31a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas slob dpon che//kye ma chos dang tshogs kyi gtso//dam/tshig de nyid bdag la rtsol//byang chub sems kyang bdag la rtsol//de la 'di ni sa chen po//yongasu bzung ba'i dam tshigo/
31a6(rgyud): rdo rje lha mo spyan drangs la//skyob pa sangs rgyas thams cad ni//spyod pa'i tshul dang khyad par dang//sa dang pha rol phyin pa la//lha mo khyod ni dpang du gyur//sgo ni dkyil 'khor brgyad cha ru//bdag nyid chen
31a7(rgyud): pos shes bya ste//khams gsum po ni ma rtogs pa'i//tshad ldan shin du 'dzes pa'o//sgo dang sgo khyud nges par bya//de'i phyed kyis snam bu ste//klu ma rnga ma gar ma swogs//rdo rje lha mo yang dag ldan//de'i phyed kyis
31a8(rgyud): rdul mtshon sa//sgo'i phyed kyi 'gram dang logs//'dir yang dra ba dra phyed dang//nyi ma zla ba me tog phreng//lha'i snam bu me tog tshad//de bzhin phyi'i dkyil 'khor ro//de la snying po chen po 'di//bdag gis 'khor lo nam mkha' rgod//
31b1(rgyud): rang gi phyag rgya'ang de ru rten//dam tshig ye shes bzhugs byas nas//dkyil 'khor rab tu bsgrub par bya//shar du dkar po chen po ste//lho ru ser po dang ldan pa//dmar po nub kyi cha la ste//byang du ma ra ka ta ldan//in+t+ra
31b2(rgyud): ni la'i 'od 'tshungs pa//dbus gyi sa gzhir yang dag bri//las kyi go cha mthong ba yis//de'i phyag rgya dgod par bya//dbus su rdo rje yang dag bri//shar la 'khor lo legs par bri//de lta bu lasogs par lta//phyag
31b3(rgyud): rgya rtag tu namakha' 'tshungs//de la lugs bzar sgang bzar tshad//'di'i tshad kyi yu ba bya//'khru gang cha dang ldan pa la//de'i rtse mor dkyil 'khor bya//sor mo bzhi'i tshad ldan la//gting tu sor gnyis zab pa la//
31b4(rgyud): de la sor phyed bcud khung du//blugs bzar pad ma'i rnam pa bzhin//'the bo'i lho gang tshad la ni//gting du sen mo'i tshad ldan no//rgyud 'di la ni rab tu sngags//dkyil 'khor du ni ma zhugs shing //'tshaMs
31b5(rgyud): med lnga ni byas pa dang //gang gis srog chags bsod pa dang //nya dang sha sogs bza' ba dang //chang dang 'o ma la chags dang //chang lta'i rtul shugs 'dzin pa dang //mngon par dbang ni ma skur dang //
31b6(rgyud): bya ba ma yin byed sro dang //grong khyer dben byed pas kyang ni//dmar po'i gsung ni ji lta bar//gshin rje gshed rgyud mchog la gzhol//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//de nas byams pa lasogs pa'i rdo rje'i
31b7(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' kun gyis//rdo rje chen po sngon med pa thob pas cang mi smra bar gyur to//de la 'di ni rdo rje dbab pa'i dam tshig ste//de yang 'byung ba'i sa bon che//'khor lo bzhi po rab sgrub bya//gnas bzhi rab
31b8(rgyud): tu sbyor ba yis//namakha' yang ni g.yo bar byed//rkang pa dag gi 'og tu yaM//lte bar ram ste snying khar lam//g+ho bo la ni ba~M yig yi//bzhu'i rnam pa nag po ste//gtum po'i rlung ni 'jigs byed pa//sreg byed
32a1(rgyud): @#//zur gsum me bzhin 'bar//nyi ma dmar po'i 'od dang 'tshungs//rdo rje sa gzhi rim pa ni//ser mo chen mo gru bzhi pa//ri rab ri yis mnon pa yi//nor 'dzin de yis sgom par bya//dkar po zlum po bum pa bzhin//sprin bzhin 
32a2(rgyud): du ni char pa 'dzin//stong gis rab tu gang ba yi//gangs kyi ri bo lta bur bsil//gnas gzhi rab tu sbyor ba yis//slob ma rdo rje can la sgom//rdo rje snang ba mtha' yas sbyor//myur ba nyid du 'bebs par byed//'di ni 'gri ba'i dam tshig go//
32a3(rgyud): rdo rje chos ni mgon chen po//snang ba mtha' yas bde ba che//gsungs shing 'dod chags chos chen po//dge dang mi dge legs par 'bri//khru gang nas ni bzung nas ni//ji srid khru ni stong gi bar//slob ma chen po 'bar bar 'gyur//
32a4(rgyud): 'di ni dbab ba'i dam tshig go//ri mo bri ba'i dam tshig rnams//bdag gis yang dag bshad par bya//sa ni bsnun pa blangs byas la//rtul shugs can gyis mnan par bya//sran bre de bzhin 'o ma sha//bu ram 
32a5(rgyud): gser yab la sogs pa//til dang nim pa'i lo ma dang //de bzhin na Ti' sa bon no//dang po bri bar bya//de la kha dog gzhan gyis ni//thod lo dkar dang cod dang la//chu yis sbyar ba rgya skyegs bzhin//
32a6(rgyud): yung ba dang ni ram sa dag la//de bzhin kha dog ljang khur 'gyur//sngon po rtag tu rams kyis so//de bzhin me dreg nag pa ste//skya ba de bzhin ba ba bla yis//de bzhin dmar po mtshal gyis so//ka bde khu ba de la byin//
32a7(rgyud): chung dus ma yin lhag de bzhin//lhag pas nag po dman pa yis//skya bo ru ni 'gro bar byed//khro dang zhi ba rnam gnyis kyis//gzugs kyi mtshan nyid bshad par bya//zhi ba sgyeg pos gsungs pa ste//khro bo sbom la thung ba ste//
32a8(rgyud): sor mo bzhi'i tshad kyis ni//dpral ba de bzhin kha la'o//'grin pa de bzhin 'gram pa la//gsang gnas la ni sor bzhi tshad//sor mo nyi shu rtsa bzhi'i lag//sor mo nyi shu bar lhu'o//lag pa rkang pa de bzhin gdong //mig la 
32b1(rgyud): 'the bo'i tshad yin te//rna ba la ni sor mo gsungs//sgong ba'i sbubs la de bzhin zhi//mchu gnyis dag la sor gnyis so//lte ba'i zabs su sor mo ste//'grin pa ma rtogs lus la ni//gdong gi sum 'gyur dag tu 'dod//ral pa 
32b2(rgyud): ni bdog gi tshad//rin chen dpung brgyan sor mo drug//de nas 'phags ma ser mo drug//sgrub thabs bdag gis bshad par bya//gang gi bsgom pa tsam gyi ni//chu'i steng du chags par byed//ser mo zhal gsum phyag drug pa//phu yig 
32b3(rgyud): sa bon las byung ba//phyag na sbrul dang gzugs chen po//rma bya la ni zhon par gyis//shar la rma bya ma dgod do//lho ru de bzhin khro ba nyer can//nub tu ri khrod lo ma can//byang du rdo rje lcags sgrog ma//rma bya'i sgro dang spyi blugs 
32b4(rgyud): dang//yal ga de bzhin lcags sgrog ste/de rnams rnam par sgom par bya//ser po dmar po ljang khu dang//kha dog sngon po dbye ba nyid//shes rab can gyis de bzhin sgom//de bzhin du ni sngags 'di zlas//oM bu dza ra/
32b5(rgyud): tho ba la sogs sgo na dgod//me tog la sogs 'tshams su'o//ar+ya~M/gu la~M sbyor ldan pas//chu'i steng du rtag tu 'gro//de nas ku ru ku la'i//sgrub thabs bdag gis rab bshad bya//gang gis sgom 
32b6(rgyud): pa byas pas ni//klu'i bu mo 'gugs par byed//kha dog dmar po zhal bzhi dang//phyag rgyad dang ni yang dag ldan//hri yig sa bon las byung ba//klu rnams 'gugs par byed pa'o//mtha' yas la sogs klu rigs brgyad/
32b7(rgyud): phyag brgyad du ni dgod par bya//sbrul gyi rgyan gyis sku la brgyan//rtul shugs can gyis 'dzes ma sgom//sngags ni ku ru ku le'i hri swAhA//de na rgyas min mdor bsdus pas//gsang ba la ni bshad par bya//
32b8(rgyud): gang gis shes pa tsam gyis ni//lha'i bu mo 'gugs par byed //zhal cig ma dang phyag gnyis ma//phyag na bzhu dang mda' snams pa//lus ni ser po chen po yi//lus med rnam par sgom par bya//shar du dga' ma sgom par bya//
33a1(rgyud): @#//mos byed 'dzes ma lho ru'o//nub tu 'dod pa'i lha mo ste//byang du myos mar sbos ma'o//'dod pa'i lha mo thams cad kyi//lag na bzhu dang mdar sgom mo//ser mo dmar dang de bzhin ljang //dkar dmar gnyis rnams sgom 
33a2(rgyud): par bya//'tshams su rtag tu dgod ma dang //ma 'gags pa dang dbang byed ma//chu srin rgyal mtshan ces pa rnams//sgo'i cha la rab tu bshad//'dod 'gying dang ni snyems can dang //dran pa de bzhin 'tshams kyi bdag//
33a3(rgyud): lha rnams de dag thams cad kyi//spyi bor gshin rje gshed po sgom//bud med rnams kyi nam mkha'i gdong //mtha' la rdo rje lus med sgom//shi ti sngags las yang dag sgom//kun du rnam par 'phro ba ste//slus shing 
33a4(rgyud): g.yeng ba sgom par bya//myos zhing bde ba 'dod yid kyis//gos ni dmar pos yongs bkris te//rkang pa dag gi drung du lhung //de la sngags ni shin tu zlas//oM yig dbyangs kyis dbye ba dang //swAhA yi ge mtha' mar 
33a5(rgyud): sbyin//shi ta yi ge'i sngags rnams brjod//tshe ge mo ni bdag dbang gis//dmar po'i gsung ni ji bzhin du//thams cad du ni sgom byas nas//rnal 'byor pa yi 'dod pa thob//de bzhin gshegs 
33a6(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/lus med pa la sogs pa'i rim par phye ba ste//bcu bdun pa'o////de nas he ru ka chen gyis//
33a7(rgyud): sgrub thabs bdag gis rab bshad bya//rnal 'byor 'khor bas bcings pa las//gang zhig shes pas myur grol ba//zhal cig pa la phyag gnyis pa//sku mdog sngon po gtum po che//phyag na rdo rje sgom par bya//gzhan la 
33a8(rgyud): thod pa dgod par bya//spyan gsum skra ni gyen du rdzes//phyag rgya lnga yis rnam par brgyan//kha tra~M kha ni g.yon gyi char//rkang rtsub chen pos rnam par brgyan//hU~M yig sa bon las byung ba//yid ta gas steng zhugs rtag tu 'dzum//
33b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas lnga ni rab sbyor bas//thod pa lnga yis dbu la brgyan//mi skyod pa yis spyod pa na bsam//he ru kar grags rnam par sgom//shar du chos kyi 'khor lo bris//sangs rgyas byang chub lho ru'o//'dod lnga'i khro shing nub 
33b2(rgyud): tu ste//byang du he ru ka dang 'tshungs//sna tshogs gzugs can sgyeg pa la//rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa la//gzugs 'dzes yid rtags steng du'o//gti mug thams cad nyer 'joms ma//chos kyi 'khor lo de bzhin 
33b3(rgyud): gzhag/yid bzhin nor dang dpag bsam shing //bum pa bzang po de sgom bya//lha mo rnams kyi phyag g.yas na//rdo rje yang ni sgom par bya//dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor thams cad la//thod pa'i snod ni g.yon du'o//
33b4(rgyud): spyan dang ma ma ki sgrol ma//gos dkar shin du yid 'ongs rnams//me la sogs pa'i 'tshams bzhi la//phyi'i 'khor lo gshin rje gshed//shes rab mthar byed rta mgrin dang //bdud rtsi 'khyil 
33b5(rgyud): bcas sgom par bya//'khor lo yi ni sgo srungs te//las rnams cho ga mthong ba yis//rnam pa lnga ni sgom byas nas//sangs rgyas byang chub rnam par sgom//'khor lo bsgom bya rnal 'byor 
33b6(rgyud): pas//klu bzhi rab tu sbyor ba yis//'khor lo can ni skul bar byed//tsar tsi ka sogs snyan pa'i klu//drug 'gyur la sogs klu rnams kyis//brtse bas 'gro ba'i rje btsun bzhengs//sa gsum pa'i gti mug 
33b7(rgyud): gsol//bsam du med pa'i rje btsun bzhengs//bdud bzhi rnams las rgyal bar 'jod//'jig rten mgon po stong par zhugs//'jig rten bsod nams rje btsun bzhengs//byang chub dngos la 'jig rten mgon//
33b8(rgyud): khyod ni stong nyid sems par 'dzad//ji ltar skar ma 'phro ba ltar//byang chub sems la ni de la zhugs//rtse bas chags pa'i sems zhu ste//rdo rje 'dzin pa rang nyid do//dang por rnal 'byor sgom par bya//rjes su rnal 'byor chen po'o//
34a1(rgyud): //shin tu rnal 'byor gsum pa'o//rnal 'byor chen po bzhi pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' rdzogs pa la//rnal 'byor zhes ni mngon par brjod//lha'i sku ni rdzogs pa la//rjes su rnal 'byor zhes grags so//
34a2(rgyud): 'khor lo thams cad rdzogs pa la//shin tu rnal 'byor yin par 'dod//lha'i mig sogs byin rlabs dang //sku dang gsung dang thugs rnams dang //ye shes 'khor lo rab zhugs dang //de bzhin bdud rtsi myang ba dang //
34a3(rgyud): mchod dang bstod pa chen po ni//shin tu rnal 'byor chen po ni//rnal 'byor chen po zhes 'dod do//slob dpon la ni rnyes mi bya//bde gshegs bka' las mi 'da' ste//de bzhin spun la 'khro ba dang //nyes pa sgrag par 
34a4(rgyud): mi bya'o//sems can kun la byams pa'i phyir//nam yang btang bar mi bya'o//byang chub sems kyang btang mi bya//bdag gzhan chos la sun mi dbyangs//yongs su ma smin sems can la//
34a5(rgyud): gsang ba sgrag par mi bya'o//bdag gi phung po smad mi bya//bcom ldan chos ni sun mi dbyung //rtug pa'i byams pa rtag tu spongs//chos la tshad bzhal mi bya'o//sems can dad pa can mi slu//
34a6(rgyud): rtag tu dam tshig sten par bya//bud med shes rab rang bzhin la//smos pa'i nges pa de bzhin no//rnal 'byor slongs mos rgyu mi bya//rnal 'byor spang bar mi bya ste//rtag par kun tu gsang sngags zlas//
34a7(rgyud): rtag tu dam tshig la sogs rten//gal te bag med gyur pa yis//bla ma'i dam tshig la nyams na//des ni dkyil 'khor zlum por bri//bder gshegs rnams la nyes pa gshegs//dad pas bla ma'i 
34a8(rgyud): dam tshig srung //snying rje can gyis mi rtog par//nyes pa ma lus spang sems kyis//bsam brtan la bzhol zlas mchog gzhol//nan cing dul la brtse ba'i sems//dad dang lhag pa'i bsam pa dang //
34b1(rgyud): mchog tu bzod pa 'dzin pa yi//skyes bu rtag tu bla mar bya//slob ma bla ma la gus dang//gshin rje gshed la rab gus pa//rtul shugs can gyis yongs smin bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bde ba chen 
34b2(rgyud): po mchog tu dga' ba'i ngo bo nyid rdo rje sems dpa'i gsung nye bar thos nas//cang mi smra bar gyur to//'di lta bu'i ched du brjod do//'khrul 'khor mthar thug gshin rje gshed//rtul shugs mthar thug thod pa che//rgyud kyi mthar 
34b3(rgyud): thug 'dus pa che//ma byung 'byung bar mi 'gyur//e ma zhi ba chen po'i chos//e ma khro bo 'jigs 'dzad pa//e ma mya ngan 'das pa'i mchog//e ma 'khor ba zhi ba nyid//e ma 'di ni rab tu 'char//nyes pa gang de yon 
34b4(rgyud): tan 'gyur//byang chub med cing mngon rtogs med//dngos po med cing dngon rtogs med//dngos bo med cing sgom pa 'ang med//khams kyang med cing rnam shes med//nam mkha'i dkyil du 'tshungs pa nyid//nam 
34b5(rgyud): mkha' bzhin du bdag med ba//chos 'di bde ba chen po'o//sa yang med cing chu yang med//me med rlung med nam mkha' med//rdo rje sems dpa' rab sbyor bas//bskyed pa 'dir ni rab tu gnas//
34b6(rgyud): dngos po thams cad dngos med bshad//des na rang bzhin med ces sgrags//rnal 'byor sbyor ba dang ldan pas//chad pa ma yin rtag pa min//yan lag med cing kha dog med//dngos po thams cad bden pa med//
34b7(rgyud): thog ma bar ma mtha' ma spangs//mchog tu dga' ba'i mtshan nyid do//de nas tsar tsi ka la sogs pa'i ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//bsod nams sdig pa dag dang ma 'brel bas//mchog tu dga' ba dri med rnam 
34b8(rgyud): dag pa//de bzhin nyid kyi gter chen gcig pu ni//kun nas snying rje'i sems kyis rab tu gnas//mchog tu dga' ba 'di'i ngo bo nyid//chos rnams ngo bo bde ba chen po'i dngos//gzugs med bsod nams med cing 
36a1(rgyud): cing sdig pa med//skye dang 'gag pa dag ni yod ma yin//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/byang chub sems kyi rim par phye ba ste//
36a2(rgyud): bco brgyad pa'o////de nas rta ma rgyud rab tu bshad par bya ste/bcom ldan 'das mngon par byang chub pa'i dus su/bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po la byang chub pa rab tu gcad pa'i phyir/shin tu 'jigs su rung ba'i 
36a3(rgyud): 'jigs pa chen po ston cing bdud kyi dpung gi tshogs chen po dpag tu med pa lhags te/de'i dus na bdud chen po las mchog tu rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/bcom ldan 'das nyid kyi sku dang /
36a4(rgyud): gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje rnams las gshin rje gshed kyi khro bo nges par 'byung ste/nges par phyung nas kyang /phyag na rdo rje bcom ldan 'das thub pa che po'i ka bzhin byed la//phyag na rdo rje khyod 'di lta bu'i gshin rje gshed kyi 
36a5(rgyud): khro bo'i gzugs su sgyur te//bdud dang gnod sbyin dang /lha ma yin dang /lha dang srin po rnams skrag cing 'jigs pa dang /sod cig ces gsungs so//de'i dus na ji ltar bdag gis bcom ldan 'das 
36a6(rgyud): kyis gsungs pa thos te/skad cig la bzung zhing don bzung ste/sdus nas shin tu dga' bas legs par sems bskyed do//gsang ba'i bdag po chen po la/'di skad ces bka' stsal to//pha rol grong du rab 
36a7(rgyud): 'jug pa'i//thabs rnams rab tu gsal bya ste//'gro dang 'ong bar 'dod pa yis//ting nge 'dzin dang gsang sngags zlas//bye ba zlas pas grub 'gyur te//bye ba lnga yis smos ci dgos//dang po de nas ni/bdag nyid nam mkha' lta bu ste/
36a8(rgyud): dang por goms par bya ba'i phyir//sems la sems nyid bzhugs par bya//dper na sems 'di'i rmi lam na//'gro dang slar yang ldog par bya//skad cig la ni de bzhin gshegs//rgyun mi 'chad par sbyor goms pas//dper na 
36b1(rgyud): mtshon cha gnas byas na//gzhu yis mda' ni 'phen ba ste//de bzhin 'dir yang nyid kyis zhus//rgyun mi 'chad par 'gro bar byed//zla ba nyi ma sbyor ba yis//rlung gis sems ni bzhug par bya//lus ni rnam par spangs 
36b2(rgyud): nas ni//de la yid ni 'gro dang 'jug//cung zad drod ni rnyed pa yis//thod pa'i phyogasu sgom pa'o//lus kyi nang du 'jug pa ni//dper na grong gzhan 'gro ba yis//zla ba nyi ma'i drod 'joms shing //sgra gcan 'jug pa sbyor 
36b3(rgyud): ba yis//drod pa'i gnas ni thob pa na//rlung gi zhon pa sbyor ba na//mchi ltas mtshan nyid shes bya ba//cho ga mtshan nyid ldan par bgrod//ji ltar 'dod pa g.yo mi g.yo//rang 'dod kun du skye bar 'gyur//thur sel 
36b4(rgyud): 'og lam bsang ba'i mtha'//gtong zhing lte bar rtsom par byed//snying kha 'grin par song nas ni//srog ni sna bug dag nas 'gro//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs 
36b5(rgyud): gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud las/pha rol gyi grong du 'jug pa ste/rim par phye ba bcu dgu pa'o//dpal gshin rje gshed dmar po'irgyud kyi rgyal po//rdzogs so//
36b6(rgyud): bla ma chos kyi rgyal po'i gsung gi 'od zer dang /b+ho Ta pa Ni ta'i gsung dang /dbon gzhon gyis gser gnang ba la rten nas/shakya'i dge slong tsan tra shri'i zhal snga nas zhus shing /paN Ti ta chen po tsan Ti ki ri 
36b7(rgyud): ta dang /yar lung pa grags pa rgyal mtshan ba la yul yam bu'i grong khyer du bsgyur ba'o//@#//rgya gar skad du shri ma tra k+ta ya mA ri tan tra rA dza na ma/bod skad du/'jam dpal 
36b8(rgyud): gshin rje gshed dmar bo'i rgyud kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba/gshin rje mthar byed dmar po la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 
37a1(rgyud): //gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa bdag med ma'i b+ha ga la bzhugaso//de la mgrin par dam mkhyud de/rang nyid sems can byed zhus pa//kyai rdo rjer gsung ba dang/thar pa de bzhin sdom par yang//'dus
37a2(rgyud): pa la sogs rgyud du yang//slar cher mang du gsungs pa yang//gang yang mngon par spyod pa dang/dgra'i sde nyams byed pa dang//skrod dang 'gugs dang bsod pa dang//sna tshogs rgyud du ma brjod pa'i//'khrul 'khor 'jig
37a3(rgyud): rten gsum rnyed dka'//khyod la phan pa'i thabs bzung phyir//bshad bya lha ma gus bcas nyon//'khrul 'khor gang gi mthu stobs kyis//rtag tu rgyal srid thob bya ni//ji srid nyi ma zla ba bzhin//'tsho bar
37a4(rgyud): 'dod de gal te min//da ni ming gi spyod yul du//khu ba rengs dang steng 'gugs dang//mkha' spyod nyid dang mchog tu myur//de yis sman ni bshad par bya//dang por byams pa sgom par bya//gnyis
37a5(rgyud): par snying rje de bzhin no//gsum par dga' ba sgom par bya//bzhi par btang snyoms rnam sgom bya//'jig rten chos rnams brgyad po ni//yongs btang stong par bsam par bya//slar yang
37a6(rgyud): stong nyid byang chub ste//gnyis pa la ni sa bon sdu//bzhi par yi ge dgod par bya//ra yig las byung nyi ma la//hU~M byung sna tshogs rdo rje'o//de nyid kyis ni rdo rje'i sa//rab zhes dang de bzhin du//sa gzhi
37a7(rgyud): rdo rje bcings pa'o//dang por stong par sgom pa ni//de nas yi ge oM dmar po//yongs su gyur pa las nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ro//de'i steng du yi ge hU~M dmar po'o//de 'i steng du bla na dang bcas pa'i mgo bos brgyan pa'i dbyug
37a8(rgyud): pa'i dbus su hU~M yig dmar po'o//der yang nyi ma la hU~M yig go//de las nges par 'byung ba'i 'od zer rnams kyi nam mkha'i phyogaso//dpa' bo'i gzugs de la 'jig rten gsum po thams cad sdus nas slar yang de nyid du/
37b1(rgyud): bcug ste dpal gshin rje gshed dmar po'i gzugasu bdag nyid bsam par bya'o//sku mdog dmar po g.yon rkyang pa rnam par 'gyur ba'i zhal mche ba brtsigs pa//ljags sgril ba 'jigs par byed pa/klu rgya dgyis 
37b2(rgyud): rnam par brgyan pa/dbu skra dmar ser gyen du 'bar ba/skyed pa'i phyogs su stag gi pags pa 'dzag pas rnam par brgyan pa/mi'u thung gsus pa 'chang zhing 'jigs pa/spyan gsum pa shin du 'jigs par byed pa/

37b6(rgyud): dmar po la g.yon rkyang gis zhabs kyis mnan bas bzhugs pa'o//byi nas bcom ldan 'das la skyes pa'i rtse gsum pa'i dbyug kha dkar po'i dbus su slar yang /sa bon kha dog dmar po bsam par bya'o//
37b7(rgyud): phyi nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje yang hU~M yig las skyes pa'o//slar yang bcom ldan 'das ma'i gsang ba'i pad ma yang pad ma 'dab ma brgyad pa dngos su A/yig gis rdzogs par mtshan pa'o//de'i rjesu gsang

38a1(rgyud): //spyi bor son pa'i re kha dkar po slar yang bsam par bya zhing bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu la bzhugs par bya'o//slar yang rdo rje'i lam la der son par blo bzang pos bsam par bya'o//phan tshun gnyis gnyis 
38a2(rgyud): snyoms par zhugs par ro mnyam pa'i sa bon gzugs can no//sa bon de lha mo bzhis rang gi gzugasu blang ba ni shar la sogs pa'i phyogs bzhi rnamasu blang ba/de rnams rang gi bdag po la lha zhing slong bar byed pas lha mo 
38a3(rgyud): thams cad gnang ba'i phyed du klu'i mchod pa byed par lha'o//thugs rje'i khro bo rje btsun nyid bzhengs cig//sa gsum rmongs pa mtha' dag so la 'jod cig//rdo rje tsar tsi ka yis so//jo bo bdud bzhi po ni las rgyal ba yi//
38a4(rgyud): sems ni g.yengs so rje btsun nyid bzhengs cig //phag mo yis so/ting 'dzin 'gro na gnyer stong nyid bzhugs sam ci//rje btsun ting 'dzin bsod nams kyis//bzhengs cig dbyangs can ma yis so//byang chub 
38a5(rgyud): rang bzhin 'jig rten mgron gnyer nas//khyod nyid stong nyid sems shing ci la zhugs//gA+au ri'o//rab tu sad par byas nas slar yang de la rab tu zhugs so//de'i 'og tu de la khu ba'i tshogs 
38a6(rgyud): gyur pa las/bdag nyid lha'i bdag nyid can ma he dmar po'i steng na/de yang gar gyi nyams dgu dang ldan pa b+han d+hu ka'i me tog lta bu drag po la zhal cig pa phyag gnyis pa/'tshe ba rnam par rtsigs pa/dmar la zlum pa'i

38b6(rgyud): de nas 'pho ba'i gnas skabs ni/bdag nyid du sgom pa'i lha mo'i khongasu dus mnyam par 'dzag pa'i khams gnyis 'dres par sgom par bya'o//dkar po dang dmar po de dag yongasu gyur pas/gti mug gshin rje 
38b7(rgyud): gshed la sogs pa rnams/bdag nyid ji lta ba bzhin du pad ma'i dbus su bsams nas/bcom ldan 'das ma la skye bar sgom par bya'o//de'i 'og tu de rnams mdun du ltas nas rang rang gi gnasu so sor gnas par

39a1(rgyud): @#//phag mo'i gdong pa can no//nub kyi 'dab ma'i nyi ma la 'dod chags gshin rje gshed dmar po dbyangs can ma dkar mo dang sbyor ba'o//byang gi 'dab ma'i nyi ma la phra dog gshin rje gshed sngo sangs/gau ri dang

39b8(rgyud): snying khar gnas pa'i sa bon dang mtshon ma rtan par byas nas gsang sngags zlas parabya'o//oM hri Shi wi Ti te na nu hU~M hU~M phaT swAhA//zhes pa ji srid 'dod par bsams nas zlas par bya'o//zhes pa'o/lhan cig skyes pa'i
40a1(rgyud): /gshin rje gshed kyi rnal 'byor gyi gnas skabs ni/bdag nyid kyi gzugs lta bur rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i gshin rje gshed du nga rgyal byed pa 'joms par byed do//rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i zhal cig pa

40a7(rgyud): pa yi 'byung gnas rnal 'byor dag/nyid kyis gzugs gang yin pa bcom ldan 'das mchog tu rnam par ma chad par 'bab ste/dper na chu klung gi rgyun rab tu 'bab pa bzhin no//'dod ji srid du sgom pa las skyon sngags 
40a8(rgyud): zlas par bya'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis gzugs gal te myur du grub par 'gyur gyi/gzhan gyi gzhan du ma yin no//ji srid du de'i dngos grub kyang phyag rgya chen po'i dngos grub kho na'o//'di rnams kyang rnal 
40b1(rgyud): 'byor goms pa kho nas thams cad sgrub par byed kyis//gzhan du ma yin no//goms pa'i sbyor bas 'gyur ba ni skyes pa dang/byung ba dang ma byung ba dang sngon du gnas pa rnams thar pa'i don dang 'dod pa'i don rnam pa sna tshog+sa

50b5(rgyud): ba ling dang/ba g+h+ra shri ka byugs na steng du 'gyur ro//shing ka bed kyi rtsa ba dang/da bi dang/ra'i gcin gyis btags nas/ling ka la kun du byugs te/pad ma dbye ba ste lan gsum du steng du

50b7(rgyud): las ling ka rengs par byed do//d+ha du ra'i rtsa ba dang/ka mA rtsa'i rtsa ba dang/o Sha nI yi rtsa ba dang/ga pur gi khu bas btugs nas ling ka la byugs na 'dzag par byed do//rgyam tsha dang/'tshal dang/gwa bur dang/

55b6(rgyud): byed pa med par lhur len pa//snying khar gnas pa'i sa bon bzhi//kun du gang bar sgom par bya//de nas gang yin de nyid kyis//ming ni thams cad shes par 'gyur//kun du dgongs pa tsam gyis ni//byung dang 'gyur

56a1(rgyud): //de bzhin bsam//reg bya thams cad shes par byed//de bzhin mgo bo'i dbus bsams na//nus pa thams cad rab 'phel byed//gang dang gang du rlung gnas pa//ro mnyam gyur par byas pa ni//'gag pa de dang
