56a3(rgyud): bzhin des sbyor bar bya//g.yon du'ang lta ba'i lta stengs ni//bum pa can gyi dbang du byed//g.yas nas dgug par bya ba ni//gang ba can gyis nges par sbyor//dpral bar gnas pa'i lta stengs gang//ra tsa ka yis bsod par byed//

56a7(rgyud): slar yang ngo//ci bgyi yod par 'gyur ba yin//rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma rgyud du//thams cad mkhyen pas gsungs pa yang//rtsod ldan dus su rab tu brjod//dngos grub med par mi lta ste//rdzogs ldan sum ldan nyis ldan dang//

56b1(rgyud): ces so////gshin rje gshed dmar po'i rgyud//rlung gi de nyid ces bya ba'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o////bcom ldan 'das gshin rje gshed kyi ming dmar po brjod pa'i ming can la//ya yig g+si

56b4(rgyud): pa'i ming can no//yang na rlung gi ldan pa la//de'i thabs ni a yig ste//a yig reg pa'i dbang po 'o//rak+ta gnyis med sbyor ba'o//yang na rak+ta 'dod chags rgyal//rlung gi ro sku la der son sems//

74a2(rgyud): za bas nyan byed na//mgo bor rdo rje thog lhung ba//char pha bas dus su the tsom med//mchog gi zhal mnga' lha mo la//bdag gis 'di ni bden par bshad//dpa'o gcig nyid rgyud 'di ni//rtum po khro bo shin tu 
74a3(rgyud): bsad//zhes pa ni dpa'o gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa'i dpa'o khro bo la rtum po chen po'i rgyud las rgyud pa/'jug pa'i rim par phye ba bya ba ste/dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das ma 
74a4(rgyud): zhe sdang rdo rjes mas rtum po chen po khro bo la dam du 'khyud nas//gsol pa/dkyil 'khor gyi ni bya ba la//gang gi tshad kyis bri bar bya//de bzhin dbus su bri bya ba//gang rtse gtso bos bdag la gsungs//
74a5(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dkyil 'khor tshad du gang gyur pa//khru gang dang ni khru do dang //khru gsum pa dang khru bzhi pa//khru lnga'i tshad las lhag mi bya//gang du de'i rdul mtshon
74a6(rgyud): nyid//kha dog sna tshogs kyis byas pa//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi yis rnam par brgyan//sgo ni brgyad cha nyid du ni//de'i tshad ni rab tu rtag//sgo'i tshad kyis sgo khyud dang //de'i phyed kyis 
74a7(rgyud): bra dang ni//logs dang de bzhin stegs bu rnams//rtsa ba'i thig le phyi rol du//de'i phyed kyis rdul mtshon dang //rdo rje phreng ba nyid dang ni//ka ba brgyad ldan rtag par bya//sgo'i sum 'gyur nyid kyis ni//sgo'i rta 
74a8(rgyud): babs mchog tu bya//mchog tu sna tshogs rdo rje bri//rdo rje ri rab khor yug ldan//dpag bsam shing sogs ldan pa dang //khro bo rtum po'i dkyil 'khor la//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i rnam pa la//'phar ma gcig ma nyid du bya//
74b1(rgyud): de'i shar swogs phyogs brgyad la//sna tshogs pad ma yang dag bri//dbus ma dang dgu de dbus su//ral gri sngon po rdo rje'i mtshan//rdo rje dri khug thod bcas 'og//shar du 'khor los mtshan pa'i//ral gri kha dog 
74b2(rgyud): dkar po bri//lho ru kha dog ser po'o//rin chen dang bcas yang dag bri//nub du kha dog dmar po bri//pad ma dmar pos mtshan pa dang //byang du ral gri rkyang pa yi//kha dog ljang khu yang dag bri//'khor lo 
74b3(rgyud): dkar pos mtshan pa yi//gri khug me'i 'tshamasu bri//rin chen ser pos rab mtshan pa//bden bral du ni bri bar bya//pad ma dmar pos rab mtshan pa/rlung du de bzhin bri bar bya//dbang ldan kha dog 
74b4(rgyud): kha ljang gu'o//ud pal sngon pos mtshan pa bri//zla ba nyi ma'i steng gnas pa'i//mtshan ma thams cad rab tu rtag//rdul mtshon dkyil 'khor sgrub thabs 'di//bdag gis 'jig rten don phyir bshad//
74b5(rgyud): yang na dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//ras ris tshul du bri bar bya//mtshan ma thams cad rag tu rtag//rdul mtshon da kyil 'khor dri bar bya//sngon bzhin dkyil 'khor dri ba las//dbus su mi g.yo mthong 
74b6(rgyud): nag bri//zhe sdang rdo rjems 'byung ba//shar du mi g.yo na ba dkar po bri//mi g.yo ser po de bzhin lho//nub tu mi g.yo dmar po bri//byang du mi g.yo ljang khu bri//me 'tshams gti mug rdo rje dkar//bden bral 
74b7(rgyud): phra ma rdo rje ser/rlung du 'dod chags rdo rje ma//lha mo dkar po yang dag bri//dbang ldan 'phra dog rdo rje ma//ljang khu ras ris dkyil 'khor la//de nas dkyil 'khor la sngags kyis lhag par gnas par bya ba ni 
74b8(rgyud): oM shra tsan Ta mahA ro Sha na sarba pa ri wa sa hi ta/a gats+tsha a g+hats+tsha/dza hU~M baM ho/a Ta ma Na Ta le a d+hi Shan ku ru hU~M phaT swahA//oM d+he sha badzre puSh+paM pra ti ts+tsha hU~M phaT swahA///oM mo ha badzra puSh+paM pra ti ts+tsha hAM phaT haM swahA//
75a1(rgyud): //oM sh+ya rak+sha tsu la pu Sh+paM pra tits+tsha hU~M phaT swAhA/oM sh+yaM ma tsa la pu Sh+paM pra tits+tsha phaT swAhA/oM d+he sha badzra puSh+paM pra tits+tsha hU~M phaDa swAhA/oM mo ha badzra puSh+paM pra tiSh+Tha hU~M phaT swAhA//oM b+hi shun badzra puSh+paM pra tits+tsha swAhA phaT swAhA/oM ra ga badzra puSh+paM
75a2(rgyud): pra tits+tsha hU~M phaT swahA//oM I ru Sha badzra puSh+paM pra tits+tsha phaT swahA//de bzhin du d+hi paM dang /d+hu paM g+han+d+haM ne we d+h+ya/de lta bu'i nyer spyod lngas dkyil 'khor la mchod par bya'o//gang tshe dbus su mi g.yo dkar//gti mug
75a3(rgyud): rdo rje ma dang bcas//de'i dkyil 'khor nyid du shes//de bzhin mi g.yo ser po laswoT//rnal 'byor lnga ni rab dbye bas//dkyil 'khor lnga ru rtag par bya//sems nyid rtse gcig gyur pa yis//sngon du snyen pa rtsam
75a4(rgyud): par bya//dkyil 'khor rab bzhugs rnal 'byor ba/rnal 'byor dang ni yang dag ldan//phan tshun tu ni phyag byas nas//bza' ba sha dang chang yang bya//zhes pa ni dga'o gcig pa'i nyid du gsungs pa'i
75a5(rgyud): dpal khro bo chen po rtum po'i rgyud las/dkyil 'khor gyi rim bar phye ba ste//gnyis pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol pa//slob ma ji lta bu nyid la/
75a6(rgyud): rgyud 'di rab tu sbyor bar bya//the tsom med par bya ba nyid//khyab bdag chen pos bstan du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as kyis bka' stsal ba dang //dang por skya bsu 'gro ba'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di'o//
75a7(rgyud): tshad ni/ji srid snying po byang chub bar//sangs rgyas la ni skyabasu mchi//dam pa'i chos la bdag skya bas mchi//dge 'dun la yang mi byed dang //de la bslab pa lnga'i tshigasu bcad pa ni//srog gcod ma byin len pa dag
75a8(rgyud): gzhan gyi bud med rdzun smra rnams//sbang bya kun la 'phags bzhin bya//lnga pa chang gi btung ba nyid//de la 'dir/bso sbyong gi tshigasu bcad pa ni//bdag gi sems ni bsad mi bya//pha rol nor ni 'phrog mi bya//
75b1(rgyud): bdag ni tshangs pa spyod pa spyad//brdzun gyi tshig kyang spangs par bya//myos byed chang gi btung ba ni//bdag gis gang du'ang btung mi bya//gar dang klu dang rgyan rnams dang//rgod ba ga rnams ni spangs par bya//khri bstan
75b2(rgyud): 'tho dang khri stan che//dus min kha zas spang bar bya//de ltar bso sbyong yan lag brgyad//dgra bcom rjes su bdag gis bslab//ji ltar sangs rgyas kyis gsungs ltar//rnam dgra bdag gis bzung bar bgyi/
75b3(rgyud): de yis g.yon can bdud las rgyal//bla med sangs rgyas mchog thob pa//'dir ni srid pa nam gyur nas//lus can kun gyi skyabs gyur te//'khor ba ji srid ma lus par//skyes bu bde bar gshegs par
75b4(rgyud): skyes//skye bo dgra bo dang grogs 'dir//blo ldan 'jig rten la phan dga'//de la 'dir ni chu'i dbang//slob ma sdig pa dang bral zhing//shel ni dag pa bzhin bsams nas//rnam par rgyal 
75b5(rgyud): ba'i bum pa nas//chu blangs te lo ma dang bcas pa'i lag pas/oM sarba ta thA g+ha ta a b+hi she Sha ta shri hU~M sa ma ya shri hU~M/zhes pa 'dis dbang skur bar bya'o//de la 'dir dbu rgyan gyi 
75b6(rgyud): dbang ni/gos la sogs pa la byas pa'i dbu rgyan rin po che sna bdun las grub par bsams nas//slob ma yang 'khor lo bsgyur ba ltar bsams nas/mgo bor dbu rgyan sbyin par bya'o//sngon byin du 
75b7(rgyud): dbang skur bar byas/oM tsaN+De mahA ro Sha a b+hi Sha a s+ya hri da ye hU~M phaT//de la ral gri'i dbang skur ba ni/lcags la sogs pa'i rang bzhin las//ral gri de'i lag pa g.yas su byin nas/
75b8(rgyud): sngon bzhin du dbang skur bar byas te/oM ha na ha na/mA ra yA mA ra yA/sarba sha truM dza na kha Ta ka hU~M phaT/de la 'dir zhags pa'i dbang skur ba ni/zangs la sogs pa las byas pa'i zhags pa de 
76a1(rgyud): /sdig 'dzub dang bcas pas lag pa g.yon bar byin nas/sngon bzhin du dbang skur bar byas te/oM g+h+rih+na g+h+ri h+na ka d+ha la ya~M ka d+ha ya/sarba duSh+Tan ba she b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/mahA sa t+ya te d+harmA te swAhA/
76a2(rgyud): de la 'dir ming gi dbang ni slob ma khro bo rtum po chen po'i phyag rgya ru nye bar bkod nas /de'i rnam pa sems kyis dmigs te/oM he shri b+ha ga wan kri kha tsha la sti d+ha ta~M hU~M phaT/de nas sngon de bzhin du dbang 
76a3(rgyud): skur bar bya'o//de bzhin du sgrub pa pos nag po la sogs pa'i dbye bas /mi g.yo ba rnam pa lnga'i ming gis dbang skur ba sbyin par bya'o//zhes pa ni dbang ldan no//bud med rnams kyi dbu rgyan gyis dbang 
76a4(rgyud): skur ba gzhag nas/sin d+hu la'i dbang skur ba sbyin par bya 'o//rgyal srid kyi lha mo chen mo'i tshul du slob ma dmigs te/oM b+ha ga wa ti a b+hi shi a b+hi sha ar+ya hri da ye hU~M phaT/lcags laswog+sa 
76a5(rgyud): pa'i gri khug de'i lag pa g.yasu byin par bya'o//oM ka ra kri ke sarba ma~M na~M mi ka~M ba~M kar ta ya hU~M phaT/lag pa g.yon du mi'i thod pa 'am/rdzas las byas pa byin par bya'o//oM badzra 
76a6(rgyud): ka pA la sarba sha tru~M na ra ka~M /d+ha ra ya hU~M phaT/de nas bcom ldan 'das ma'i phyag rgya ru rnam par bkod nas/de'i rnam par dmigs te/A+o~M hi shrI d+he sha badzra si d+hi sta~M hU~M phaT /de bzhin du bud 
76a7(rgyud): med ni nag mo la sogs pa'i kha dog gi dbye ba la rnal 'byor ma lnga po/rnams kyi ming gi dbang skur bar bya/'dir shes rab kyis dbang skur ba'i gnas su thabs kyis dbang skur bar bya'o//zhes so//
76a8(rgyud): de nas gsang ba'i dbang skur bar gyur pa ni/bla ma la sogs slob mas gos la swogs mas/yang dag par mchod nas/de la yang yid du 'ong ba'i gzugs dang lam chos rgyan pa'i shes rab dbul bar bya'o//
76b1(rgyud): khyod la 'di ni dbul lags kyis/'dod kun bde ba rab tu rtsol//bdag ni 'dor ba'i bde don phyir//mgon po khyod ni rtse bas bzung//de nas bla ma la phyag 'tshal nas/slob ma phyir byung ste/oM tsan Ta ma hA 
76b2(rgyud): ro Sha na hU~M phaT/zhes ba'i sngags zlas zhing gnas par bya'o//yang bla mas chang dang sha la sogs pa rnams kyis bdag nyid mchod nas/shes rab kyang tshim par byas te/yang dag par sbyor ba las nye bar byung 
76b3(rgyud): ba'i dkar dmar lo ma'i skyod bu la sogs te gzhag nas slob ma bos te/de'i lce la 'the 'dod dang ming med kyis rdzas bzung nas/hU~M phaT kyi yi ge bri bar bya'o//de nas a ho su kha zhes brjod par 
76b4(rgyud): bya'o//de nas brjod par byas te/di ring bdag gis sangs rgyas kyi ye shes bskyed par bya yis/gang gis 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
76b5(rgyud): 'das rab tu mi gnas pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa thob par 'gyur gyis/'on kyang khyed kyis dkyil 'khor ma mthong ba rnaMs kyi 'dun du smras par mi bya'o//de nas slob ma'i snying khar gzhag 
76b6(rgyud): nas 'di skad brjod par byas te/gal te smras na de'i tshe//shin du rno ba'i ral gri 'di//khro bo rtum po'i phyag na gnas//gang gis dam tshig 'jig byed pa//de la 'di yis mchog tu gcod//
76b7(rgyud): skye ba bye ba stong rnamasu//mi rnams ral gri lag phyar bas//yan lag kun spyod mgo bo ni//'di yis mchog tu gcod par byed//gal te nan tshig 'di nyid kyis//khyod kyis 'jigs pa thob par 'gyur//
76b8(rgyud): de nas slob mas brjod byas te//de ltar nges zhes so//de nas gdong g.yogs bcings nas/dkyil 'khor du metog dbul bar bya'o//de nas gdong g.yogs gsal nas/dkyil 'khor rab tu bstan par bya/
77a1(rgyud): //gang dang gang gi mtshan ma de nas stan par bya'o//de nas slob ma la shes rab ma byin par bya'o//gzugs ma yid 'ong 'di khyod kyis//sten byar sangs rgyas rnams kyis gsungs//rmongs pa 
77a2(rgyud): gang zhig 'o byed pa//de la mchog gi dngos grub med//de nas dga' ba bzhi'i rnam par dbye ba bla mas rnam par smra bar bya'o//de nas bla ma phyi rol du byung nas/shes rab gcer bur tsog bur gsang nas 
77a3(rgyud): gsang gnas 'dzub mos bstan nas/ci ste bu khyod 'di spros sam//nga la mi rtsang ba dang ni//gshang lci rag ta za ba dang //b+ha ga'i gnas la 'o byed dam//sgrub pa pos smras bar byas te//khyod la mi 
77a4(rgyud): rtsad za ba ni//ci'i phyir ni bdag mi spros//ji srid byang chub snying po'i bar//bdag ni mi mo kun mos bya//b+ha ga'i gnas la 'o byad dam//sgrub pa po smra bar bya ste//des kyang e ma ho bdag 
77a5(rgyud): gi pad ma 'di//bde ba thams cad yang dag ldan//gang zhig cho gas bsten byed na//bdag ni dngos grub rtsol 'dzad ma//blo ldan rtan par rab sbyar ba//pad mar bya ba ji bzhin gyis//
77a6(rgyud): rang nyid khro bo rtum po chen por gnas pa/bde ba chen po ste/de nas sgrub pa po bdag nyid khro bo rtum po'i rnam par bsams nas/shes rab kyang zhe sdang rdo rje ma'i tshul gyis yang dag par sbyor bas 
77a7(rgyud): dga' ba bzhi mtshon par bya'o//de nas yongs su rdzogs pa dang /bla ma la sogs pa la chang dang /sha la sogs pa'i tshogs kyi 'khor los mchod pa dbul bar bya'o//zhes pa ni shes rab 
77a8(rgyud): kyi dbang po'o//zhes pa ni dpa'o gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa'i dpal khro bo rtum po chen po'i rgyud las/dbang skur ba'i rim par phye ba ste//gsum pa'o////de nas 
77b1(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/yid dang 'thun pa'i sa phyogs ni//bar du gcod pa kun bral bar//ji ltar rnyed pa'i stan nyid la//de las mnyam gzhag rtag par bya//dang po byams pa sgom par bya//gnyis 
77b2(rgyud): pa snying rje sgom par bya//gsum pa la dga' ba sgom//kun gyi mtha' la btang snyoms so//de nas rang gi thugs ka'i sa bon las//pad ma dang zla ba la gnas pa'i 'od zer rnams kyi 'dun du bsam gtan gyi khro bo rtum 
77b3(rgyud): pos bsams na yid kyis mchod pa byas te/mkhas pas me tog la sogs pas/yid kyis de rnams mchod par bya/de 'dun sdig pa bshags par gyis//bsod nams la yang rjes yid rang //gsum la skyabs su 
77b4(rgyud): 'gro bar bya//skul zhing gsol ba rab tu 'debs//bdag nyid yongs su 'bul ba dang //bsod nams yongs su bsngo bar bya//de nas smon lam btab nas ni//byang chub sems ni bskyed par bya//
77b5(rgyud): de nas phyag ni btsal byas la//'od zer rnams ni slar yang sdu//mkhas pas stong par bsams pa ni//sngags pas 'di nyid brjod par bya//oM shu n+ya ta dz+nyA na badzra saM b+ha wa ad mo ke ha~M/
77b6(rgyud): rab tu 'bad pas hU~M yig ni//'od zer de yis sregs par bsam//ga pur sregs pas ji bzhin bsam//'od zer la yang rnam mi rtogs//thams cad nam mkha' ji bzhin du//skad cig gis ni rnam par sgom//
77b7(rgyud): dri med dag pa'i shel bzhin du//rang lus rnam par sgom par bya//sngon du sgom bya phyi nas ni//ya~M ra~M la~M bzhi rnams las//yongs su sgom bya rlung dang ni//b+h+ru~M yig de las sgom bya ba//gzhal yas khang du rab tu sgom//
77b8(rgyud): gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//ka ba brgyad kyis nye bas 'dzes//de'i dbus su bsam bya ba//sna tshogs pad ma 'dab ma brgyad ldan//ba~M yig sa bon las byung ba//de las oM yig las byung bla//ra~M yig las byung nyi ma ste//
78a1(rgyud): //de stengs hU~M yig las kyang slar//des bskyed mi skyod pa ru /bsam//ma ma ki bcas yang dag sbyor//de la ri bzhin rnal 'byor dbang //de'i spyi bo la thim nas//skar mda' ji bzhin sbyor ba yis//ma ma
78a2(rgyud): ki'i ba gar bsam//sha za bar na rab tu bsam//de la khu ba ro mnyam 'gyur//lhung 'gyur de'i b+ha ga'i nang /gtum po'i gzugasu gyur nas ni//b+ha ga'i mtha' las nges 'byung ba//mi skyod pa las ral gri yis//
78a3(rgyud): snun nas phyi nas rab tu zla//ma mA ki las de las de'i//sha za bar du rab tu bsam//de phyir ma mA kis bzung nas//ma dang yang dag rab 'dod bya//des kyang mkhyud par bsam bya ba//zhe sdang rdo rje ma'i gzugs//
78a4(rgyud): phyag g.yas na ni ral gri 'char//g.yon pa zhags pa bcas pa yi//sdig 'dzub kyis ni sdigs par byed//so yis ma mchu yang dag sngams//rkang g.yas rdebs pa'i sngabs kyis bdud//sa bzhi
78a5(rgyud): rnam par nyed par 'dzad//pus mo g.yon pa sa la btsugs//'jigs byed 'khyog bor gzigs pa can//nor 'dzin la ni sdig 'dzub bstan//pus mo g.yon pa'i rtse mos gnas//mi skyod sngon
78a6(rgyud): po dbu rgyan 'dzad//rin po che'i spyod pan can//rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan gyur pa'i//gzhonu zur phung lnga dang ldan//brgyad gnyis lo'i rnam pa can//sbun gnyis dmar po rtso bo yi//sems nyid rtan pas sgoM
78a7(rgyud): par bya//bdag nyid khro bo rtum grub na//de nas srub pa'i sbyor ba yis//shar du mi g.yo dkar po'o//me 'tshams gti mug rdo rje ma//ston ka'i zla 'od dang 'tshungs spro//lho ru mi g.yo ser po spro//phra ma rdo rje bden bral ser//
78a8(rgyud): mi g.yo dmar po nub tu spro//rlung du 'dod chags rdo rje ma//mi g.yo ljang khu byang phyogasu//dbang ldan phra dog rdo rje ma//phyi nas shes rab dang bcas des//rang gi mgrin pa las byung ba'i//lha mo rnams kyi klu yis bskul//
78b1(rgyud): ba hU~M mI trI tu b+hi badzra a ho hi ma shun+nA ba ha ba ho//dz+wa bi yo e phe TA mi sa the dza ka hi b+ha b+ha ba/gti mug rdo rje sems so//mA ka ru na tsi ya/pha n+ya hi ba hu ma ho hi te tu/shun+nA sA ma mo dzu te ha/su d+ha k+shi i ha/i dzi ba bi 
78b2(rgyud): hu n+na/phra ma rdo rje ma yis so//ki pa tu ha ra pa/bi to hi ta shun+nA hi ka ri pi ba be sha//ta~M dzu ni man+na ka ri Ta/ma ni a ts+tsha i lo a sha la/'dod chags rdo rje ma yis so//yo ba na ba ni bu pa/khi a ni Sh+Tha la/
78b3(rgyud): u rtsa a d+hi TA sh+tsa rtsa sa hA ba bi go i a kar hi tu mo li sa/ma g+hi Ta/phra dog rdo rje ma'i 'o//khyab bdag byams par rnam grangs nas/yo ba na ban+nI mu bu/stong pa'i rang bzhin du zhig 'dzad//khyod dang rnam bral 
78b4(rgyud): bdag ma gus pas//'gro kun stong par ma mdzad cig//phra ma rdo rje sems so//ci ste mkhas pa khyod nyid ni//dga' ba spangs nas stong par 'jug//'jig rten ma lus pa rnams dang //bdag gi 
78b5(rgyud): yid ni khyod la bstod//'dod chags rdo rje ma yis sa//lang mtsho dar ldan btang snyoms pas//mig ni stong bar 'khrul med cing //stong pa'i rang bzhin nyid spongs la//bdag la mnyam nyid sbyor bar 
78b6(rgyud): 'dzod//phra dog rdo rje sems so//'di ni rmi lam ltar thos pa'i//khu ba las 'khrungs skad cig gis//sngon ma'i gzugs bzhin bsam par bya//bdag nyid yang dag sbyor de la//mi g.yo dkar po la snun nas//
78b7(rgyud): gti mug rdo rje rab 'dod bya//mi g.yo nad kar po'i gzugasu bya//mi g.yo ser po slar bsang nas/'dod chags phra ma rdo rje ma//bdag nyid mi g.yo ser por bya//de bzhin mi g.yo dmar po gsang //'dod chags 
78b8(rgyud): rdo rje mar 'dod bya//mi g.yo dmar po bdag nyid bya//mi g.yo ljang khu gsang nas kyang //phra dog rdo rje mar 'dod bya//mig g.yo ljang khu bdag nyid bya//lha mo bzhi la rjes chags pas//dkyil 'khor thams cad yang dag tu//
79a1(rgyud): /bdag nyid gcig tu yang dag sbyor//'bad pa yis ni sgom par bya//de la rgyal ba byas pa ni/bdag la dngos grub the tsom med//kha dog gnag phyir rnal 'byor bas//gang sgom de ni mi g.yo gnag//
79a2(rgyud): kha dog dkar phyir rnal 'byor bas//gang sgom de ni mi g.yo kar//kha dog ser phyir rnal 'byor pa//gang sgom de ni mi g.ye ser//kha dog dmar phyir rnal 'byor pa//gang sgom de ni mi g.yo dmar//kha dog ljang khu 
79a3(rgyud): rnal 'byor ba//gang sgom de ni mi g.ya ljang //kha dog nag mo'i bud med des//zhe sdang rdo rje ma sgom bya//kha dog dkar mo'i bud med des//gti mug rdo rje ma sgom bya//kha dog ser po'i bud med des//phra ma rdo rje ma sgom bya/
79a4(rgyud): kha dog dmar mo'i bud med des//'dod chags rdo rje ma sgom bya//kha dog ljang khu'i bud med des//phra dog rdo rje ma sgom bya//skyes kun rdo rje rnal 'byor ma//bud med rdo rje rnal 'byor ma//nag phyogs 
79a5(rgyud): kha dog dbye ba yis//'di rnams kun la rab tu rtag//yang na las kyi dbye ba yis//dbye ba lnga ru rab tu rtag//gnag phyir bsad dang zhe sdang la//zhi la dkar po dag tu rtag//rengs dang rgyas 
79a6(rgyud): la ser po ste//dbang dang dgug la dmar po'o//skrod bu las ni ljang khur bshad//yang na rigs kyi rab dbye bar//bram ze dkar po rmang rigs nag//rtum mo ser mo dag la 'dod//dmar mo gar ma dag la 'dod//
79a7(rgyud): ljang khu chos ma zhes bya'o//bu mo sngo bsangs mig yangs ma//nag mo sgom pa pos bya//bu mo kar mo kar mo yis//bu mo dmar mo dmar mo yis//bu mo ljang khu ljang khu yis//yang na gang gis de yis 'dzin pa//de dang 
79a8(rgyud): de yi sgom pa mchog//ji lta ba yang ni shes pas//sems nyid rtan bas 'dod par bya//phyogs tsam rab tu sbyor ba las//bu mo des ni dngos grub ster//rdo rje shud krar gyur ba las//lce yis kun nas 'byib bar bya//
79b1(rgyud): ji srid 'dod pa'i gshang lci yang//de'i kha dang b+ha gar sbyin//dgu nga pad ma rab 'jib pas//boSh+Na ci 'dod rab bza' bya//ngo tsha skyu ga dro byed 'gyur na/rnam pa kun du dngos grub nyams//'di ni rang gi bza' ba mchog//
79b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyis rab tu bzas//zhes bya ba ni dpa' bo gcig pur gsungs pa'i dpal khro bo rtum po'i rgyud las//lha nyid kyi rim par phye ba ste/bzhi pa 'o////de nas sngags rnams kun
79b3(rgyud): las btus//bdag gis rab tu bshad par gyis//de nas bcom ldan 'das bdud thams cad kyis mi thub pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin las snyoms par zhugs nas/sngags kun las btus pa 'di gsungaso//
79b4(rgyud): oM tsan+d+ra ma hA ro Sha na hU~M phaT/rtsa ba'i sngags so//oM a tsa la hU~M hri/rtsa ba'i sngags gnyis pa'o//oM hU~M phaT/rtsa ba'i sngag gsum pa'o//hU~M snying po'i sngags so//hU~M snying po'i sngags gnyis
79b5(rgyud): pa'i/hU~M snying po'i sngagasagasum pa'o//oM hrI hrI hrIM tsan Da ru be tsa Ta tsa Ta ka ha ka ha/pra phu ra pra phu ra/pra phu ra ya ha na ha na/rab tu spyod pa kun spangs te/yid dang 'thun pa'i sa phyogasu//
79b6(rgyud): rang sems 'od ldan ma dga' ma//rab tu spabs pas rtsam par bya//shes rab pha rol phyin dga' mas//bdag ni sangs rgyas mi g.yor grub//rang gzugs shin tu 'bad pa yis//mkhas pas sems kyis sgom par bya//
79b7(rgyud): skye bos dben byas gnas su ni//ji ltar rnyed pa'i gos kyis ni//gnyis gnyis phan tshun sbyor ba yis//rab tu 'bad de sgom par bya//mi mo mngon sum byas nas ni//mngon du phyogs pas nyer 'dug te//phan tshun gnyis
79b8(rgyud): po chags pa yis//rab dga' phan tshun lta bar bya//de nas mthong bde'i bsam gtan ni//yid ni rtse gcig gnas par bya//de yis de la de nyid brjod//bde ba'i gzi mdangs byed ba smra//de la bu khyod bdag gis bdag//
80a1(rgyud): //khyod ni ming po mes po dang//bdag ni khyod kyi chung ma dang/sring mo dang ni tsha mo yin//skye bo bdun brgyud zhabs ring ba//khyod ni bdag gi g.yog yin te//bdag ni khyod kyi rin gyis nyos//bdag gi khyod ni
80a2(rgyud): rje dpon ma//rkang pa dag gi drung btud nas//shin du bal mo rab sbyar nas//de la 'di bzhin brjod par bya//bde ba'i gzi mdangs mchod byed brjod//khyod ni bdag gi pha ma dang//bdag gi chung ma tsha mo yin//sring mo
80a3(rgyud): bu'i chung ma dang//khyod ni bdag gi rje dpon yin//khyod ni bdag gi rnam kun du//shin du gus pas dpung gnyen mchog//bdag la yum gyis rtse bas gzigs//yid 'ong lta bas de ltas nas//de nas yang dang yang du ni/
80a4(rgyud): skyes bu yis ni btsum par bya//yi ge gsum ni g+ho swogs sbyin//kha las kha'i bdud rtsi dang//langs nas pad ma stan par bya//mig 'phrul kha yis 'byi ba byas nas//ku tsa snying khar btsir bar bya//
80a5(rgyud): mngon phyogs de'i gdong ltas nas//tsu ta'i gnas la smra bar bya//de la lta ba'i tshig la ni//bdag gi rnam par snang 'dzad bza'//khyod ni mi skyod chu 'thungs na//bu dang pha dang bran po 'o//khyod kyi
80a6(rgyud): rje dpon ma nyid dang//rgyal rigs ma'i yum nyid dang//bu khyod rab tu 'bad pa yis//bdag gi zhabs la skyabs su song//gang phyir bdag ni 'phel byed ma//khyod la rin chen nye bar 'ongs//bu khyod byas bzor gyis
80a7(rgyud): shig kye//rdo rje bde ba skyed la byin//dbus su ze 'brus rgyan pa yis//chu skyes 'dab ma gsum par gzigs//a ho bde ba can gyi zhing//sangs rgyas sku mdog dmar po 'dzes//chags can zhi bde ster 'dzad pa//
80a8(rgyud): rtog pa thams cad rnam par spangs//bdag ni gan rgyal yang dag sbyor//chags pas yid ni rnam par myos//phrag par rkang pa bzung byin nas//bdag gi steng dang 'og tu ltos//de nas rdo rje rab gsang bas//pad dbus ran trar
80b1(rgyud): rab tu bzhug//brgya stong 'bum dang bye ba phrag//de nas skyod pa nyid du bya//bdag pad 'dab ma gsum ldan pa//sha yis rab tu gang ldan la//rang gi rdo rjer de zhugs pas//sems nyid bde bas rab tu mchod//rlung dang rlung 
80b2(rgyud): gis bdag pad la//snying po las ni snying po mchog//rdo rje'i rtse mor rab tu rig//b+han d+hu dmar po dang 'tshungs pa//'dzag pa'i bsam gtan de la ni//slar yang sems nyid tshim par bya//shin du mi g.yo'i sems nyid kyis//
80b3(rgyud): bde ba bde skyes sgom par bya//de la lan ni rab tu sbyin//dga' ma khyod ni skad cig sdod//ji srid ma lus b+ha ga'i gzugs//skad cig tsam zhig sgom par bya//de la yum gyur nyid nges par 'gro ba gsum//zhing dang 
80b4(rgyud): bde ba'i ma ma de//sdig pa'i las la gnas nyid mu cor smra ba'i zhe sdang ba las/'di ni smon par byed//de dang de rnams gting mtha' med pa'i dmyal bar de nyid mi bza' rtag tu sdug bsngal nyid//
80b5(rgyud): lus ni lan mang me yis sreg cing du 'bod du ni bskal pa gsum du gnas par byed//mi mo'i yon tan gang brjod na//sems can thams cad yongasu 'dzin//ji snyed rtse dang srung ba dang //mi mo'i sems nyid 
80b6(rgyud): la rab gnas//rang gzhan skye ba de nyid kyang //kun nas de la yang yang rgyas//dpal gyi rdo rje rnal 'byor ma//gal te de gzugs las gzhan min//de yis lta dang rengs pa dang //rnyed po kun nas ring zhog gis//gang 
80b7(rgyud): zhig dran pa tsam gyis kyang //skad cig de la bde ba thob//yul lnga rnams la mi mo yis//gzugs 'di nyid ni mchog tu gnas//de la bde ba 'bab 'dzad pa//skyes bu rnams kyis rol par gyis//de phyir de las nges 
80b8(rgyud): grol ba//yon tan dam pa kun gyis rgyan//skad cig de la bde bar thob//yul lnga rnams la ni mo yis//gzugs 'di ni mchog tu gnas//bsod nams bsod nams bsod nams che//gsum gyis bdag la bka' drin 'jod//
81a1(rgyud): //de nas de la shin du lta//mchu ni so yis sdam par bya//nu ma 'gram dang si ta yog//de'i gos bral rnal 'byor pas//byed cing gis ni bde 'byung bya//de li la zhon bcing ba dang //pus mo bzung la de nyid bcing /
81a2(rgyud): bcing ba rlag rnyed pa yang yin//rkang pas gcod byed bcing ba yang //bcings pas sa ni mnan par bya//mkhas pas mnyam cing de nyid ni//bcing ba ri mo can zhes bya//bung ba'i dra ba bcing ba dang //'khrul 'khor de zhon
81a3(rgyud): rkang pa can//de bzhin nyid du rus sbal bcing //kun nas bzang po de bzhin nyid//de la dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//mi mo tsug pu stan la 'dug//dpung pa bzung gis prag pa la//de la myur du sbyar bar bya//rang gi lag pa bzung dag
81a4(rgyud): de'i//rkyed pa'i dbus su nyer son te//pad mar rdo rje rab bcug nas//bde ba 'byung ba'i bcings par klags//gnyis ka'i lag bzung rab bcug nas//phan tshun sbyor bas bcings par bya//
81a5(rgyud): gnyi kas cung zad skyod par bya//do lo'i skyod pa zhes grags so//de'i pus gnyis rang gi ni//snying ka nyid du yang dag sbyar//de la skyod pa'i byed pa dgod//'di ni pus mos bzung nas byin//de'i
81a6(rgyud): rkang 'thil rang nyid kyi//brla'i khar yang sbyar bar bya//bde ba 'byung byed bkod pa yis//'di'i rla rnyed bcing ba'o//de'i nu ma rkang pa dang //lte ba snying spogs gnyis bkab pa//de la g.yo ba'i byed pa dgod//bcing ba
81a7(rgyud): 'di ni rkang pas gcod//de'i 'phongs gnyis sa la gzhag//pha rol la ni 'khyud pa pos//bde ba 'byung byed bkado nas ni//bcing ba 'di ni sa la gnan//tsug pus yang dag gnas de la//rkang pa gnyis po rab rkyang bya//
81a8(rgyud): bcing ba mnyam du shes byas la//re re zhib yang rkyang bar bya//de'i rkang zur 'khor lor bya//g.yob par rab tu sbyar bar bya//kun nas mngon par phyogs pas kyang //rgyab nas 'phongs la reg par bya//lag pas dang por
81b1(rgyud): gnye bar bya//bcing 'dir ci 'dra can zhes bya//slar yang bde ba 'byung byas la//de la gan rgyal sgyel bar bya//lag pa g.yas pas de nyid du//pad mar rdo rje des bzhug bya//de'i phus mo'i 'og bzung nas//gri khug steng du
81b2(rgyud): sbyar bar bya//phan tshun lag pa gnyis kyis ni//bung ba'i dra ba zhes su shes//de'i rkang pa zung dag ni//phrag pa'i steng du nges par byin//bcing ba de ni 'khrul 'khor zhon//'bebs shing 'bebs pas rab tu sbyor//
81b3(rgyud): de'i rkang g.yon phrag pa la//g.yas g.yon rla'i rtsa ba las//de'i rkang g.yas g.yon pa las//g.yas pa yi ni rtsa ba ru//'dir ni rkang pa steng bcings pas//yang dag bde bas stug bsngal 'joms//de'i rkang
81b4(rgyud): ba'i 'thil la ni//brang gi dbus su mnyam du sbyor//lag pa gnyis kyis bcing bar bya//pus mo rus pa bcings par gsungs//de'i rkang pa'i 'thil la ni//mig dang rna ba mgo bo la//sbyar//bcing
81b5(rgyud): ba 'di ni kun du bzang //'dod kun bde ba rab tu ster//tsi tra ji srid dkyil dkrungs byas//kun nas sna tshogs pa yis//ni//khro bo rtum po'i sbyor ba yis//kun nas rnam pa sna tshogs
81b6(rgyud): pa//ji srid 'dod pas yang dang yang //de'i kha lo bya'o//mig ni zim bus gdong du lta/ji ltar 'dod tshig smra ba ni//gdong la lce yis 'jib par bya//de'i kha nas byung ba 'ang btung //so'i dri ma 'ad
81b7(rgyud): za zhing 'jib//bde bar rnam par sgom par bya//so yis lce dang mchu ni sdam//lce yis sna'i bu ga dang //mig gis zur kyang sbyang bar bya//so dang 'chan khung las byung ba'i//dri ma kun kyang za bar bya//mgo dang
81b8(rgyud): mig dang 'grin par rna//logs dang rkyed ba nu ma lag//'os 'ji ba sen mo sbyin par bya//mig gnyis sbangs la bud med la//lag pas nu ma gnye bya zhing //so yang gang ba cing 'jib par bya//rang gis gan
83a1(rgyud): /rgyal byas nas 'jib//lto ba 'dzes pa 'jib par byed//bdag gis sngon gnas 'di nyid ni//yang dang yang du dran par bya//pad mar lag pas reg par bya//rang gis 'dzes pa zhes smra ba//de la
83a2(rgyud): 'ongs dang sen mo sbyin//lta zhing lag pas bsal bar bya//mi mos dri ma snam bya zhing//bu ga'ang ro 'dzin gyis sbyang bya//ji lta bar ni bdag khugs nas//'di yis kyang ni dpag med bskyed//de lta lta zhing
83a3(rgyud): tshig 'di brjod//lam 'di nyid ni drang ba ste//de nyid mi shes sbyor ba yis//lam 'di 'gro ba dug du 'jug//sbyor ba rab tu shis pa yis//khro bo rtum pos dngos grub 'gyur//de phyir pad byung dkar
83a4(rgyud): dmar ni//kha yis dgus par byas nas ni//yang dang yang ni lta byas nis//de yi ni kha yis bza' bar bya//sen mo legs byas brla dang ni//bran bzhin rkang pas gnye bar bya//mgo bor
83a5(rgyud): yi ge gsum byin bya//snying dbus khu tshur chung dus sbyin//rnal 'byor pa ni mnyam gzhag pas//de phyir gzhan don ldan par bya//yid ni rtse gcig pa yis ni//'dod pa'i bya ba
83a6(rgyud): de la byin//ji ltar 'dod pa rab 'dzag pas//'pho med yid ni rtse gcig bde//'phos 'gyur pad ma la 'jib ste//pus btsugs rab tu sbyor ba yis//pad mar khu ba rtul 'gyur pa//lce yis
83a7(rgyud): blangs nas bza' bar bya//sna rtse yis ni rab sbyor bas//stobs ni 'phel 'phel btung bar bya//kha lo yi ni byas nas kyang//shes rab pad ma lca yis myang//'khyud byas de'i phyi nas ni//sha dang nya ni
83a8(rgyud): bza' bya zhing//'o ma chang ni yang yang btung//'dod pas rab tu 'phel 'gyur cing//ngal dang rnyil gyur de las ni//phyi nas 'dod gyur bde sogs kyi//slar yang snga ma'i rim pa bzhin//gnyis gnyis
83b1(rgyud): phan tshun rab tu rtsam//goms pa'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//bde ba chen pos sgrub par bya//skye ba 'di nyid sbyor ba na//rig na khro bo rtum po'i go 'phang 'dzin//chags can dngos grub don gyi phyir//bdag gis
83b2(rgyud): sbyor ba rab sbyar bya//g.yon pa'i byin pa steng gzhag nas//byin pa g.yon pa rol pa can//'di ni pad ma'i dkyil dkrungs grags//'dod kun bde ba rab tu ster//pad ma'i dkyil dkrungs bcings nas ni//
83b3(rgyud): byin pa g.yon pa rab tu dgod//byin pa g.yas pa rol pa ste//rdo rje dkyil dkrungs zhes byar shes//rkang pa sa la gzhag nas ni//mtshungs par ring du gdod phyogs pa//'dod kun rab tu sbyin shes bya/
83b4(rgyud): 'dir ni tsog pu'i gdan la ni//rkang pa sa la rgod byas pas//skyog por phreng la ring du bya//zla ba phyed par shes par bya//'dis ni bde ba rab tu ster/pus bzung phreng la sa la gzhag//
83b5(rgyud): long bu'i dbus su 'phongs chos gzhag//'dir ni bzhu'i bden byas pas//'dod pa mchog gi dga' ba ste//sos dpa'i dkyil 'khrungs de bzhin du//rdo rje dkyil dkrungs zhas
83b6(rgyud): byar rtag//tsog pus zla ba phyed pa dang/bzhu'i gdan yang 'dir 'dod do//zla ba phyed pa'i gdan gnas la//rtag tu mi mo byas nas ni//lhung na pad mar lce yis blang//gsang gnas yi ge gsum mtshon bya//slar yang bzhu'i
83b7(rgyud): gdan byas nas//rang gi ma'i bshang nang du//khro bo rtum po sbyor ba yis//des bskyed bde ba sgom par bya//rnam par rtog pa kun spangs nas//de'i rnal 'byor 'dod par bya//chags bral sems ni spang byas la//ma la
83b8(rgyud): rab tu 'dod par bya//rjes su chags pas bsod nams thob//chags bral las ni sdig pa thob//chags bral las nisdig gzhan med//bde la bsod nams gzhan pa med//de phyir sems nyid mnyam gzhag nas//
84a1(rgyud): //'dod pa'i bde ba bskyed par byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das ma thugs rab tu dgos nas/mngon par dga' ba bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/'di skad du yang gsol ba//kye ma bcom ldan
84a2(rgyud): 'das ci ste 'dir 'di nyid/mi rnams b+ha zhig sgrub pa'i thabs yin nam/gzhan rnams kyi yang yin//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/phyogs kun du rnam par gnas pas/'di la rjesu chags par

84a5(rgyud): de la dbang phyug chen po rdo rje bde chen nyid du grub po//nor lha rdo rje sred med kyi bu nyid do//lha'i dbang po phyag na rdo rje nyid do//'dod pa'i lha yan lag med pa'i rdo rje nyid do//de lta bu la sogs pa'i
84a6(rgyud): gang ga'i klung bye ma dang mnyam pa'i lha'i bu rnams kyis grub po//sems can don kun sgrub pa po//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga ldan nyid//'byung po sna tshogs gzugs 'dzin pa//sgyu ma kun las rnam par rgyal//ji ltar bad mo
84a7(rgyud): las byung ba'i//pa dam 'dam gyis gos pa med//de ltar 'dod chags las byung ba'i//nyes pa rnams kyis gos pa med//zhes pa'i dpa'o gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa'i/dpal khro bo rtum po'i rgyud las////

84b2(rgyud): chang ni btung bya mnyam gzhag pas//gzhan yang ji ltar rnyed pa za//chu'i rag ta la swogs bza'//thog ma bud med la byin nas//de'i lhag ma za bar bya//de'i lhag ma'i snod la ni//longs ni rtag tu spyod par bya//
84b3(rgyud): de'i kha bshal chu dang ni//pad ma bkrus pa'ang btung bar bya//bshang sgo pad ma bkrus bzung nas//rtul shugs can gyis kha sogs bkru//de'i skyugs pa za bya zhing //de'i gzhi mnyam za bar bya//skye ba'i 
84b4(rgyud): gnas las btung bar bya//gshang lci dag ni bza' bar bya//ji ltar zhing la lud sogs kyis//'bras bu lhag par 'gyur ba ltar//de bzhin mi rtsang sogs spyod pas//bde ba dam pa skyes 
84b5(rgyud): bu la//na dang rga ba nyid dang ni//de'i lus la mchi ba med//gang de'i mi rtsang sten byed pa'i//rnal 'byor pa yis de 'grub byed//gang du bza' dang bza' min dang //'dir ni rnam rtog mi bya'o//
84b6(rgyud): sbyor rig bya dang bya min dang //bgrod min bgrod pa'ang de bzhin no//bsod nams med cing sdig pa'ang med//mtho ris thar pa'ang rtag mi bya//lhan cig skyes pa cig bu'i gzugs//rnal 'byor ba yis mnyam 
84b7(rgyud): gzhag bya//de nyid sbyor ldan rnal 'byor pa//gang tshe sgom pa mchog gyur pa//khro bo rtum po gcig sbyor bas//de'i nga brgyal 'dzin pa po//gal te bram ze brgya gsang kyang //sdig pa rnams kyis gos mi 'gyur//
84b8(rgyud): de phyir mgon po cho ga yis//khro bo rtum po de nyid sgom//skye bo gang zhig drag po yis//las kyi dmyal bar 'gro byed kyang //de la sdig pas mi gos shing //mtho ris 'gro bar the tsom med//yid ni sngon du 'gro ba yis//
85a1(rgyud): /dge dang sdig pa kun la 'dod//yid dang rnam rtog rnam pa la//gnas laswogs pa'i dbye bar bstan//ji ltar sngags dang bral ba yi//dug zos pas ni tshe zad 'gyur//sngags dang de nyid shes pa yis//
85a2(rgyud): bde ba dang ni tshe 'phel byed//de nas skad cig de nyid la//lha mo mchog gis shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin mas/gri khug dang thod pa'i tha phyag gis khro bo rtum po'i phyag rgya/rdo rje rtum mo khro chen mos//de nyid mchog
85a3(rgyud): la gzigs la gsungs//bdag gi gzugs ni sgom byas nas//nga rgyal mchog 'di byas gyur na//gal te tshangs 'joms de yang ni//sdig pa yis ni gos mi 'gyur//khro chen sems nyid gcig pu yis//
85a4(rgyud): bdag gi gzugs ni sgom byed na//nya dang bya ni bsad gyur kyang //rtse med g.yo dang khro ba dang //bsod pa'i don phyir sems dpa' can//rnal 'byor sdig pas gos mi 'gyur//de rnams kun la 
85a5(rgyud): phal cher ni//mthu chen bar ni rab tu bstan//zhes pa ni dpa' bo gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa ni/khro bo rtum po'i rgyud la lus kyi bde ba'i rim par phye ba ste/bdun pa'o////

85a7(rgyud): mar gus bya bar//rnal 'byor pa yis gang la bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das mas bka' stsal pa//nga ni 'jig rten gsum po na//mtho dang dma' ba'i gzugs gyur pa//bud med gzugs de la ni 'dod//ji srid 
85a8(rgyud): gyis mi mthong //lha mo lha min mo dang ni/de bzhin gnod sbyin srin mo dang //klu mo 'am 'byung mo mi ci mo//de bzhin mi'i bu mo dang //de bzhin dri za mo dang dmyal ba mo//dud 'gro'i bu mo yid tags mo//tshangs ma 
85b1(rgyud): rgyal rigs rje rigs mo//rmang rigs mo ste shin du mang//yi ge mkhan mo rgyal mo dang//gtso mo ka la u d+hi ni//chod mo bskyed mos tshal mo dang//smad 'chong ma dang chos mkhan ma//ko bags snod mkhan sha ba ri//ha Ti 
85b2(rgyud): ToM bi tsan Ti li//khrus ma chang ma la du 'khan//lcags 'gar gre ga mkhan gar ma dang//'gar ba mo dang gser mkhan mo//nya pa mo dang sha ba mo/sru ba ma mkhan dang me tog ma//thod pa can dang dung 'khan ma//gdugs 
85b3(rgyud): mkhan ma dang khang mkhan ma//ba lang skyong mda' mda' mkhan ma//'bru ga long rngo mkhan ma dang ni//shing mkhan rgyan cha mkhan mo dang//rigs rnams kun la rab tu 'jug//ma dang sring mo chung ma dang//a ne dang ni tsha mo dang/
85b4(rgyud): kha TI ga dang sgyug mo dang//gzhan dang nye 'dab ma kun dang//rtul shugs can dang rnal 'byor ma//yugs sa mo dang dka' thub ma//zheswogs mang po kun la ni//nga ni bud med gzugs gyur nas//sems 
85b5(rgyud): can kun gyi don phyir gnas//rang rang tshul du nges pa yis//de rnams ji ltar rnyed par dka'//'o dang 'khyud pa la sogs pa//rdo rje pad ma mnyam sbyor ba//rnal 'byor rnams kyis sten par bya//
85b6(rgyud): sems can kun la phan 'dod pas//mi mo snyen pas dngos grub po//skad cig tsam gyis snyen byed pa//de phyir bud med sten par bya//bud med mtho ris bud med chos//bud med de nyid dka' thub mchog/
85b7(rgyud): sangs rgyas bud med dge 'dun nyid//bud med shes rab phar phyin nyid//kha dog rnam lnga rab dbye las//so sor rtog par 'dod pa yin//bud med gang zhig kha dog sngo//zhesdang rdo rje ma ru grags//bud med kha gang zhig 
85b8(rgyud): kha dog dkar//gti mug rdo rje ma 'dod do//bud med gang zhig kha tog ser//phra mo phra ma rdo rje ma//bud med gang zhig kha ngog dmar//'dod chags rdo rje ma ru grags//budamed gang zhig kha dog ljang//phra dog rdo rje 
86a1(rgyud): /ma zhes grags//bcom ldan shes rab phar phyin ma//gcig nyid lnga'i tshul gis gnas//chang dang bza' ba me tog swogs//rnam mchod lus la gos kyis mdzes//yang dag smra bas 'dung byas nas//
86a2(rgyud): thal mo yang dag sbyar byas nas//lta dang reg pa nyid dang ni//dran pa yis ni smra bar bya//rtag dang 'o ma lag pa 'khyud//rdo rje rnal 'byor ma mchod bya//lus kyi nus pa rnam mchod nas//mi nus pa rnams ngag yid kyis//
86a3(rgyud): mchod pas rnyed des bdag la ni//dngos grub thams cad rtsol bar byed//bud med kun gyi lus spangs nas//bdag ni gzhan du gyur pa med//sna tshogs spangs nas bud med mchod//'di ni bdag la rab mchod 'gyur//
86a4(rgyud): 'di yis bdag la snyen bkur na//bsgrub pa po mnyes dngos grub mchog//de yis mthong ba'i lam zhugs na//rnam kun rtag tu kun du ster//ma lus gzugs kyis bdag sgoms na//rang gi bud 
86a5(rgyud): med 'dod sten bya//rdo rje pad ma mnyam sbyor bas//bdag la byang chub ster ba mo//de phyir rnam pa kun gyi ni//bdag la gus pas mchog tu de//'di yis de nyid rab sbyor ba//rtul shugs can 
86a6(rgyud): gyis dad gus bya//dmyal ba la sogs dam 'gro yi//sdig pa yis kying 'jigs mi bya//'jig rten sdig pas 'jigs gyur na//ji srid nus pa thob mi 'gyur//sdig pa cung zad yod min phyir//bsod nams 
86a7(rgyud): cung zad yod min phyir/sems nyid mi srung 'jig rten gang //sdig dang bsod nams rnam thar gnas//gang du sems nyid tsam gyis ni//thams cad skad cig tsam de gnas//dmyal bar 'gro ba 'dir gang gis//gang 
86a8(rgyud): gis 'dir ni mtho ris 'gro//gang gis dug rtags mthu yis mchi//ji lta ba la de nyid med//dug gi mthu ni yang dag rgyal//de bzhin mtho ris ngan 'gror 'gro//de nyid yongs su shes gyur pa'i//mkhas pas mya ngan 'das 
86b1(rgyud): ba thob/myang 'das stong pa'i tshul nyid du//mar me rlung gis ji bzhin du/nyer chad ltung ba de yang ni//byang chub go 'phangs ma yin no//de phyir thams cad yongs spangs nas//bdag nyid gus bya rtul shugs can//
86b2(rgyud): skad cig tsam gyis rtum pa yis//dngos grub ster bar the tsom med//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/bcom ldan 'das ma shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa laswogs pa/ji lta bu'i rnam pa yis//gtum po'i dngos 
86b3(rgyud): grub ci 'dra zhig//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as mas bka' stsal pa//kha dog lnga yis rab dbye bas/rnal 'byor ma ru rab grags gang//de yang rang rang bdag po yis//kha dog lnga yis rab dbyi las//
86b4(rgyud): rtum po kun gyi de nyid 'dis//bdag ni rnal 'byor ma ru shar//kha dog sngon po gang de yang//bdag po mi g.yo sngon por shes/kha dog dkar po'i bdag po gang//mi g.yo dkar po zhes bya'o//
86b5(rgyud): mi g.yo ser po yang dag grags//kha dog dmar po bdag po gang//kha dog dmar po zhes gragaso//kha dog ljang khu'i mi g.yo gang//mi g.yo ljang khu zhes bya'o//gtum po de nyid gcig bu nyid//
86b6(rgyud): rnam pa lnga'i tshul gyis gnas//'di'i dngos grub rtan pa nyid//de ni rtum por yang dag bshad//nam mkha'i mthar thug ji snyed pa//lha mo'i gzugs kyis yang dag gnas//rtum mo'i dngos grub ji ltar 
86b7(rgyud): gsungs//de bzhin du ni rtum mor 'grub//zhes bya ba ni dpa'o gcig pa nyid du 'grub pa'i gsungs pa'i dpal khro bo rtum po'i rgyud las//ngo bo nyid kyi rim par phye ba ste/brgyad pa'o////
86b8(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das ma thabs dang shes rab kyi nga rgyal ji ltar sgom par bya//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/mi mo mngon byas rnal 'byor pas//phan tshun 
87a1(rgyud): //de yis lta ba mchog//lus ni drang por mnyam gnas nas//rtse gcig yid ni bsam gtan bya//sku bzhi yi ni rang bzhin phyir//tha dad med pa'i yid kyis so//slar yang byang chub dbyer med pas/
87a2(rgyud): thabs dang shes rab nyid du 'dod//mchi ba nyid ni chos su brjod//longs spyod rdzogs pa bar srid dang//'gro ba drug gzugs sprul pa dang//'dod pa'i longs spyod bde chen po//sku bzhi yi ni rang bzhin
87a3(rgyud): skyes bu la ni khams gsum pa//skyes bu sku bzhi'i rang bzhin phyir//bud med gzugs la khams gsum mo//sku bzhi yi ni rang bzhi las//skyes bu 'dir ni sangs rgyas yin//bud med shes rab phar phyin nyid//
87a4(rgyud): phyogs rnams kun du rnam par gnas//de nyid nga rgyal byas pa yis slar yang bdag la dngos grub po//khro bo rtum po'i tshul nyid du//rang nyid gzugs kyis yang dag gnas//'dod mogs rtum
87a5(rgyud): po'i gzugs sgoms nas//bdag la dngos grub rnam kun du//de nyid gsum rig dus ring du//'di ni gus pa dang bcas sgom//las kun yongs su spang byas nas//bla ma sten pa de nyid mchog//
87a6(rgyud): ji srid dngos grub ma thob par//bde ba'i sems nyid gcig pu gnas//gang tshe rnal 'byor dngos grub thob//sdig pa dang bral dri med 'gyur//rlung dang sems kyi 'dzum 'dangs ni//'jig rten ba yis mthong ba
87a7(rgyud): med//kun mkhyen kun la khyab pa po//nyon mongs kun las rnam par 'das//de la nad dang rgas pa med//de la mchi ba yod min te//de la dug gi mda' ba med//chus min mes kyang tshig pa med//mtshon
87a8(rgyud): gyis ma yin/dgra yis ni//dbang du nam yang 'gro ba min//yid kyis bsams pa tsam gyis ni//dgos 'dod ma lus 'byung bar 'gyur//skad cig de la 'bad med par//yid bzhin nor bu dangs 'tshungs 'gyur//
87b1(rgyud): ma lus 'jig rten khams rnamasu//gang dang gang du de bzhin gshegs//de ni gzhal yas khang 'gro ste//der ni 'dod kun de rnams kyis//gzugs dang lang mtsho rab brgyan pa'i//lha'i bu mo dga' ma der//
87b2(rgyud): ji snyed mtho ris skar ma rnams//the tsom med pa nyid du 'gyur//tshangs dang khyab 'jug dbang phyug gang //brgya byin yan lag med swogs gang //mngal 'jug 'gyur zhing 'gro drug tu//gnas pa'i srog chags
87b3(rgyud): kun kyang //rnal 'byor dngos po ji lta bzhin//de bzhin rnal 'byor mas kyang ngo//skyes bu rdo rje 'dzin pa ltar//bud med rdo rje btsun mo'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das 
87b4(rgyud): lus 'di la thabs dang shes rab kyi sbyor ba las/bde ba chen po ji ltar skyes pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rkyang ma shes rab rang bzhin te//g.yon pa yi ni rtse ru grags//
87b5(rgyud): ro ma thabs kyi rang bzhin te//g.yas pa la ni yang dag gnas//rkyang ma ro ma'i dbus dag la//kun d+har ma ni rab tu gsungs//gang tshe a b+ha d+hutir rlung //khu ba dang ni ro gcig 'gyur//rdo rjes 
87b6(rgyud): mgo bos 'tshams su 'gro//bud med b+ha ga'i ran tra las//thabs dang shes rab mnyam sbyor bas//lte bar gnas pa'i tsan tra li//mar me ltar 'bar de yis ni//khu ba'i khu ba mchog la ni//de las skyes pa'i
87b7(rgyud): bde ba la//cung zad cung zad rab sbyor las//de yang chen po cher sbyor las//de yang dngos po'i rang bzhin las//gang gis de'i bde ba 'phel//rtag tu goms pa'i sbyor ba las//de ni skye ba 'di nyid la//dpaldan
87b8(rgyud): rtum po nyid du 'gyur//zhes ni dpa'o gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa'i dpal khro bo rtum po'i rgyud las/bsam gtan gyi rim par phye ba ste dgu pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol pa/
88a1(rgyud): //ci ste bcom ldan 'das sgrub pa pos//khro chen rtum po'i go 'phang ni//bud med dang blal sgrub nus sam//yon tan sgrub par nus ma yin//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//lha mo sgrub par nus
88a2(rgyud): pa ma yin no//bcom ldan 'das mas gsol pa//bcom ldan 'das ci'i phyir bde ba ster ba med par mi nus//bcom ldan 'das kyis bga' stsal pa//e ma bde ba skyes tsam gyis//byang chub mchog ni
88a3(rgyud): thob byed pa'i//bde ba khyad 'phags 'byung ba las//de nyid thob kyis de gzhan med//de med de'i bya ba yang//de nyid med par skyes pa yin//byed pa bud med dang sbyor ba//gzhan du nam yang ma yin no//sgyu ma'i
88a4(rgyud): de nyid thams cad la//bud med sgyu ma'i de nyid spangs//de'i de nyid las 'das gang//de las dngos grub lhag 'byung med//de phyir bud med dang bral 'di//nam yang bya ba ma yin no//de nyid
88a5(rgyud): gal te sdug bsngal 'gyur//'chi dang mchi ba'i 'jigs pa yang//de rnams thams cad bzod bya yis//'dir ni bud med spang mi bya//gang 'dir bud med bde de nyid//kun nas sangs rgyas ster
88a6(rgyud): ba po//g.yo dang spobs can 'tsher ngo bral//rtag tu 'dod pa'i dpung gnyen no//rtag tu 'dod pa'i dpang gnyen no//dngos grub ster ba 'di nyid ni//dngos po kun gyis sten par bya//bud med tshul ni cig
88a7(rgyud): brjod pa//dga' ba'i phyir ni mchi ba 'ang byed//bdag po dang ni bral gyur na na//'dod pa mchog ni ci zhig smra//de phyir kun gyis bud med ltar//rnam pa kun du rab tu rtag//shing dang rdo la sogs rnams dang/
88a8(rgyud): yid kyis yongasu rtag pa 'ang bya//skyes pa bud med lha mo dang//bud med kyi ni skyes pa lha//rdo rje pad ma rab sbyor bas//phan tshun du ni mchod bya ba//mchod par bya ba gzhan du min//rang nyid byin gyis rlab pa de//
88b1(rgyud): de phyir rnal 'byor rtse ldan pas//dkyil 'khor mdun du byas nas ni//de la nye zhugs bud med ni//shes rab phar phyin rnam pa la//rtag tu me tog gis mchod cing//de bzhin spos dang mar me spogs//
88b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor lnga ni rab sbyor bas//phyi nas phyag ni btsal bar bya//de nas g.yasu skor bar bya//rtum mo la ni mchod par bya//gus pa dang bcas sems kyis ni//de bzhin skyes bus bud med mchog/de nyid mchod 
88b3(rgyud): pa'i cho ga ni//rgyal bas phan tshun bya bar gsungs//bud med smad par yod mi bya//de nyid 'dod pa'ang spang mi bya//snyan pa'i tshig gis smra bya zhing//rjes su 'thun par sbyin par bya/dngos po kun gyis phyag byas te//
88b4(rgyud): ji bzhin bdug can shes mi bya//gang du'ang bud med mi spong ba//'di ni sangs rgyas gsung du thos//gang du 'ang khyod kyis gzhan byas nas//de'i sdig pas dmyal bar 'gro//bud med dang bral ci byas kyang//
88b5(rgyud): gzhan du 'chi 'gyur dngos grub min//khro bo rtum po'i sgrub thabs ni//dka' thub kyis ni 'grub mi 'gyur//yid ni dri med ma byas bar//'bras med gti mug dra bas bcings//'dod ldan 
88b6(rgyud): 'dod pa spong byed pa//rdzun gyis mtsho bar skye 'gyur te//rdzun gyis gang mtsho sdig pa de//sdig pas dmyal bar 'gro bar 'gyur//mchi ba'i dus ni thob pa na//rdzun gyis mtsho bar the tsom med//de phyir sgrub bya 
88b7(rgyud): sgrub par bya//rgyal bas bdag nyid 'dod pas so//'dod yon lnga po de spangs nas//bdag nyid dka' thub gdung mi bya//gzugs med pa ni lta bya zhing//sgra yang de bzhin mnyan par bya//dri ni snum par bya ba ste//re li mchog 
88b8(rgyud): ni bza' bar bya//reg bya ba reg par bya ba ste//'dod pa lnga po sten par bya//khro bo rtum po de mchog ni//myur du sangs rgyas mchog 'gyur ro//gzhan la slu ba med 'gyur cing//gti mug mchog tu med par bya//mi'i lang mtsho 
89a1(rgyud): /thob pa la//mi mo'i bde ba mi spyod na//chen po mchog tu zad byas nas//lta ba'ang zas su med par byas//'dod can mi rtag rten byas nas//'dod pa tsam gyis dpung gnyen gyis//btsun mo'i skye gnas
89a2(rgyud): yang dag sten//khro bo rtum po'i go 'phangs thob//gnyid ni ji ltar spang bar bya//bza' ba dang bgo ba nyid//'jig rten ma dang spang ba'i phyir//lha mo sgyu 'phrul sras mkhan mas//pho brang 'khor gyi btsun mo ni//
89a3(rgyud): stong phrag brgyad khri spangs nas slar//na ra dza na'i 'gram gshegs nas//sangs rgyas dngos grub rab gsungs nas//gang du bdud ni 'dul byed pa//de nyid gzhan don kho na yin//gang zhig btsun mo'i
89a4(rgyud): nang sangs rgyas//gong bu dang bcas bde ldan grub//rdo rje pad ma mnyam sbyor bas//gang phyir bde ba las thob po//bde ba las thob sangs rgyas ni//bde ba bud med bral dang min//
89a5(rgyud): gang du sbyor bral bya ba ni//'jig rten nyams pa spang phyir ro//'jig rten gang gis gang 'dul ba//de dang de yis sangs rgyas 'gyur//rgyal ba'i sgyu ma'i gang nyid kyis//de dang de nyid
89a6(rgyud): tshul gyis so//mdo sde chos mngon thams cad dang //bud med rnams ni smon 'dzad pas//sna tshogs slob ma'i snod phyir bshad//de kho na nyid ni sbas nas gsungs//phung po lnga dang rnam bral ba'i//mya ngan
89a7(rgyud): 'das la lta bar bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das ma shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin mas gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das lha mo sgyu 'phrul gyi sras gang lags/bu yang gang lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis
89a8(rgyud): bka' stsal pa//lha mo sgyu 'phrul sras nyid kyang //khro bo rtum po bdag yin no//sa mtsho khyod nyid bcom ldan 'das//shes rab phar phyin bdag nyid ma//ji snyed pa'i bud med kun//khyod ni tshul nyid 'dir 'dod//
89b1(rgyud): skyes bu bdag gi gzugs nyid de//de bzhin kun du yongasu grags//'dir ni rang bzhin gnyis yin no//'gro ba thabs shes bdag nyid do//de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das ci'i phyir/
89b2(rgyud): nyan thos laswogs pa rnams bud med sun 'byin par byed//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//gang zhig 'dod pa'i khams gnas pa'i//nyan thos laswogs grags kun gyis//bud med la ltos kun 
89b3(rgyud): ster ba'i//thar pa'i lam ni mi shes so//gang na nye bar gnas gyur pa'i//gur gum laswogs rnyed sla na//de la rin ni thob mi 'gyur//gang du rings nas rin chen nyid//thog med mi shes sbyor ba yis//
89b4(rgyud): dad pa dman pa'i skye bo 'di//de nyid la ni sems mi 'jug//bdag gi 'di ni rab tu sbas//rtsod pa'i dus la de nyid 'di//sems can re re ba grangs pa yi//bye ba'i nang du g+ha zhig kyang//
89b5(rgyud): dad dang brtson pa'i dpung gnyen can//kun nas de phyir gsungs pa ni//byang chub myur du rab sgrub phyir//zhes pa ni dpa'o gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa'i//dpal khro bo rtum po chen 
89b6(rgyud): po'i rgyud las//mi mo rab tu bsngags pa'i rim par phye bste//bcu pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol ba//bcom ldan 'das khyod ci ste/chags pa dang 
89b7(rgyud): ba 'am/chags pa dang bral ba lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/bdag ni kun de kun la'ang khyab//kun byed kun gyi 'jig pa po//kun gyi gzugs 'dzin sangs rgyas te//byed po 'joms 
89b8(rgyud): po khyab bdag bde//gang dang gang gi gzugs kyis ni//sems can gdul byar 'di nyid 'jug//de dang de nyid tshul phyis ni//'jig rten don 'gyur bdag gnas so//g+ha zhig sangs rgyas grub thob 'ga'//
90a1(rgyud): la lar chos dang dge 'dun no//la lar yid dgas la lar phyugs//la lar dmyal ba'i gzugs can no//la lar lha dang lha ma yin//la lar mi'i gzugs can no//la ltar rtan pa'i gzugs can no//
90a2(rgyud): sna tshogs gzugs can the tsom med//bdag ni skyes pa bud med dang //ma ning gzugasu'ang la lar ro//la lar chags can la lar sdang //la lar rmongs shing la lar gtsang //la lar mi gtsang gzugasu nga //sems 
90a3(rgyud): kyi gzugs kyis bdag gnaso//bdag ni semasu lta bar bya//gzhan du cung zad yod min te//dngos po dngos med rab dbye ba//nga ni bskyed bya bskyed myed dang //bdag gi bgegs dang grub pa dang //kun gyi gzugs 
90a4(rgyud): kyis yang dag gnas//nga ni skye dang mchi ba 'ang nga //nga ni na dang rgas pa'ang nga //nga ni bsod nams sdig pa 'ang nga //de'i las kyi 'bras nyid nga //'gro kun rang bzhin sangs rgyas nyid//kun du bdag gi 
90a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas 'di//rnal 'byor de nyid sems dpa' bos//ro mnyam rnam par shes par bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das mas gsol pa//bcom ldan 'das ci ste khyod kyi gzugs nyid 
90a6(rgyud): 'di lta bu yin nam//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/ji ltar khyod kyi gzugs nyid kyang //rnam pa de ltar kun nas gsungs//rtan dang g.yo ba'i 'gro kun la//'dir ni khyod kyis khyab pa po//
90a7(rgyud): zhes pa ni dpa'o gcig pa nyid du gsungs pa'i dpal khro bo rtum po chen po'i rgyud las//sna tshogs pa'i rim par phye ba ste bcu gcig pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das las gsol ba//
90a8(rgyud): sngags pa rnams kyis sgrub pa'i thabs//zhi dang rgyas pa de nyid gsungs//dbang dang dgug pa'i rab sbyor dang //bsad dang skrad pa la sogs dang //dug sel ba dang nad zhi dang //me dang ral gri swogs rengs dang //
90b1(rgyud): g.yul du rnam par rgyal ba dang//mkhas pa yi ni mchog nyid dang//gnod sbyin mo sgrub dran dang ni//pho nya 'byung swogs sgrub thabs dang//dpag med rnam shes nus pa rnams//bdag la nges par gtso bos 
90b2(rgyud): gsungs//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//kye khyod la ci zhig sbyin par bya//zhes pa dang sgrub pa pos smra bar bya ste//sangs rgyas nyid rtsol cig ceso//de nas bcom ldan 'das kis 
90b3(rgyud): de'i lus la rab tu zhugaso//rab tu zhugs pa tsam gyis lo brgyad gnyis kyi rnam pa can/mngon par shes pa drug pa/sa bcu gsum gyi dbang phyug lha'i gzhal yas khang na spyod pa can gyi/lha'i bu mo'i 
90b4(rgyud): tshogs brgyad stong gis brgyan pa//'dod pa'i gzugs can thams cad mkhyen pa/bcom ldan 'das ji lta ba bzhin du 'gyuro//yang ral gri dang/mig sman dang/ri lu dang rkang 
90b5(rgyud): 'gyogs dang/rkang pa'i byug pa dang/rgyal srid dang/'dod pa la longs spyod pa'i dbang phyug dang/rig pa dang nor can dang/rig pa la mkhas pa dang/gnod sbyin dang/gnod sbyin mo 
90b6(rgyud): dang/bcud len dang reg rdo dang/khams smra ba ji ltar mngon par 'dod pa bzhin zhu bar byas te/de thams cad bcom ldan 'das kyis ster bar byed do//yang na dpa'o gcig pa'i ras gris la/sngan ma 
90b7(rgyud): bzhin du bsgrub par bya'o//de la dpa'o gcig pa'i ri mo ni mi g.yo ba nag po zhe sdang rdo rje mas 'khyud pa'o//mi g.yo ba dkar po gti mug rdo rje mas so//mi g.yo ba ser po phra ma rdo rje mas so//mi g.yo pa dmar 
90b8(rgyud): po la 'dod chags rdo rje mas so//mi g.yob ljang khu la phra dog rdo rje mas 'khyud pa bri bar bya'o//yang na shes rab dang bral ba'i bcom ldan 'das cig pu bri bar bya'o//yang na ri mo la bcom ldan 'das ma lnga po 
91a1(rgyud): @#//rnams kyis dbul ba cig tu byas la/der bdag nyid de'i bdag po'i gzugasu bsams la/sngon bzhin du bsgrub par bya'o//yang na rang gi bud med lha mo'i gzugasu bsams nas//sgrub par bya'o//sgrub 
91a2(rgyud): par byed na sangs rgyas nyid kyang ster bar byed na/dngos grub gzhan smos ci rgos /yang na zhabs g.yon rkyang ba la ral gri dang zhags pa rnam pas sgrub par bya'o//yang na sems dpa'i dkyil dkrungs ral gri dang zhigs

121a1(rgyud): //'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi gnyis su med pa'i mnyam pa nyid rnam par rgyal po zhes bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po ston to//rdo rje spyod pa 
121a2(rgyud): mngon par brtson pa zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyang /bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de nyid kyi phyogs bcu rnams su de bzhin no//de bzhin du yon tan khyad du 'phags pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang /de bzhin du yon
121a3(rgyud): tan dang ldan pa'i dkyil 'khor dang /thabs gcig tu phyogs bcur bzhugs so//'di lta ste yang stong gsum gyi stong chen po zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnams/mkha' snang gi 'od zer gyis g.yogs par gyur to/
121a4(rgyud): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi lhan cig pa dang /'thun tsa mi yang ma bzod pa'i 'od zer du byas pa de lta kho nar de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi 'od/rdo rje sems dpa'i 'od kyi mog mog por 
121a5(rgyud): gyur nas kun nas gnas so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /phyag na rdo rje la byed chub sems dpa' dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad mchod pa'i sprin gyis 
121a6(rgyud): yang dag par mchod te/kun du bzang po zhes bya bar bskyed te/cang mi gsung ba'i rnam par gnas par gyur to//de nas kun du bzang po rdo rje 'chang chen po byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyang rang gi gzugs 
121a7(rgyud): la gzhag nas/ye shes kyi rigs de bskyed pa gsang chen dang bcas pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi gsang ba bsal bar byed pa'i rgyu/rang gi ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/ye shes kyi rigs rang gi 
121a8(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin ston to//logs bzhi pa la zur bzhi ba/rin chen bzhi dang yang dag ldan//'o ma zho ni ku ni kun bkram par//byas nas bsam gtan yang dag bsam//de'i dbus su pad ma che//shel dang zla ba mnyam pa'i 'od//
121b1(rgyud): bsal ba'i dkyil 'khor yang dag ldan//dkyil 'khor rgyun du bsgom par bya//'bar ba 'dus pa de rnamasu //sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam par sgom//kun nas yongs su zlum po ni//khru ni nyi shu bsam par bya//
121b2(rgyud): cho ga shes pa de dbus su//rang gzugs dge ba sgom par bya//bdag dang gzhan gyir rnam rtog par//dor ba'i bsam gtan yang dag brtsam//ye shes rigs kyi cho ga yis//gsang sngags sems la rjes dran bya//
121b3(rgyud): oM sarba ta thA g+ha ta dz+nyA na ku la saM b+ha wa ad mo ko hoM/'di yis phyag rgya mnyam par 'dus//sdig pa thams cad yang dag par dor bar bya'o//'od zer stong phrag bcu par ni//dang por bsams nas dri med 'byung /
121b4(rgyud): ye shes rigs kyi cho ga yi//rig pa'i rgyal mo rnam par sgom//'od dkar phra mo 'bar bar ni//de ni kun du rnam par dgod//thams cad de nyid du bya ste sgom par bya//bcom ldan 'das 
121b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i dbang skur ba 'di gsungaso//rang lus shin du rtan byas nas//'od kyi tshul gyis cho ga rnams//sangs rgyas gzugs ni 'dus pas ni//
121b6(rgyud): mtshon pas rdor sems bskyed paso//cho ga shes pas rang brtsams ni//de'i dngos grub rnyed mi dka'//rang gi gzugs ni mi g.yo bar//byas na rdor sems cho ga rnams//ji srid namakha' dgongs gyur ba//cho ga shes 
121b7(rgyud): pas sbyor bar bya//sangs rgyas gzugs dang rang gzugs shing //byas nas kun sbyor mtshan nyid kyis//rnal 'byor bsam gtan dus su ni//rdzogs byas de la rab tu zhugs//rang gi gzugs dang sangs rgyas gzugs/
121b8(rgyud): ji ltar gnas ltar blo dang ldan//ji ltar rigs par sbyor ba ni//rdo rje sems dpa' sgom par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi rje//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungaso//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrugs pa'i 'od//
123a1(rgyud): //rang lus sgom bya rnal 'byor pa//de nyid rdo rje'i tshul gyis ni//rdo rje dbyer med de sgom mo//'od lnga shin tu phyung nas ni//byang chub sems ni rnam par sgom//snying khar sangs rgyas kun gyis mchod par bya//
123a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi rje//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungs so//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrugs pa'i 'od//dkyil 'khor lnga ni bsam par bya//sangs rgyas gzugs ni 'dus pas ni//bgang ste sngags 
123a3(rgyud): ni sgom pa las//sangs rgyas gzugs rnyan ltar byas na//sgom pa'i bdag nyid can sgom bya//rang gi gzugs ni der bsam bya//rtog pa bskyed pa'i cho ga las//thugs la byang chub sems kyang ni//sgom bya 
123a4(rgyud): de la rnal 'byor skyes//rdo rje sems dpas rnam par brgyan//cho ga shes par dbyung bar bya//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' la//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam sgom bya//cho ga shes pas sangs rgyas 
123a5(rgyud): gzugs//yi ge phra mo rnam par sgom//yig rnams su'ang thams cad du//sangs rgyas gzugs ni snam sgom bya//de rnams la ni yi ge gnyis//rang gzugs rnam par sa rnam sgom las//nyi ma'i 
123a6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor tshul gyi ni//mkhas pa rang gzugs sgom par bya//ji srid nam mkha'i mthongs gyur pa//zhing 'di khyab par g.yog par bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyi ni/rdo rje rnal 'byor rnam sgom bya//sbyor ba 
123a7(rgyud): rnams su//sangs rgyas gzugs ni dang rnam sgom bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor/tshul gyis ni//'jig rten khams 'dir sbyor bar bya//ji srid nam mkha' mgyongs gyur pa//zhing 'di khyad pa g.yogs par bya//bral ba'i dkyil 
123a8(rgyud): 'khor tshul gyis ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor sgom pa las//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor tshil gyis ni//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam sgom bya//sangs rgyas gzugs ni ni rnam sgom pas//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor rnam sgom pa//zla ba'i 
123b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam sgom pa//chos kyi rgyal/pad ma'i rnal 'byor cho ga yis//sgom pa thams cad rtag par bya//sgom pa thams cad yongs 'dzin las//gzhan du mchod bya min pa nyid/
123b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam bsgom nas/shin du rtan par rtan pa'i sems//byang chub sems ni yang dag bskyed//rgyun du bdag nyid rnam sgom bya//'di ni rdo rje'i tshul zhi ba//rnon po zhi dang mnyam pa'i 'od//rdo rje sems 
123b3(rgyud): dpa' rnam sgom pas//cho ga shes par dgang bar bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos gsungs so//de nas kun du bzang po rdo rje ma chad chen pos byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi thugs las byung ngo//
123b4(rgyud): de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po de rnams namakha'i khams thaMs cad bkang ste/byang chub sems dpa' thamasacad mchod pa'i srin rnams kyis yang dag par mchod te/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang 
123b5(rgyud): chen po la 'dud pa dang/ldan par kun du gnas par gyur ba /de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad slar yang 'dus par gyur nas//kun du bzang po byang chub sems dpa' chenapo la 
123b6(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad mchod pa'i srin chen po rnams kyis/yang dag bar mchod te/phyag 'tshal nas/byang chub kyi sems rdo rje la rab tu zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
123b7(rgyud): cad kyi 'od zer gyis kun du gang ste/'od zer de rnams de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi 'byung gnas yang dag par skul bar gyur to//de nas kun du bzang po rdo rje 'chang chen po de bzhin gshegs pa tham
123b8(rgyud): sa cad bstan par 'dzad nas/ye shes kyi rigs 'di rgyas par byed pa'i rgyu/rang gi gzugs rnam par snang 'dzad kyi gzugs su byin gyis rlabs nas/rang gi gzugs kyi rnam pa'i 'od zer rnams phyung ste/
124a1(rgyud): //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dam tshig gi snying po ye shes rigs kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u gsungaso////de nyid thams cad byed bzhin pa'i//bsam gtan kun sbyor mtshan nyid kyis//cho ga
124a2(rgyud): shes pas rab tu rtsam//'di ni de nyid tshul dag pa'o//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor mchog gyur pa//khru ni stong phrag bsam par bya//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//wa yig de la sgom par bya//kun nas yongs
124a3(rgyud): zlum por ni//'the chung 'tho tsam bsam par bya//'bar ba de la cho ga shes//kha dog sna tshogs rnam par sgom//der yang 'bar ba yang dag skyes//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam sgom bya//phyogs rnams kun du oM
124a4(rgyud): yig ni//kha dog sna tshogs rnams bsam bya//rnam snang la sogs rnam sgom pas//cho ga shes par bskyed par bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnang par//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor sgom par bya//
124a5(rgyud): bdag nyid de la bsam pa ni//dga' ba bskyed pa'i cho ga las//snying gi dbus son phra mo ni//dri med zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la//sngags yig phra mo'i gzugs rnams kyis//de de sems la
124a6(rgyud): bsam bar bya//oM yig 'od zer yang dag sbyor//'bar ba'i phreng ba mnyam pa'i 'od//sangs rgyas gzugs swogs mtshan nyid kyis//cho ga shes pas dbyung bar bya//sangs rgyas gzugs rnams thams cad du//
124a7(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor sgom pa ni//sangs rgyas bskyed pa'i cho ga las/rang gi gzugasu de la bya'o//rdo rje thugasu cho ga las//nyi ma lnga ni brjod bya ba//sngags pas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor can//bral ba ru ni rnam bar
124a8(rgyud): sgom//'od zer 'bar bar mnyam pa'i 'od//sangs rgas gzugasu rnam par sgom//'jig rten khams rnams rim par 'das//slar yang de la rab zhugs bya//khru ba'i tshad las 'das pa yi//zla ba'i
124b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor sgom pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa'i yang dag bskyed//rang gi gzugs nyid sgom par bya//bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos de skad ces gsungs so//ye shes kyi rigs rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//
124b2(rgyud): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//rang gi gzugs ni sgom bya dge//zug rngu rnam snang gzhag bya zhes//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam sgom bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//cho ga shes 
124b3(rgyud): pas gzugs bcu drug//kha dog bzhir ni rnam bsam bya//snying kha rnams dang thams cad du//lha rnams de la sgom par bya//zhes bcom ldan 'da'as rdo rje 'chang gis gsungs so//rang rang gzugs las bgegs 
124b4(rgyud): 'joms par//sems la rtan par bsgom par bya//hU~M yig dbugs las byung nas ni//'od zer rnam par rab tu zhug//zhes bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis gsungs so//
124b5(rgyud): rang gzugs la sogs 'tshungs ba'i 'od//lha ru skad cig de 'gyur//yi ge rnams kyi tshang kyi ni//wa'i gzugs ni rnam sgom bya//sngags pa'i dbus su yi ge nyid//phyi nas las ni kun du brtsam//
124b6(rgyud): sngags pa'i dpral ba'i steng nas ni//ji srid lte ba zhes par dran//gnyis su med pa mnyam pa nyid//rnam par rgyal ba'i rtog pa chen po las//rnam par dgod pa'i le'u dang po rdzogs so////shing rta'i 'khor 
124b7(rgyud): lo'i tshad du ni//pad ma chen po rnam sgom bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//pad ma'i chu ba de rnams sgom//las kyi rdo rje yang dag bskyed//rang gzugs de la sgom par bya//las kyi rdo rje rab sgoms 
124b8(rgyud): pas//rdo rje sems dpa' mnyam par 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//'o ma dang zho byas nas ni//dbus su pad ma rnam par sgom//rang gzugs sangs rgyas ltar byas nas//
125a1(rgyud): //mkhas pas rang gzugs rnam sgom bya//nam mkha'i khams ni kun bkang ste//slar yang de nyid sbyar byas te//'o ma dang zho mnyam byas nas//sna tshogs kha dog mnyam pa'i 'od//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//
125a2(rgyud): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor sgom pa ni//rang gzugs sangs rgyas 'tshungs pa ni//cho ga shes par dbyung bar bya//rang gi gzugs dang thams cad du//lha la swogs pa rnam sgom byed//khru gang tsam las 'das par ni//
125a3(rgyud): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khaur sgom pa ni//bgegs rnams 'joms par ltar byas nas//'jig rten khamasu 'di spro bya//rang gi phyag rgya snying kha ru//bsams nas nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus//sangs rgyas rnam par cho ga shes//
125a4(rgyud): 'od kyi dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//de dbus yi ge byin nas ni//'jig rten khamasu 'di spo bya//gdul ba la swogs can byas nas//slar yang de nyid la sbyar bya//khru gang tsam las 'dasapar ni//zla ba'i 
125a5(rgyud): dkyil 'khor sgom par bya//rdo rje ral gri yang dag bskyed//'jig rten khams ni yangs dgrod bya//pad ma dkar po'i 'od 'bar dang //byang chub sems kyis rnam par rgyan//dbus su 
125a6(rgyud): rang gi lha'i gzugs//bzho gsu btsun mo dgod par bya//cho ga shes pas de ltar bzung //rang gi rdo rje de la bzhugs//'jig rten khams ni thams cad du//sangs rgyas de nyid kyis mchod bya//zhes bcom 
125a7(rgyud): ldan 'das kun tu bzang pos gsungs so//ji srid yang dag thob sbyor skyes//sngags pas slar ni bsdu bar bya//'tho gang tsam las 'das par ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor sgom par ni//rang gzugs shin tu rtan byas 
125a8(rgyud): nas//lha la sogs pa rnam par sgom//snying khar sngags kyi yig byin nas//sangs rgyas kun ni mchod par bya//mtho bdun tsam las 'das par ni//dkyil 'khor bzhi ni sgom par bya//lha'i gzugs kyis dbus 
125b1(rgyud): gnas par//snying khar de la sgom par bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje dam tshig gis gsungaso//rang gzugs can zhes bya bar ni//byas nas sangs rgyas thams cad sgom//thugs rje nyid dang thams cad du//
125b2(rgyud): mkhas par rang gzugs sgom par bya//de ru snying po'i tshul gyis ni//sems dpa'i rnam 'byor rnam par sgom//rgyang grags tsam las 'das par ni//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam sgom bya//gzugs kyi namakha' 
125b3(rgyud): dgongs par ni//snying po rnams las yang dag bskyed//rgyang grags tsam las 'a das par ni//bsal ba'i dkyil 'khor sgom par ni//rang gi rnal 'byor legs brtan bya//sangs rgyas yang dag rnal 'byor sgom//
125b4(rgyud): rgyang grags tsam las 'dasapar ni//byang chub sems ni rnam par sgom//lha'i yang dag bskyed nas ni//sngags kyi yi ge rnam par sgom//mtho gang tsam las 'das par ni//pad ma'i tshul du rnam par sgom/
125b5(rgyud): rang lus dri ma med byas nas//bsal ba'i dkyil 'kho sgom pa'o//de dbus legs par brtan byas nas//rang gzugs deng nas dang por sgom//'od dkar 'bar ba'i phreng ba swogs//pad ma lnga po rnam 
125b6(rgyud): sgom bya//de'i 'bras bu rang gzugs dang //gzhogasu btsun mo dgod par bya//rang gi khu ba deng swogs 'byung //cho ga shes pas spro bar bya//sangs rgyas gzugs ni nam mkha'i khams//kun tu gang bsgom par ni//
125b7(rgyud): rang gzugs ral gri ltar byas nas//kun gyi snying khar dgod par bya//rang gzugs sangs rgyas yang dag spyod//mtshan nyid laswogs chog rims kyis//byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha'i khams//yang dag gang bar rnaM 
125b8(rgyud): sgom bya//rang gzugs zhags pa yang dag sbyor//khyab bdag sangs rgyas gzugs dgod bya//ji srid nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor du//cho ga shes pa dbyung bar bya//rang gzugs sang rgyas yang dag 'byor//byang chub 
126a1(rgyud): //sems su rnam sgom mo//sngags kyi yi ge swogs sbyor gyis//de'i rnam pa myur sgom bya//byang chub sems ni yang dag bskyed//'od zer 'bar bas yang dag rgyan//rang gzugs sangs rgyas 'tshungs par 
126a2(rgyud): cho ga shes pas dbyung bar bya//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//spyan ni snying khar rnam par dgod//cho ga shes pas 'od rnam par//rdo rje sems dpa' rab bzhug bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rjes sems dpas
126a3(rgyud): gsungaso//rang gzugs myur ba'i dkyil 'khor du//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//lha'i rnam par yang dag sbyor//sgom bya sbyor ba gzhan min no//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbusu ni//rdo rje sems dpa' yang
126a4(rgyud): bskyed//lcags kyu'i rnam ba zhags pa swogs//byas nas slob ma la rab zhug bya//rdo rje sems dpa' yangadag bskyed//rang gzugs snying khar rnam dgod bya//dgod par byas nas lag swogs kyis//
126a5(rgyud): de nas grangs ni yang dag brtsam//bzlas pa swogs sam btung bya ba//blo ldan des ni de bya'o//sgrub po rnams kyis nam zlas bya//gzhan du yang dag grub min nyid//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin
126a6(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi dam tshig gis de skad ces gsungs so//rang gi gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//dubasu rang gi gzugs sgo mo//mthar yang dgra dang bgegs 'joms par//snying po de ru sgom par bya//
126a7(rgyud): 'di rnams kyi ni cho ga yis//sgom pa la swogs cho ga'i rim//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//rig pa'i rgyal po rnam sgom bya//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrugs 'bar ba'i//khro bo'i gzugasu sgom par bya//kha dog
126a8(rgyud): gang de kho nar brjod//de nyid mthar thug de nyid brjod//de nyid de yis bsam par bya//gzhan du grub pa min pa nyid//bcom ldan 'das da bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi kun du bzang pos de skad ces gsungs so
126b1(rgyud): rang gzugs dkyil 'khor byas nas ni//dbus su 'bar ba mnyam pa'i 'od//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad du//de nyid shes pa'i snying khar dgod//de la bdag nyid yang dag bskyed//de'i rang bzhin der rtsam bya/
126b2(rgyud): rang gzugs pad ma'i dbus gnas par//snang byed la sogs mnyam pa'i 'od//snying khar yi ge sgom byas nas//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam sgom bya//rdo rje sems dpa'i 'dus pa las//sangs rgyas gzugasu rnam

126b4(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsungaso//thams cad de bzhin nyid ces bya ba ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbusu ni//gzugs ni rnam snang 'dzad bsams nas//snying khar sngags ni rnam sgom bya//
126b5(rgyud): de nyid sngagasu rjesu dran//rig pa'i tshul gyi cho ga ni//slob dpon yid ni dga' ba ru//cho ga shes bar dbyung bar bya//snying kha rnams dang thams cad du//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor sgom pa 'o//
126b6(rgyud): rang gi gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//de la ral gri la sogs sgom//cho ga shes pas yang dag bskyed//'bar ba lnga ni rnam sgom bya//gzugs dang kha dog rnam par shes//'jig rten khamasu 'di spro bya//bcom ldan 
126b7(rgyud): 'das de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyis de skad ces gsungaso//gnyis su med ba mnyam pa nyid rnam par rtog pa'i rgyal ba'i rgyal po chen po las//ye shes rigs kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
126b8(rgyud): ////de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas te/rnam par snang 'dzad kyi gzugasu byin gyis rlabs nas//de rnams kyang dkyil 
128a1(rgyud): //'khor chen por 'dus pa rnamasu /rang gzugasu gzhag go//sdus pa'i rigs kyi ting 'dzin gyis/le'u rnams bka' stsal par 'dzad/'o ma dang zho bsams nas su /'phags pa'i 
128a2(rgyud): dam tshig bzhi po dang /dbus su sngags pas ud pa la//sgom bya gzhan ni mi bsam mo//ye shes dkyil 'khor yang dag sbyor//de'i steng du rnam sgom bya//khro bo'i rnam pa yang dag bskyed//'od zer yang 
128a3(rgyud): dag sbyor ba sgom //der ni 'od zer 'dus rnams su//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam sgom bya//cho ga shes pas de rang gzugs//dbus su yang dag gzhag sgom bya//bcom ldan 'das dam tshig rdo rjes de skad ces gsungaso//
128a4(rgyud): oM sarba ta thA g+ha ta dz+nya na saM b+ha wa ad mo ko haM//gsang sngags rgyal po 'di yis ni//rang gi rnam rtog yongs dor byed//ra gzugs hUM yig zhes brjod pa//byas nas kun du rnam sgom bya//cho ga
