128a7(rgyud): sngags kyi yi ge thams cad du//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam sgom bya//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rnam sgom pa'i//cho ga shes pas spro bar bya//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sras kyi gtso bos
128a8(rgyud): de skad ces gsungaso//rang gzugs sems dpa'i tshul gyis ni//sems dpa' mtshan mar sgom par bya//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun du ni//cho ga shes pas spro b.rabya//rang gzugs sems dang pa'i tshul gyis ni//
128b1(rgyud): lha'i snying khar sgom par bya//sangs rgyas gzungs der dbyung bar bya//'jig rten khams der spro bar bya//rang gzugs ye shes zhes brjod pa//'bar bas phreng bas rnam par brgyan//byang chub sems su de byung nas//'jig rten
128b2(rgyud): khams su der spro bya//rang gi gzugs zhes bya ba ni//'bar ba'i phreng ba mnyam pa'i 'od//rang gi gzugs kyi 'od zer gyis//zhing 'di khyab par rnam sgom bya//rang gzugs lcags kyu dang bcas nas//
128b3(rgyud): rang gi gzugs kyi lag par dgod//cho ga shes pas bkug nas su//sangs rgyas gzugs la rab bzhugs bya//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rje bos de skad ces gsungs so//rang 
128b4(rgyud): gzugs gzugs kyi tshul gyis ni//gsang sngags yi ge rnam par sgom//rdo rje sems pa rnam sgom pas//cho ga shes pas spro bar bya//byang chub sems su rang gzugs kyis//de nyid sgom pa 
128b5(rgyud): sgom par bya//bgegs ni sngags la yang dag bskyed//'jig rten khams su'ang spro bar bya//mi sdug rtsigs shing 'jigs pa ni//dgegs kyi dgra de nga byung bar bya//byang chub sems
128b6(rgyud): ni de phyung nas//'jig rten 'di spro bar bya//rang gzugs nyi ma'i gzugs gnas par//rnon po 'bar bas rnam par brgyan//las kyi rdo rje shin du 'dzes//cho ga shes pas dbyung bar bya//dang po'i rnal 'byor yang 
128b7(rgyud): dag bskyed//shin du brtan pa brtan pa'i sems//cho ga nye bar mthong ba yis//dang po'i rnal 'byor yag dag brtsam//rang gzugs ud pal ltar bsams nas//sna tshogs 'od zer mnyam par ldan//
128b8(rgyud): lha'i gzugs su yang dag sbyor//de'i steng du rnam sgom bya//ji srid nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor du//sngags pas 'jig rten gsum du spro//sangs rgyas gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//kha dog dkar po shin du 'dzes//
129a1(rgyud): //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//yid dga' ba ni sgom par bya//tho ba'i rnam par yang dag sbyor//cho gas rtag tu sgom par bya//rdo rje sems dpa' swogs gsungs pas ji ltar rigs par //
129a2(rgyud): bsdus nas ni//rang gzugs bsal bar 'bar ba'i 'od//ma mi ki swo gas rnam rgyan pas//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//'jig rten khamasu yang dag spro//rdo rje ye shes yang dag bskyed//khro bo yang 
129a3(rgyud): dag sbyor sgoms pas//rang gzugs 'bar ba 'tshungs pa yis//cho ga shes par spro bar bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnasapar//mkhas pas rang gi gzugs sgom bya//rtse mo lnga'i rdo rje che//
129a4(rgyud): 'jig rten khamasu yang dag spo//khro bo'i gzugasu yang dag skyed//rang gi gzugs kyi yi ge rgyan//sangs rgyas gzugasu rnam sgom bya//cho ga shes pas 'khor lo'i lha//'od zer tshul du rnam
129a5(rgyud): sgom bya//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje'i rang bzhin gis de skad ces gsungaso//rang gzugs 'khor lo'i tshul gyis ni/'bar ba'i phreng bas rnam par rgyan//rang gzugs sangs rgyas
129a6(rgyud): dang 'tshungs pa//cho ga shes pas dbyung bar bya//rang gzugs 'khor lo'i tshul gyis ni//ral gri'i 'od zer gyis rgyan pa//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrugs bsal ba//kun nas rnam par 'phro ba'o//pad ma rol pa'i
129a7(rgyud): snying dbus gnas//bram ze tshangs pa rnam par brgyan//byang chub sems su yang dag bskyed//da la sngags kyi yi ge bkod//snying khar ud pal bsams byas nas//sna tshogs 'od zer rnam par brgyan//cho ga shes
129a8(rgyud): pas byang chub sems//sas nas sngags gsum rnam sgom bya//snying kha'i dbus son phra mo ni//in tra ni la 'tshungs pa'i 'od//ye shes sbyor ba kun bskyed nas//sangs rgyas gzugs su sgom par bya//rang gzugs phra mo dbus
129b1(rgyud): gnas par//sna tshogs 'bar ba mnyam pa'i 'od//snying po yi ge bsams nas ni//rang gzugs de la sgom par bya//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje ye shes kyis de skad ces gsungaso//khru gang tsam las 'das pa'i//phra mo'i
129b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor sgom par ni//rang gzugs yi ger yang byas nas//rdo rje 'bar ba rnam sgom bya//khru gang tsam las 'dasapa ru//ye shes dkyil 'khor sgom par ni//dbus su gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//bsams nas
129b3(rgyud): zlas ba sgo brtsam par bya//khru gang tsam las 'dus par ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor sgom par ni //rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//bsam sa nas zlos pa brtsam par bya//khru gang cam las 'das pa ru//bsams nas zlas
129b4(rgyud): pa brtsam par bya//khru gangs tsam las 'das par ni//nyi ma'i dkyi 'khor sgom par ni//chos kyi dkyil 'khor sgom par ni//bgegs dgrar yang dag bskyed bsams nas//bzlas pa kun du rtsam
129b5(rgyud): par bya//mtho gang tsam las 'das pa ru//ye shes dkyil 'khaur sgom par ni//rnam snang 'dzad kyis gzugs bsams nas//'jig rten thams cad 'dir spro bya//mtho gang tsam lasa'adas pa ru/
129b6(rgyud): pad ma'i dkyil 'khor sgom bar ni//dbus su gzugs ni yang bskyed//gzhogasu sangs rgyas rnam par sgom//rgyang grags tsam las 'das par ni //phra mo dkyil 'khor sgom par ni//dbusu gzugs ni yang
129b7(rgyud): dag bskyed//'jig rten khams 'dir spro bar bya//bcom ldan 'das rna rdo rje can gyis 'di skad ces gsungaso//rgyang grags can las 'das bar ni//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor 'khor sgom par ni//sangs rgyas gzugs ni yang dag
129b8(rgyud): bskyed//gzhogasu btsun mo dgod par bya//btsun mo rnams ni thams cad du//'od zer la ni rnam sgom bya//me tog yid dga' 'di rnams kyi//cho ga shes bas mchod par bya//'thog byed 'di'i sprin laswogs//
130a1(rgyud): //bsams nas sangs rgyas mchod par bya//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje can gyis 'di skad ces gsungaso//cho ga shes pas btsun mo ni//ser po lta bur bsams nas ni//sangs rgyas sras po 'dus par ni//
130a2(rgyud): rnal 'byor bskyed te sgom bar bya//de dbyung ji ltar rab bskyed la//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam sgom bya//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//rigs kyi dbye ba sgom par bya//rgyang grags tsam las 'das par ni//
130a3(rgyud): ye shes phra mor rnam par sgom//rang gzugs gzugs kyi tshul gyis ni//dbusu yang dag gzhag sgom bya//cho ga shes pas rdo rje'i rtser//rnam pa lnga ru rnam sgom bya//dkar po ye shes rigs rab brjod//
130a4(rgyud): nag po sdus pa'i rigs su dran//dmar po pad ma'i rigs zhes bya//ser po 'dod pa'i rigs zhes bya//'dzes pa'i gzugs ni rab bsal bar//rig nas sna tshogs rgyan gyis brgyan//
130a5(rgyud): pad ma rab tu brgyan byas nas//rang gi rdo rje de la bzhug//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas de skad ces gsungaso//de ltar byas pas gang ldan pa//de ltar ye shes rnyed par dka'//rang gzugs 
130a6(rgyud): shin tu rtag byas nas//bsam rtan rtson par rab tu bzhugs//'dzes ldan ma dga' ltas par ni//gzugs dang ldan par de 'gyur ro//btsun mo mthong ba tsam gyis ni//mthong 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//
130a7(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' zhing 'dir ni//yongasu gang bar rnam sgom bya//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rtan byas nas//rdo rje rnam par ltung bar bya//bcom ldan 'das rdo rjes gsum dbyer mi phyed pas de skad ces gsungs so//
130a8(rgyud): gnyisu med pa nyid kyi rnam par rgyal ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las//rigs sdus pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u gsum pa rdzogs s+ho////de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes chos kyi rigs kyi/
130b1(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u bka' stsal to//dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du ni//thams cad du ni de nyid bya//chos kyi dkyil 'khor rnam sgom pa//cho ga shes pas rab tu brtsam//chos kyi kun 'byor mtshan nyid kyis//rnam par
130b2(rgyud): rtog pa yongs dor bya//gsang sngags dbye bar brjod nas ni//thams cad rnal 'byor rnam sgom//oM sarba ta thA g+ha ta d+harma dz+nyA na/saM b+ha wa ad ma ko haM//dam tshig chen po rnal 'byor nyid//cho ga shes pas yang
130b3(rgyud): dag brjod//dngos po thams cad yang dag bskyed//dngos med sgom bya min pa nyid//bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten dbang phyug gis de skad ces gsungs so//blo gros ldan pas pad ma bsam//pad ma ra ga'i
130b4(rgyud): dbang phyug 'tshungs//dkyil 'khor de la bsam bya ba//gzugs rnyan dbus su rnam sgom bya//rig pa'i rgyal po yang dag bskyed//'bar ba'i phreng ba mtshungs pa'i 'od//'bar ba
130b5(rgyud): 'dus pa de rnams su//'jig rten dbang phyug rnam sgom bya//bcom ldan 'das 'jog rten dbang phyug gis deskad ces gsungs so//nam mkha'i khams ni kun tu gang //slar yang de nyid
130b6(rgyud): la 'du bya//rang gi gzugs dang zla dbus gnas//sna tshogs kha dog 'tshungs pa'i 'od//snying khar de rnams sgom byas nas//lce la gsang sngags sgom pa'o//rang gzugs de'i dbus gnas par//
130b7(rgyud): sangs rgyas ye shes rnam par brgyan//'jig rten dbang phyu yang dag bskyed//'jig rten khams 'dir spro bar bya//rig pa'i rgyal po yag dag bskyed//pad ma'i dkyil 'khor dbusu ni//'jig rten dbang
130b8(rgyud): phyug rab sgom nas//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//'jig ten khams su 'di spro bya//bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang pos de skad ces gsungs so//chos kyi dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//rdo rje sems dpa'
141a1(rgyud): /yang dag bskyed//ji ltar rigs pa'i gnas lnga ru//khyab bdag 'jig rten dbang phyug dgod//bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten dbang phyug gis de skad ces gsungs so//snying kha'i dbus son phra mo ni//

141b5(rgyud): gzugs kyis ni phye mar rag/bsal ba'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//ye shes rigs ni zlog par byed//ye shes dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni //sdus pa'i rigs ni rnam ltung zhing //chos

150b2(rgyud): par byas te phyag bya min//rang gzugs su ni rang gzugs kyi//bsams nas de nyid cho ga las//snying khar rdo rje sogs byas nas//yang dag brjod byar de sbyar bya//gal te rdo rje sems dpa' rung //kha cig phyis
150b3(rgyud): ni la dgod na//rang gi byang chub sems smras pas//ma go bo tshal pa bdun du gas//b+ha gar ling ka rab gzhag nas//gsang sngags yang nas yang du zlas//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//rang gzugs
150b4(rgyud): la ni sgom par bya//sangs rgyas slob dpon de nyid 'di//rtag tu srung ba'i khyad par las//gzhan du slob dpon rnams kyis ni//sangs rgyas nyid ni thob mi 'gyur//rang gi gzugs su rnam sgom nas/
150b5(rgyud): de nyid la ni khro mo'i gzugs//byang chub sems ni yang dag bskyed/slob dpon sangs rgyas su sgom bya//sgrub po rnams kyi ye shes 'di//de nyid dam tshig 'byung ba'o//dam tshig la swogs sbyor ba 'dis
150b6(rgyud): sangs rgyas kyi yang 'grub mi 'gyur//dam rgya shin du rnal 'byor gyis//rang gi snying khar gzugs sgom bya//de la rdo rje yang dag bskyed//ji srid 'dod pas sten par bya//dus ltos ltos pa laswogs ldan/
150b7(rgyud): 'di ni dam tshig mchog yin no//ji srid dngos grub sgrub bya ba//rtag tu de yis srung bar bya//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems kyis de skad ces gsungs so//rang gi gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//rang gzugs 
150b8(rgyud): laswogs mtshan nyid kyis//snying khar bdud rtsi brjod pa ni//de'i sngags ni yang dag rtsam//'di yang dam tshig ye shes te//kun du bri ba bsal ba nyid//'dod pa'i dngos grub rtan pa ni//rtul shugs 
151a1(rgyud): @#//brtsom pas srung bar bya//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam sgoms nas//bsgrub bya'i rnam par bsam par bya'o//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bskyed//snying po de la yang dag bskyed/cho ga 'di 
151a2(rgyud): rtag tu/dus gsum du yang sgom par bya/gal te gzhal du bzlas byas sam//sgrub na de ni ma yin nyid//rig ma gzugs dang gzugs swogs can//'bar ba'i phreng bas brgyan byed pa/sems dpa'i
151a3(rgyud): rin chen yang dag bskyed//de'i lag tu'ang rnam sgom bya//bsdus ba yis ni sgom bzhin du//ji srid dngos grub rab thob byed//gzhan du gal te bdag rmongs na//de'i sbyin pa der mi 'gyur//shin du rnam bkra 
151a4(rgyud): dri ma med//ye shes rin chen spyod pa na du//gzhan nas sgrub pa yi ni mchog//sa stengs lhan cig rgyu bar byed//rdor sems bdag nyid dngos grub de//rdo rje sems dpas byin gyis rlabs//
151a5(rgyud): rtag tu rdo rje ye shes chos//go 'phangs su ni sgral bas bgyi//ye shes rdo rje dngos grub nga//bdud rtsi zhes sogs byin gyis brlabs//nam mkha' mchog tu phra ba yi//bzang po ra ni rnal 
151a6(rgyud): 'byor byed//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje mchang chen pos de skad ces gsungs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rjes/slar yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs ye shes kyi gsang sngags
151a7(rgyud): yongs su dag pa de kho na nyid byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'i/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dam tshig bka' stsal to//rdo rje sems dpa' skye sgom pa//thugs kyi rdo rje zhes rab brjod//sems de la ni 
151a8(rgyud): sgoms nas ni//ye shes rdo rje rab zhug bya//rdo rje rab bzhin dag pa'i don//gsung gi rdo rje yongs su rtag//dag pa'i ye shes rnam sgoms pas//sku gsung dag par rnam sgom bya//rdo rje'i byin rlob shin du gsal//
151b1(rgyud): mi byed rdo rje'i sku nyid do//sangs rgyas sku'i rang bzhin nyid//cho ga shes pas byin gyis rlab//rdo rje 'di yis byang chub thob par byed//cho ga shes pas zla ba drug gis sangs rgyas nyid 'di thob par byed//chags skyes 
151b2(rgyud): chos kyis ma gos pas//rdo rje de rnams thams cad 'byung //de ltar de la cho ga byas nas//dngos grub thams cad sdus pa yin//de nas phyag na rdo rjes//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi kun du spyod pa'i//
151b3(rgyud): dam tshig bka' stsal to//mi slu ba'i tshul gyis ni//rdo rje ye shes gsum 'gyur bas//de kun nye bar longs spyod pa//sangs rgyas kun spyod nyid du 'gyur//nam mkha'i khams ni kun bkang ba'i//sangs 
151b4(rgyud): rgyas rnams ni gal te bcom//skye ba 'di nyid sangs rgyas nyid//rdo rje slob dpon sdus pa'o//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//rdo rje'i gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//mkha' khams 
151b5(rgyud): sangs rgyas gzugs kyis ni//yongasu gang bar rnam sgom bya//snying khar sngags ni yang dag bskyed/mnyam par gzhag pas skul bar bya//kun du dges pa'i dam tshig gang //rdo rje 
151b6(rgyud): slob dpon rtag bso bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dpus gnas par//rang gi sngags ni yang dag bskyed //mkha' khams gzugs kyis yang dag bskyed//snying khar thams cad rnam sgom bya//de nyid de ni byed 
151b7(rgyud): bzhin pas//sangs rgyas bka' nyid go 'phangs gnas//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//rang gi snga gas ni yang dag bskyed/cho ga shes pas gzugs rnam par//gnas lnga rnamasu sgom par bya//
151b8(rgyud): byang chub sems ni yang dag bskyed/de kun skul bya rtul shugs can//chos kyi dkyil 'khor dbus su ni //rang gi sngags ni yang ngag bskyed//spyod pan tu de rnam sgoms nas//mchod par bya zhing sgom par bya//sangs rgyas

153a3(rgyud): de nas de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad bsdus pas byon nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs dang /ye shes kyi mchod pa'i srin gyi bkod pas/bcom ldan 'das phyag
153a4(rgyud): na rdo rje la yang dag par mchod de//'di skad ces gsol to//dngos grub thams cad byed bsam gtan//bsam gtan dkyil 'khor byed gyur pa//dkyil 'khor chen po mchog gyur pa//
153a5(rgyud): de nyid bcom ldan bshad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi gsol ba 'debs par byed pa'i dam tshig chen po bsan nas/de bzhin

153a7(rgyud): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor chen po dam pa ste//rdo rje ye shes lta bu ste//sangs rgyas byang chub rab bsal byed//'o ma dang zhor bsam bya zhing//bad ma phra mor nye bar mdzes//sna tshogs me tog

153b3(rgyud): shes pas ser po'i mdog//de nyid sgom pas sgom par bya//dbus su khyab bdag rnam snang 'dzad//yang dag bskyed nas sgom par bya//slob ma'i rnam pa thams cad du//cho ga shes pas rnam sgom bya//rig pa 
153b4(rgyud): chen mo spyan laswogs//dgra rnams nyid du rnam sgom bya//cho ga shes pas bdud rtsi zhes//sgo rnams kun du sgom bya bar//me tog gnyis sogs mtshan nyid kyis//rang gi rdo rje bzhugs bsam bya//
153b5(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor yang bar bya//de rnams kha dog bsdus pa yis//so so so sor rtogs par bya//de rnams kun gyi gzugs rnams su//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam sgom bya//'od zer lnga po yang
153b6(rgyud): dag bskyed//'dod yon lnga rnams bsdus pa yis//khyab bdag la ni dbul bar bya//sangs rgyas slob dpon cho ga las//mkha' khams sangs rgyas kun gyis ni//yongs su gang bar rnam sgom bya//des ni khu ba

156b3(rgyud): 'das phyag na rdo rjes slar yang nam mkha'i khams thams cad ye shes kyi snang bas yang dag par skul bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/dam tshig chen po'i de kho na nyid bskyed par byed pa'i sngags

170a1(rgyud): //rje gshed ni ye shes rigs//bsdus pa'i don la mchog rtsor skyes//khro bo chen po rta mgrin ni//chos kyi ye shes rigasu ston//stong phrag dang ni brgya yang rung//ther 'bum gcig tu zlas nas ni//rang nyid

174b2(rgyud): 'joms 'gyur 'dir ni the tshom med//cho ga shes pas khrag gis byugs//rang nyid rang gi lus su bya//skra krol gtsug tor gyur nas ni//mi sdug mche rtsigs 'jigs par byed//dud khrod khyim du yang dag sbyor//rang gi de'i

174b4(rgyud): khyab 'jug laswogs lha min lha//rkang par byas nas rtsam par bya//cho ga shes pas nyin bdun gyi//khams gsum po yang 'joms par byed//bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang 'dzad chen po sngags thams cad 'chang bas de skad 
174b5(rgyud): ces gsungaso//le'u bcu dgu pa rdzogaso////de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par nang 'dzad chen pos sgrub pa po thams cad kyi sdom pa'i le'u gsungaso//bshang dang lci ba bza' bar bya//sems 
174b6(rgyud): can kun la bde ba byin//rdo rje mchang rgyal de nyid ni//rmang dgu las ni yang dag 'byung //g+ha zhig tu ni bza' bar bya//rdo rje 'chang ba kun gyi rgyal//kun mchog sa'i dbang phyug go//'dod chags bza' bar bya ba ni//
174b7(rgyud): phan dang bde ba thams cad sbyin//de ni rdo rje 'chang rgyal po//kun mchog ma'i dbang phyug go//zhe sdang bza' bar bya ba ni//rdo rje ye shes rab rtogs byed//de nyid rdo rje 'chang rgyal po///kun mchog sa'i dbang phyug po//gti mug
174b8(rgyud): bza' bar bya ba ni//ye shes thams cad rtogs pa'o//de nyid zhi ba 'dzin rgyal po//sangs rgyas rnam par snang 'dzad bzhi//bde sdug bza' bar bya ba ni//slob dpon ji srid mtsho'i bar//de nyid zhi ba 'dzin rgyal po//
175a1(rgyud): //sangs rgyas rnam par snang 'dzad bzhi//slob dpon ji srid mtsho'i bar//chos dang chos min bza' bar bya//de nyid rdo rje 'dzin rgyal po//kun mchog de nyid ston pa po//ji srid mtsho bar g+ha zhig tu//sems can
175a2(rgyud): khams ni bza' bar bya//de nyid rdorje 'dzin rgyal po//kun mtsho ga sa'i dbang phyug//ji srid mtsho bar rmad pa yi//bya ba thams cad pa'i sngags//go rims bzhin du de zlas bya//dbusu bdag po sgoMs nas ni//ngos su lha
175a3(rgyud): rnams dgod par bya//snying po sbyor ba de rnamasu//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor sgom par bya//sngags kyi yi ge mchog sgoms nas//de'i rnam pa rnam sgom pa//thams cad kyi ni lag par ni//rdo rje 'khor lo pad ma sgom//mdung dang yid
175a4(rgyud): bzhin nor bu yang //sgoms nas myur du sgrub par byed//sngags pas rang sngags zlas byas nas//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor sgoM pa yis//ngos su zla ba yang dag sbyor//dbusu ser por rnam sgom bya//sdus te gong bu'i
175a5(rgyud): tshul gyis ni//rang gi lus la dgod par bya//sangs rgyas thams cad rab skul bas//'di ni nges par byed pa'i mchog//dus rnams kun tu rnam sgoms nas//nges par bskal par der gnas 'gyur//
175a6(rgyud): chog shes pas mi skyod spogs//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor la dgod bya//rigs kyi dbang phyug rnam sgoms nas//zla ba'i dkyil 'khaur dgod pa ni//thog ma tha mar bsam gtan 'di//bzhi'i dbusu bsam par bya//
175a7(rgyud): de'i sangs rgyas nyid sgrub na//yun mi ring bar sangs rgyas 'gyur//de'i rang gi sngags kyi mchog//phyag rgya shes nas sgrub pa po//cho ga shes pas bskyed par bya//myur du 'grub byed gzhan du min//phyag rgya chen po rab sgoms na//
175a8(rgyud): nam mkha'i lam gyi dbus las ni//dbusu nyi ma dang 'tshungs pa'i//dkyil 'khor dge ba sgom par bya//sngags par dkyil 'khor las ngosu//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor sgom par bya//bdag po'i gzugs ni yang dag bskyed//
175b1(rgyud): de yis mi g.yo sgom par bya//rin chen rin 'byung sgo da ru//sbrang rtsi mar gyis rab tu bkang//sngags kyi yi ge rnam sgoms nas//bsam pa de yis sbyin sreg bya//dkyil 'khor lnga'i tshul gyis ni//dbusu hU~M gi yi ge 'gengs//'bar
175b2(rgyud): ba khro ba'i 'thibs pa ni//kun nas rnam par 'phro bar byed//de la gnas pa'i lha'i gzugs//tshig pa'i dam tshig gsum byung bas//cho ga shes pa'i gtsug tor gyi//gtsug tor rnams ni sgom par bya//bdag po'i sngags ni yang
175b3(rgyud): dag bskyed//mnyam par gzhag pas sgom par bya//kun nas sor mo bcu gnyis pa'i//ud pal chen po 'dab rgyad pa//chos kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pa//de nyid kyis ni sgom par bshad//rtse mo lnga po'i rdo rje che//ud
175b4(rgyud): pal sngon po 'tshungs pa'i 'od//phreng ba bcu drug yang dag ldan//rtag tu sgom bya 'grub par gyur//sngags pas sor mo nyi shu pa'i//rtse gsum ser po dang 'tshungs pa//cho ga nyid kyis sgom byas nas//grub 'gyur
175b5(rgyud): 'di la the tsom med//pad ma las kyi rdo rje dang//yid bzhin nor bu gsum pa can//bdag po'i sngags ni ya nga dag bskyed//myur du sgrub byed gzhan du min//'khor lo de bzhin pad rdo rje//bsam nas
175b6(rgyud): rdo rje'i cho ga las//sgom pa'i go 'phangs gyis sgrub bya//gzhan du 'di ni 'grub mi 'gyur//rdo rje chen po mdung swogs can//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'tshungs pa'i 'od//kun gyi las can gsang sngags kyi//brjod pa rnams kyis
175b7(rgyud): brjod byas nas//ral pa 'bar ba 'khrugs pa'i sems//'khro zhing 'thibs pa kun du 'khrug//'khru gang tsam du yang dag bskyed//khro bar byed pas khyad par bya//pho nya mngag bzhug swogs sngags rnams//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor
175b8(rgyud): sgom pa'o//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor sbyor ba las//sgom pa'i mchog tu mthong bar 'gyur//dbyug pa tho ba dgra sta dang//zhags pa T+wi sa sgoms pas//cho gas sgoM ma byas nas ni//rdo rje mchang rnams mnyes par byed//'di de sngags ni
176a1(rgyud): /thams cad kyi//sku gsung thugs kyi sgom pa ste//rdo rje ye shes can gyis gsang //ye shes dngos grub can du brjod//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas de skad ces gsungs so//gang zhig dngos grub mchog 'dod na/
176a2(rgyud): cho ga shes pas sbyin sreg bya//de nyid gsang ba'i ting 'dzin gnas//rnam pa sna tshogs sbyin sreg bya//dkyil 'khor bzhi'i sbyor ba las//rigs kyi las ni grub par byed//oM yig sngags kyi sbyor ba yis//sgom pas dngos grub thob 
176a3(rgyud): par 'gyur//zhi ba can la dkyil 'khor zlum//rgyas pa can la zur bzhi pa//mngon spyod la ni gru gsum mchog//dbang bya bzhu'i rnam pa'o//zhi ba me'i dbus son par//bdud rtsi lta bur sgom par bya//de nyid la ni lha'i 
176a4(rgyud): gzugs//rang gi tshul gyis sgom par bya//bdag nyid bdag po'i tshul gyis ni//'di ni rdo rje'i tshul las byung //kha ni rab tu gdangs byas nas//mi rtag 'thibs pas 'khrugs pa yis//sngags pas 
176a5(rgyud): lce'i dbus son pas//hU~M yig de yis bsam par bya//bcom ldan 'das sbyin sreg rdo rjes de skad ces gsungs so//cho ga shes pas 'o ma sbrang //mar ni zhi ba'i rigs la sbyar//mar khu chen 
176a6(rgyud): po'i bye brag las//ye shes rdo rje'i rigs su brjod//'di la rdo rje slob dpon gyi//snying kha'i dbus son rnam sgom bya//yid bzhin nor bu lag bsams nas//'di ni dag pa'i tshul mchog go//rnam par snang 'dzad 
176a7(rgyud): gzugs rnyan che//pad ma'i yon tan 'byung ba la//gzugs ni 'dod chags rigs mchog gi//gzugs ni 'dod mchod rdo rje can//las bzhi rab tu dbye ba las//sngags kyi yi ge dgod byas nas//bcom ldan 'das 
176a8(rgyud): bsang ba'i sbyin sreg rdo rje mchang chen pos de skad ces gsungs so //gnyis su med pa mnyam nyid rnam par rgyal ba'i rtog pa'i che rgyal po chen por de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sngags yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i le'u ste//
176b1(rgyud): nyi shu pa rdzogs s+ho////de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi stobs kyi sngags kun las btus pa zhes bya ba'i snying po'i le'u 'di gsungaso//sangs rgyas rdo rje chen po //'dod
176b2(rgyud): pa'i dam tshig dges pa'o/sngags rnams thams cad yang dag dmigs//de'i bsam gtan rab skul bya//dam tshig ye shes sangs rgyas kun//rigs dmigs dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//gzugs ni sangs rgyas 'tshungs pa'i 'od//
176b3(rgyud): oM yig ye shes dbye ba yis//'di ni rdo rje'i tshul bstan pa'o//ral pa'i cod pan longs spyod pa//kha dog dkar po shin tu 'dzes//phyag bzhi pa la spyan yangs pa//me stag 'thigs pa'i kun tu 'khrugs//rnal 'byor mchod
176b4(rgyud): sbyin thogs laswo gas//rgyan cing dkyil 'khor lasogs pa//cod pan du ni rdo rje'i sems//mdzes pa'i gzugs ni rgyal ba'i gzhi//dbang po gnyis kyis rab sbyar ba'i//'di ni bsam gtan tshul mchog go//
176b5(rgyud): sku'i rdo rje sbyor swogs kyis//sngon shes lnga'i rab sbyor bas//rkang gnyis mchog de nyid shes pas//thod pa can gyi gtso bor brjod//sangs rgyas kyi ni ye shes la//rigs zhes bya ba'i ting 'dzin to//
176b6(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje/rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad kyi dam tshig gi de kho na nyid/byin gyis brlabs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/rdo rje thams cad kyi dam tshig ga snying po
176b7(rgyud): 'byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba 'di gsungaso//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor tshul gyis ni//dbus su gzugs ni rab rtag bya//nag po shin tu rnam 'gyur khro//ma rungs zhal ni rnam par gdangs//me stag thigs po 'bar ba ste//'jig
176b8(rgyud): pa yang ni 'jigs par byed//phyag bzhi pa ste rab 'bad pas//dbang po gnyis kyis rab sbyar bar//'di ni rdo rje thugs swogs yang dag 'byung //rab tu gnas pas de sgrub bya//'dod chags rdo rje rab skul bas//dngos
177a1(rgyud): @#//grub thams cad sgrub par bya//rdo rje dam tshig zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes chos thams cad 'chad pa'i dam tshig byin gyis rlob pa//zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par 
177a2(rgyud): zhugs pa nas/chos 'chad pa'i dpal zhes bya ba 'di gsungaso//chos kyi dkyil 'khor sbyor ba las//dbus su gzugs ni rnam sgom bya//dmar po 'dod chags 'dzin pa 'bar//sna tshogs rgyan gyis brgyan pa yi//
177a3(rgyud): phyag bzhi pa la yid 'ong ba'i//dbang po gnyis kyis rab sbyor bas//de'i sna tshogs sgom pa ni//'di ni 'dod chags tshul stan pa'o//gsung gi rdo rje rtso dngos grub//rdo rje chos rab sbyor sogs kyi//snying rje'i bdag nyid can 
177a4(rgyud): gyis sgrubs//rdo rje chos rab sbyor swogs kyi//snying rje'i bdag/nyid can gyis sgrubs//sgom pa yang ni myur 'grub byed//chos 'chad pa'i dpal zhes ba bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to //de nas bcom ldan

177a6(rgyud): 'dod pa'i dkyil 'khor bsam gtan gyis//gzugasu dbus su dgod par bya//khyab bdag zhi ba ser po'i mdog//spyan gsum rtul shugs can gyis sgom//kun tu dag pa'i ye shes ldan//me stag tshogs ni kun tu 'khrugs//
177a7(rgyud): phyag bzhi par ni cho ga yi//sgom bya myur du grub par byed//rdo rje mthar thug nor dngos grub//rab tu gnas pas de sgrub byed//'di de sngags rnams thams cad kyi//thar pa'i ting 'dzin snying po can//sku gsung thugs kyi sgo

177b1(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas de skad ces gsungaso//rang gi sngags yig dbye bas sam//yang na rigs kyi dbye ba yis//rigs kyi bdag po'i sngags kyis sam//rtul shugs can gyi rigs rgyal pos//thams
177b2(rgyud): cad las kyi sngags kyi dang//gtsug tor sngags kyi rnam sgom bya//'di de sngags rnams thams cad kyi//gsang ba'i de nyid gsung ba yin//bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de skad ces gsung ngo//zhi ba'i las kyi rab 
177b3(rgyud): dbye bas//rigs kyi sngagasu rab tu grags//rgyas ba can la rigs kyi sngags//sgom pas myur du 'grub par byed//dbang du bya ba'i cho ga las//rig pa'i rgyal po sgom par bya//thams cad las can sngags kyis ni//
177b4(rgyud): mngon spyod rnam par sgom par byed//skyod par byed pa'i cho ga las//gtsug tor du ni rnam sgom byed//sngags shes pas ni phur bad ba swogs//pho nya pho nya mor bsam bya//bcom ldan 'das phyag ni rdo rjes de skad 
177b5(rgyud): ces gsungaso//'od zer lnga'i tshul gyis ni//rtog pas de la sgoM par bya//gzhan du dngos grub med pa nyid//sgom pa rnams su shes par bya//rigs kyi las kyis rab dbye bas//dkyil 'khor 
177b6(rgyud): rnams kyis rnam sgom pa'o//sngags kyi yi ges rab dbye bas//sgom bya dngos grub thob bar 'gyur//snying kha'i dbus son phra ma ni//dkyil 'khor rnams kyi rnam pa la//sngags kyi yi ge rab dbye bas//sgom bas dngos
177b7(rgyud): grub thob par 'gyur//snying kha'i dbusason phra mo ni//dkyil 'khor rnams gyi rnam sgom pa//steng du bkod pa'i dbye ba las//'di ni bsam gtan tshul mcho go//dkar nag dmar po'i sbyor ba las//bsam gtan thams 
177b8(rgyud): cad dus rtags bya//gzhan du dngos grubamed pa nyid//sngags rnams kun du zlas par bya//hU~M yig oM yig dbye ba yi//swahA hU~M yig gzhan gyi gzugs//skyed pa'i cho ga byas nas ni//snyen pa gang zhig nges sgrub bya//
178a1(rgyud): //rigs gsum la yang hU~M yig dang //oM yig ye shes rigs mchog yin//sngags kyi yi ge laswogs rnams//pral bar bkod nas rnam sgom bya//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje mchang gis de skad ces gsungaso//
178a2(rgyud): thun bzhi'i sbyor ba laswogs kyi//rdo rje can la bdud rtsi'i gzugs//spro ba dang ni gnas par bya//'di ni dngos grub tshul stan pa'o//sgrub pa rang gi lha'i gzugs//rdo rje can la bdud rtsa'i gzugs//nye bar sgrub pa 'dus

178a8(rgyud): par 'dzad do//gsang ba dag pa dmigs pa med//ye shes yon tan kun gyi gzhi//kun nas ye shes bdag med pa//bsang ba'i de nyid don dang ldan//mchog gi spyod pa rgya mtsho che//kun nas 'dod pa yang dag ldan//

179b1(rgyud): rdo rje byang chub rab tu gnas//mtshan nyid med pa'i chos rnams la//spros pa dag ni yod ma yin//rdo rje'i rang bzhin ye shes kyi//nam mkha' lta bu dri ma med zhes so//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje ting nge 'dzin las bzhengs nas/

208b2(rgyud): de la de ni dga' bar byed//gal te 'di ni sngags pa dag//sha chen dag ni 'tshong 'dod na//dang por dud khrod song nasu//dang po'i sems kyi sha chen blang//sngags pas srung ba byas nasu//lag pa g.yon pa bya dri thogs//

208b4(rgyud): med pa dang//sems can 'phro mo de dag gis//thos nas rab tu 'jigs par 'gyur//gang gzhan lhag pa'i sems yod cing//drag po rab tu zhing skyong ba//sna tshogs rab tu stobs po che//de dag lus ni ston 
208b5(rgyud): par byed//sgrub pa po ni 'jigs mi byed//de nas sems ni drag po de//legs par 'ong zhes smra bar bya//de nas zhing skyong de dag ni//zhi ba'i gzugasu gnas nas kyang//skyobs chen khyod kyi sha chen 
208b6(rgyud): 'di//ci rung 'tsho zhes de la ni/sgrub pa po la 'dri bar byed//'dod pa'i don ni rab sgrub phyir//yang na a dzA na dag dang/rdzas ni gang rnams 'dod pa des//sha yis rab tu sbyin byas zhes//sgrub pa pos kyang smra bar bya//
208b7(rgyud): sgrub pa po ni gzhan mi smra//sems ni dpa' bar shes nas ni//sbyin par byed kyis gzhan du min//de na gzhan yang 'di'i las//dpung rnams gnon par sbyor ba gang//khro bo mchog gis gsungs pa ni//de dag 'dir yang 
208b8(rgyud): bshad par bya//ri mo gos las byas pa ni//ba dan shin du bzang po bya//de'i 'thing ka legs bya ba//sor ni bcu drug tshad du bya//myu gu yu ba sbu ba gu ni//khru gang tshad du byas pa las//stegs ni rab bzang du ba ni//dud khrod 
209a1(rgyud): //du ni mtshan mo grub//dkyil 'khor rgya chen byas nas kyang //der ni btsug pur gnas nas kyang //ba dan la gan thogs nas ni//mtshan mo thog thag zla bar bya//de nas nam ni langs pa na//de'i lag na thogs

209a4(rgyud): yang dud khrod kyi//sbyor ba bkra shis dge ba dang //khro bo mchog gis gsungs pa de//de ni nges par bshad par bya//de ni mi ro sregs pa ni//mtshan mo'i 'bar ba ltas nas su//bkra shis gos kyang med par bya//

209a7(rgyud): dpung dag mnan pa'i phyir//thal ba 'di ni gtor byas na//shing rta dang ni blang po dang //mtshon cha thams cad 'khrugs pa dang //skye bo mang po ldan pa'i yul//thal ba de'i thams cad du//lan cig gtor ba tsam gyis kyang //

209b1(rgyud): 'khrugs pa la//byas nas 'gro ba mi 'khrugs 'gyur//dung khrod dag gi sbyor bas ni//de bzhin dbang rnams bshad zin to//'gro ba dbang med sbyor ba ni//rnam pa gzhan dag bshad par bya//dud khrod dag tu dkyil 'khor bri/

210a7(rgyud): dang rgya mtsho g.yo ba dang //shing rnams rgya mtsho lhags pa dang //sa'i 'og kyang g.yo ba dang //de bzhin klu rnams skrag pa dang //de mtsho chen dag ni 'khyil pa dang //klu rnams rab tu tshar bcad dang //gzhan yang gang dang gang 'dod pa'i/

231a3(rgyud): ba slar yang gzhag//gzhin snyer phyag rgya bcing bar bya//khro bor lta zhing yid kyang khros//rab tu dpal la sras dang bcas//rtsal gyis mnon pa rab tu 'dud//sngags pas tshul bzhin sngar tshul gnas//gang 'dod pa ni
231a4(rgyud): bzhom pa yi//de nas rkang pa ya cig kyang//drag shul can du rkyang ste gzhag//gzur te 'dug pa'i tshul du gnas//gdong yang yon par rnyed par bya//'dzub mo gdengs te sgrengs pa dang//be con phyag
231a5(rgyud): rgya dag gis sdigs//dgra dang skrang par 'dod na ni//myur du skrad par byas nas ni//de nas rkang pa mnyam par gzhag//lag pa g.yas pa mthong bar bya//dpal dang ldan pas g.yon pa smad/
231a6(rgyud): zhi bar 'dod na zhi bar 'gyur//phyed na dmar ba bkug ste gzhag//rkang pa g.yas pa tsham ngam byed//sngar tshul gnas pas sgrims nas ni//rgyas par 'dod na thob par 'gyur//slar yang de bzhin shes bya ba//zlog par
231a7(rgyud): bya ba gzhan du min//grub pa bdag nyid chen po yi//tsham ngam rim pa drug gsungs pa//dang po ming ni log par phyogs//de'i 'og ma rgyab kyis phyogs//de bzhin tsham ngan gsum po ni//khro bo chen zhes sgrags pa yin//
231a8(rgyud): bzhi pa 'dzub mo sgreng ba yis//lnga pa la ni stobs zhes bya//drug pa stobs po che zhes bya//'di rnams tsham ngan drug du sgrags//tsham ngan gdangs pa byas pa yis//dgra'i 'khor rnams dben phyogs pa//'khor lo mchog
231b1(rgyud): la goms byed pa//grags pa chen po thob par 'gyur//khro bo chen pos gsungs pa yi//rab tu gsungs pa'i rgyud 'di dkon//khro bo rnam par rgyal ba 'di//de bas kyang ni shin du dkon//gsang ba'i rtog pa bzang po 'di/
231b2(rgyud): kun du dngos grub kun spyod pa//gsang ba'i rtog pa de las kyang //'khor lo 'di ni shin du dkon//ho dang zlas pa yod mi rgos//sngon du snyen pa bya mi dgos//khro bo'i rgyal po'i rtul shugs dang //dka' thub
231b3(rgyud): dag kyang bya mi rgos//bsam gtan sbyor ba'ang mi rgos te//gang la 'khor lo b+ha zhig yod//gang gsungs pa'i dngos grub gnas//de ma thag tu dngos grub thob//de bas 'di ni gsang byas nas//thams cad sgrub pa'i sgrub pa
231b4(rgyud): pos//bdag gis mchog gang 'dod pa 'dis//shin du legs par bsgrub par bya//khro bo rnam par rgyal pa'i rtog pa las//gsang ba'i rtog par rtogs pa//gnyis pa'o////
231b5(rgyud): khro bo'i rgyal po'i dngos grub ni//sna tshogs thogs med tha dad pa//sngags pa 'dod pa cho ga mchog/gang 'dod byas na dngos grub thob//rab tu gsang ba'i sngags 'di ni//thub pa gang rnams sngar gsungs pa/
231b6(rgyud): re shig 'di'i mtshan nyid ni//gzhan dag 'dir ni bshad par bya//de'i las ni gang gsungs pa//gang dag phyogs kyang 'grub 'gyur na//dpa'o gcig pu b+ha zhig gis//thams cad nyid de gzhan du min//thams cad du ni bshad
231b7(rgyud): par bya//sngags des las rnams kun byed pa'i//rgyud las dngos grub gang gsungs pa//dpa'o gcig pu'i mthus kyang 'grub//oM ni de'i thog ma ste//bde bar gshegs pa'i dam tshig yin//thog ma dang ni gnyis pa yang //
231b8(rgyud): drag po cig gis pral ba'i gzugs//glad kyi 'khor gyis gnas pa'i rigs//sngags kyi yi ge phreng ba bkod//sngags las thams cad bsal byed pa//khro bo'i rgyal po'i snying po de//khyad par med par de nas sbyar//de nas khro bo'i
233a1(rgyud): /rgyal po yi//sku ni de'i 'og tu bstan//sngags pa sngags rnams kun gyis mtshan//de dag rtag tu rig par bya//thogs pa med pa'i sngags rnams kyis//bdag nyid chen po khyed rnams kyis//srung la sogs pa'i
233a2(rgyud): las rnams kun//go rims bzhin du bshad par bya//thams cad bdag nyid chen po 'dis//srung ba dag ni byas nas su//sngon gyi tshul bzhin rab dben pa//sku gzugs mngon mchog gzhag nas ni//cho ga ji skad gsungs pa yi//
233a3(rgyud): cho ga bzhin du mchod par bya//nyin re me tog phreng ba la//lan bdun zlas pa rnams kyis mchod//mchod nas ka ra pi ra yi//me tog dkar po kha 'bus pa'i//brgya ni rtsa brgyad grangs rnams kyis//bu don la ni thog mar dbul//
233a4(rgyud): cho ga 'di ni dus gsum du//zla ba gcig tu byas pa yi//khro bo mngon sum nyid gyur nas//rmi lam du ni dngos grub ster//cho ga 'di ltar sngar byas nas//gang 'dod pa'i dgos pa dag/de tshe gdon mi za bar 
233a5(rgyud): 'grub//de ni cho ga mchog bshed pa//de ltar zla cig brnyes byas nas//mi'i las rnams gang bya ba//de'i cho gar mdor sdus nas//da ni legs par bshad par bya//'chi 'pho ba dang sbyar gnas pa'i//
233a6(rgyud): dug gi rnam pa gsum dag dang //gdon dang 'byung po dug rnams dang //lto 'phye chen por bcas par 'jig/bdag gis byas pa'i phyag rgya'i sngags//sbyor ba lan cig bdag gis srung //'di la bsam pa tsam gyis kyang //
233a7(rgyud): gzhan dag gis gcod par byed//bzhi pa'i rigs la sogs de yang //thos pa myur du med par byed//ma dang byis pa'i gdon rnams la//thal ba 'am yang na sas kyang rung //bdag nyid dag par bsams nas ni//bdag dgar phan 
233a8(rgyud): par dgos pa'i don//gang dang gang dag 'dod pa ni//sgom pas de dang de dag thar//rdo la lan gsum zlas pa yis//chu srin ku b+hi ra la sogs//mche ba can rnams dogs med par//chu nas bsal bar mchog tu 'gyur//
233b1(rgyud): 'gro che yid la zlas na ni//lam na rkun po'i 'jigs pa med//seng ge dag dang stag rnams dang/nad kyi 'jigs pa med par 'gyur/rgyal po'i drung du 'gro na ca yang//sngags kyis spyi rtsug bcings nas ni//rgyal po khros 
233b2(rgyud): pa myur rab tu//rkyang mnan thal mo sbyor bar byed//shags 'gyed pa dang rgyal po dang/mtshon dang me'i 'jigs pa dang/yid la bran bzhin zlas byed na/mi de kun las thar bar 'gyur//'tshe ba thams cad zhi bya'i
233b3(rgyud): phyir//mi bde nyams thag gyur pa la//sngags pa 'di myur grog brjod na//srin dag mnan par bya ba yang//char yang myur du gcod par byed//gnam lcags ser ba 'bab pa dang//dbyug pas phyag rgyas zhi 
233b4(rgyud): bar byed//phyag rgya bcings pa de nyid kyis//yid kyis srin rnams bda' bar bya//de ltar phyag rgyas sdigs nas ni//smin 'tshams dag kyang bsgyur bar bya//rnam pa de dag nyid kyis ni//gnam 
233b5(rgyud): lcags ser ba gcod par bya//sbyor ba mkhas pa ci 'dod pa//chad pa dag kyang bcad par bya/gos ni sngon po bgos nas ni//kho ra khor yug srung byas nas//med pa tsam du tshur 
233b6(rgyud): 'dug na//nyin gsum legs par zlas byas nas//'dod pa bzhin du char pa 'bebs//de nas char srin las byung ba//char pa sa stengs khyab par 'gyur//nyi ma la ni mngon phyogs nas//nyin re rtag tu zlas byas na//
233b7(rgyud): bdag dang de bzhin gzhan rnams kyis//nad rnams de dag med par byed//rgyal po dbang du byar yang rung//nyungs kar dkar po bzang po las//gzugs rnyan legs par byas nas su//de'i gzugs kyi steng 'dug la/
233b8(rgyud): gang gi ming smos nyin bdun du//zlas pa legs par byas nas ni//de nas myur du cho ga 'dis//dran bzhin du ni dbang du 'gyur//zhag cig snyung ba byas pa yi//rmang rigs dag ni dbang du 'gyur//zhagaganyis 
234a1(rgyud): //snyung ba de bzhin du//zlas pa rje rigs 'dod pa bzhin//zhag gsum byas pas rje rgyal rigs te//zhag bzhi dag gis bram ze dag//bzlas pa cho ga bzhin byas na//'dod pa bzhin du dbang du 'gyur//
234a2(rgyud): 'bru dang phug ma thams cad dang //bu dang bu mo gang yang rung //de bzhin gzugs rnyan byas nas ni//rmang rigs la mtshan gcig gis//rab tu sdang bar byas par 'gyur//rje rigs la ni zhag gcig gis//
234a3(rgyud): de tshe btsog pur 'dug nas ni//de bzhin 'dza' dang bral bar byed//nyin zhag bzhi dang gsum dag gis//rgyal rigs dag dang bram ze'o//de ltar ji skad gsungs pa yis//las kyi bya ba'i gzhan du min//gang 
234a4(rgyud): yang yang na dgra'i gzugs//dung khrod sol bas legs par ru//snga ma bzhin du legs par byi//de nas seng ldeng phur bu la//sngags kyis stong du zlas byas nas//de'i 'og tu lus la dbab//dgra bo de'i
234a5(rgyud): ming smos nas//de la khro bar byas nas su//de nas de'i snying kha ru//phur bu btab ste zlas byas na//de nyid lag pas bzung nas su//dus gsum nyi ma gsum byas nas//dgra bo gang du btab pa der//
234a6(rgyud): gzer thabs drag pos btab nas su//'gul mi phod par sa la ltung //zhag bdun lon nas shi bar 'gyur//ma mo'i gnasu song nas ni//lag pas be con phyag rgyas bcing //lag pa g.yon pa'i 'dzub mo yis//
234a7(rgyud): sdigs shing nyi ma nub kyi bar//khros shing dgra bo'i ming smos zlas//de nas dgra bo ma gdon gyi//nyen nas sbyor ba sna tshogs bgyis//'chi bar 'gyur ba sna tshogs byed//de nas grub pa'i sa gzhi'i//tshogs de la zhi bar byas//
234a8(rgyud): ma mo rnams kyis 'dun 'dug nas//zhi ba'i cho ga mkhas pa yis//dbang po bsdams nas yid bde bas//nyin zhag gsum du zlas byas nas//de nas de la zhi bar bya//zhi bar 'gyur ba gdon mi za//nyi ma la ni mngon phyogs nas//
234b1(rgyud): lag pas dgra'i mgo byugs shing //dgra'i ming smos mnan nas su//nyi ma nub tu zlas byas nas//de'i glad pa nar gyur pa//tsha bas chen shi la thug par 'gyur//de nas thal mo sbyar nas su//nyin mo zlos shing zhi bar 'gyur//
234b2(rgyud): sa stengs rkang pa gcig gis 'dug/nyin bar lag pa gyen du rkyang //mi rga khro zhing zlas pa bya//dgra bo'i ming smos ci 'dod par//nyi ma nub khar de nas dgra//mi dgra dang myes 'gyur te//gnyen dang 'dza'o rnams bor te//
234b3(rgyud): de las gzhan pa'i gnasu 'gro//de las 'dir ni zhi ba 'am//'on te rgyas par bya 'dod na//gtsang mar byas te gos dkar gon//bde zhing rtan pa ma g.yos pa//mchod pa bzang po byas nas su//de bzhin gshegs
234b4(rgyud): la phyag 'tshal nas//'o ma 'thung zhing zlas byas nas//zhi bar 'gyur gyi bar du ni//de ltar de ni sna tshogs pa'i//sbyor byas rgyas par bshad pa yin//rtog pa'i ga bzhin du ni//ma gsungs par ni 
234b5(rgyud): legs par bsgrub//khro bo rnam par rgyal pa 'jam dpal gyi rtsa ba'i rtog par rtogs pa//gsum pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bzang po 'di//he la'i 'khor lo rgyas pa ni//
234b6(rgyud): de las bzang po gzhan med te//ji ltar lhag par bshad par bya//dang po he zhes gang gsungs pa//skul ba'i sa bon thams cad pa'o//de ni len cing 'dzin pa ste//las la myur du bskul ba'o//de bas thog mar skul ba'o//
234b7(rgyud): dang //mngon bzhin du ni bzung ba dang //khro bo yi ni rgyal po yis//he la'i 'khor lo rim bzhin bstan//hA+e hA+e hA hA hI hI hI hI ha ha he he//gnyis sbyor ba//khro bo chen po tshig gi thog mar sbyar//zung cig pa'i tshig gnyis pa'o//
234b8(rgyud): zung drug 'khor lo'i mtha' mar dgod//mu ran rtsibs kyi mig rnams su//sngags las byung ba re re'o//don rnams thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//bcu gnyis dag tu gsungs pa'i sngags//gnasu ji ltar dgod pa dang //
235a1(rgyud): //ji ltar smos shing gang gnas pa//so so'i las la sbyor bar bya//de nas 'di ni bkod pa dang//snga rnams sdu ba tshul bzhin bshad//de nang mu ran gsum po dang//dbus kyi ran bzang por ldan//mtha'i mu ran
235a2(rgyud): drug po dang/rtsibs kyi zur drug rab bzang ba//rab dben 'du 'dzi med pa'i sar//cho ga bzhin du legs bris nas//de'i dbus su sbyar ba dang//de'i go rims bzhin du sbyar//de'i 'tshams ngan gsal ba yi//
235a3(rgyud): sngags rnams 'dir ni bsdu bar bya//dbus su de bzhin gshegs pa yi//snying po mtshan ldan brjod pa'i mchog//gzhag nas de'i gnas dag la//sngags ni bsgrub par byed pa gzhag//mu ran gnyis na gnas
235a4(rgyud): nas su//khro bo'i snying po gzhag par bya//de ni khro bo'i zhabs kyis mnan//dril bu bkrol ba'i sgra yang sgrogs//khro bo'i zhabs kyis bcag byas la//rtsibs nas khro bo'i mthu dang ldan//dbus su
235a5(rgyud): snying po dag dang ldan//rab du 'bar zhing des byas la//mu ran gsum gyi bar du ste//thams cad don rnams kun grub pa'o//hA+e hA+e hA hA//hI hI hI hI/hA+e hA+e hA hA gnyis su sbyar//khro bo chen po'i tshig dbus nas/
235a6(rgyud): sa bon bzung ba'i phyi rol gzhag//tha mar skul ba'i tshig rnams dag//khro bo du mas skul ba ste//thams cad kun du don rnams grub//grub pa'i phyir ni 'di la gnas//de ltar 'khor los byas pa yis//phyi nas bdud
235a7(rgyud): rtsi 'khyil pa gzhag//skul ba'i tshig rnams bkod pa'i mthar//khro bo gzhan rnams bkod par bya//de nas gzhan yang rims bzhin du//khro bo chen po dgod pa'i phyir//skul bar byed pa bcu gnyis pa//'di dag tshul
235a8(rgyud): bzhin bshad par bya//dang po khro bo'i rgyal pos gsungs//'bar ba'i phreng ba che zhes bya//rdo rje me'i rgyal po bzhin//rab tu grag po'i shugs gzhag go//rab tu grag shul stobs po che//rgyal chen khro bo lnga pa yin//
235b1(rgyud): drug pa gzhan gyis mi thub pa//'jigs par byed pa zhes bya ba//khro bo bdun par grags pa yin//zur drug khro bo brgyad par grags//phongs pa sgrol ba de bzhin dgu//rab tu 'joms pa bcu pa yin//de bzhin ki li ki li gzhag/
235b2(rgyud): ki li ki la chen po dang //bcu gnyis su ni gsungs pa yin//khro bo rab tu snang byed pa//bcu gnyis sgrags pa 'di dag ni//khro bo'i rgyal pos kun du gnon//thams cad rim pa bzhin du gsungs//'khor lo'i phyi rol 
235b3(rgyud): gnas la dgod//'bar dang drag po'i mthu dang ldan//thams cad bstan nas mchod pa ni//khro bo'i snang ba chen snang byed pa//mthu tsha rtsa la chen po'i stobasu ldan//kun kyang bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'o//sa bon snga ma mtha' yas rnaMs/
235b4(rgyud): dgug byed drug dang ldan par bya//steng du glad kyis 'khor gyis mtshan//sa bon bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'o/dang po'i dkyil 'khor nang na gcig//tshig dang ldan pa'i don kun sgrub//rab tu 'bar ba sngags kyi mthar//
235b5(rgyud): 'bar ba'i phreng ba can gyis sgrubs//sa bon gnyis pa gang yin pa//tshig dang bcas pa de bzhin gnas//rdo rje me rgyal zhes bya ba//khro bo'i don kun bsgrub byed pa//dang po sa bon gsum pa yang //
235b6(rgyud): snga ma bzhin du tshig dang bcas//rab tu shugs drag cher grags pa//khro bo'i rgyal po khro bar snang //tshig bcas de bzhin rab shes bya//snang byed khro bo'i rim pa las//tshig bcas de bzhin khro bo che//de ni stobs po che zhes 
235b7(rgyud): gsungs//gnyis pa'i khro bo drag mtha' can//gang gsungs khro bo rgya chen ni//de ni snying por sgrags pa'o//mi thub khro bo tshig dang bcas//de'i gsum po tshig bcas pa//de yang de bzhin bsgrags pa yin//'jigs pa 
235b8(rgyud): zhes bya rab tu phye//khro 'di khro bo snang byed pa//rgya chen gyi ni rim bzhin du//bzhi po yongasu bsgrags pa yin//tshig bcas rab tu grag po nyid//zur cig khro bo de bzhin gzhag//de'i sa bon lnga pa yin//'bar ba'i phreng can 
237a1(rgyud): //zhes bya gang//tsheg dang bcas pa de bzhin du//khro bo 'phongs pa sgrol zhes bya'o//lnga po rgya chen nyid yin no//tsheg dang bcas par bsgrags pa'o//rab tu 'joms pa zhes grags pa//khro bo khro bo snang byed pa//sa bon
237a2(rgyud): snga ma mtha' med las//snga ma tsheg bcas de bzhin gzhag//ki li ki la bya ba'i//dngos grub thams cad rab tu ster//de bzhin de'i sa bon nyid//tsheg bcas de bzhin 'dir bshad do//ki li ki la chen po de//khro bo khro
237a3(rgyud): bo'i snang byed pa'o//snga ma bzhin du tsheg dang bcas//khro bo'i rgyal po de dag ni//sa bon 'di dag rnams kyis sgrags//khro bo'i rgyal por spyi bor ni//rab tu snang bar byed ces grags//khro bo'i rgyal po'i 'khor lo gnas
237a4(rgyud): gshin rje gshed po yin par gsungs//dang po de ni 'khor lo bstan//khro bo bdud rtsi 'khyil pa dang//khro bo'i rgyal po snang byed pa'o//de ltar khro bo gsum po de//gang dang gang du rab sbyar ba//de dang de ru sngags rnaMs ni/
237a5(rgyud): skul bar byed pa de bzhin skul//cho ga shes pas nan tan tu//gang dang gang 'dod bsgrub par bya//khro bo sgom dang ldan pa yis//he la'i 'khor lo yongs skor nas//dka' bzhin sgrub tu'ang mi
237a6(rgyud): rung ba//rtul shugs haM dang bcas lasogs//de yis dka' ba ma byas kyang//sngags bkod shes nas sgrub pa'o//he la'i 'khor lo'i tshul shes pa//de dag dka' ba ma shes kyang//rdon mi za ba'i
237a7(rgyud): myur du 'grub//brtul shugs ha~M dang bsam gtan dang//ji skad gsungs pa'i dka' thub la//mngon par rtsam zhing dad pa'i bdag//lo grangs bcu gnyis dag snyen pa//rtog las gsungs pa'i las rnaMs
237a8(rgyud): byed//rab tu ya mtshan ston pa po//he la'i 'khor lo'i tshu shes pa//ring por mi thogs bde bar ni//khro bo sgom pa dang ldan pa'i//he la'i 'khor lo'i gnas 'dug la//khams gsum dag kyang
237b1(rgyud): lan cig gis//ma thogs myur du g.yo bar byed//de bas rab tu sgribs nas su//khro bo sgom pa goms bya ste//he la'i 'khor lo las byung sngags//sbyor ba dag gis goms par bya//khro bo rnam par rgyal ba yi//rtog pa
237b2(rgyud): dang po gang sgrub pa//de'i rtog pa gsang bshad pa//cho ga 'di ni gsang ba'i mchog//khro bo'i 'khor lo bsgrags pa 'di//cho ga shes pas shin du bsad//khro bo'i 'khor lo gsang bshad pa//rab tu snang byed de bas kyang//
237b3(rgyud): he la'i 'khor lo bsgrags pa 'di //rab tu gsang ba'i mchog yin te//rtog pa 'di dag thams cad kyi//rab tu gsang ba bshad pa yin//rtog pa rab tu gsang ba 'di//'di dag rnam pa kun la yang//he la'i khor
237b4(rgyud): lo 'dra ba yi//'khor lo ma byung phyis mi 'byung//gang zhig 'di yis don thos na//khro bo sgrubs pa goms byed pa//de'i 'bad pa ma byas kyang//'jig rten na ni sgrub dka' med//he la'i 'khor lo'i
237b5(rgyud): gnas la ni//ji ltar 'dod par gnas nas su//ji ltar rig pa'i tshul bzhin du//khro bo sgom dang ldan pa yi//khro bo'i gzugs su gnas nas ni//de bzhin du ni tshig gis mchog//sgrub pa mchog ni 'byin

237b7(rgyud): ces pa'i tshig gnyis gzhag//skul cig ces pa'i tshig gnyis mthar//khro bo bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'o//khro bo chen po 'bar phreng can//bdud rtsi 'khyil pa 'di'i mthar//kun du dang po g.yo ba dang//sngags shes tshad ngam shes
237b8(rgyud): pa yis//he la'i 'khor lo la gnas te//dang po'i gnas la 'dug nas ni//be con phyag rgya lan cig bcing//de nas khro bo'i sngags bzlas pa//sngags pa 'greng ste 'dug pa na//rkang pa gnyis ni bsgyur ba dang//khro bo sgom pa
238a1(rgyud): //'di la ni//sems la rab tu zhen byas nas//sa ni rab tu g.yo ba 'am//ri dgas g.yo ba'i bar du 'o//de nas 'khor lo sgyur ba'i//gnyis pa'i gnas la song nas ni//khro bo chen po'i tshig mthar gnas//
238a2(rgyud): sgoms shig zhes bya rab tu skul//nges tshig thog mar bkod nas ni//de'i thog mar bdud rtsi 'khyil//de bzhin bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'i mthar//rdo rje me'i rgyal po'i sngags//rab tu g.yo ba 'dzes pa dang//tsham ngan gsal ba
238a3(rgyud): dag la gnas//be con phyag rgya bcings nas ni//khro bo'i sgoms pas sngags zlas so//rab tu sbyor ba'i thabs 'di ni//mtsho dang rdzing bu klung chen dang//khros pas myur du skam par byed//'bab chu dag kyang myur du 'bebs//
238a4(rgyud): 'khor lo mthu chen gnon ldan pa//sngags kyi yi ge dgod bya ba//rtsibs gnas rim pa kun mnan pa//gnyis po de nas 'phos nas ni//de nas gsum po'i gnas la 'dug//mthu rtsal ldan pa'i rim par gnas//khro bo chen po'i
238a5(rgyud): tshig gis mthar//sgrag par byas shes tshig gnyis smros//de ni snga ma bzhin bkod nas//de'i mtha' mar bdud rtsi 'khyil/khro bo bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'i mtha'//rab tu drag po'i shugs gzhag go//
